# Source: results\pops/initial_pool.json (item 0)
# Objective: 19732.46
# Description: The algorithm implements Base Stock Policy where the inventory level is always brought back to a fixed target level 'base_stock' at the beginning of each period.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    base_stock = 160
    order_amount = max(0, base_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/initial_pool.json (item 1)
# Objective: 9769.98
# Description: The algorithm implements Constant Order Policy by ordering a fixed quantity at regular intervals 't', regardless of the current inventory level.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    t = len(history_inventory)-1
    interval = 2
    if t%interval:
        order_amount = 0
    else:
        order_amount = 100
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/initial_pool.json (item 2)
# Objective: 17428.42
# Description: The algorithm implements Capped Base Stock Policy, where the order quantity is limited by a cap (maximum order size). If the inventory position is below the base stock level,  an order is placed to bring it back up to 'base_stock'. However, the order quantity cannot exceed a predefined cap 'order_cap'.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    base_stock = 70
    order_cap = 30
    order_amount = min(base_stock - history_inventory[-1], order_cap)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 0)
# Objective: 2609.50421
# Description: A demand-responsive algorithm that uses weighted demand volatility and cost-adaptive safety stock, dynamically adjusted by recent inventory performance and lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.4
    beta = 0.3
    gamma = 0.5
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = 0
    else:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/len(history_demand[:-1])
        demand_volatility = max(0, (max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:]))/smoothed_demand) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    inventory_ratio = 1 - (sum(history_inventory[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:])) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_adjustment = gamma * (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio + holding_cost * inventory_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment + demand_volatility) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 1)
# Objective: 2804.63261
# Description: A demand-shaping inventory policy that combines exponential smoothing of demand with cost-sensitive safety stock adjustment, dynamically weighted by recent lost sales patterns.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.3
    beta = 0.2
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/len(history_demand[:-1])
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    cost_adjustment = (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 2)
# Objective: 3114.64736
# Description: A volatility-adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on demand variability, cost-sensitive safety stock, and lead-time-aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    volatility_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-volatility_window:]
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / volatility_window
    demand_std = (sum((x - avg_demand)**2 for x in recent_demand) / volatility_window)**0.5 if volatility_window > 1 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_factor = 1.5 * cost_ratio * (1 + demand_std / (avg_demand + 1e-6))
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand * (1 + safety_factor) - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 3)
# Objective: 3423.84158
# Description: A momentum-driven inventory algorithm that adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand trends and cost-sensitive safety stock, incorporating lead time awareness and lost sales momentum.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 50
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(1e-6, holding_cost)
    momentum_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    momentum = sum(history_demand[-momentum_window:]) / momentum_window
    
    lost_momentum = 0.4 * sum(history_lost[-3:]) / min(3, len(history_lost)) if history_lost else 0
    safety_factor = min(2.0, max(0.5, 0.7 * cost_ratio))
    
    base_demand = 0.6 * momentum + 0.4 * (sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand))
    target_level = base_demand * safety_factor + lost_momentum
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 4)
# Objective: 3703.76
# Description: A quantile-based demand shaping algorithm that dynamically adjusts order amounts using empirical demand percentiles weighted by cost-sensitive risk factors.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    risk_factor = min(2.0, max(0.5, cost_ratio ** 0.7))
    window = min(5, max(2, len(history_demand) // 2))
    
    effective_demands = [d + 0.5 * l for d, l in zip(history_demand[-window:], history_lost[-window:])]
    sorted_demands = sorted(effective_demands)
    quantile_idx = min(len(sorted_demands)-1, int(0.75 * len(sorted_demands)))
    target = sorted_demands[quantile_idx] * risk_factor
    
    order_amount = max(0, target - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 5)
# Objective: 3941.2
# Description: Probability-Weighted Demand Shaping with Cost-Adjusted Safety Stock: Uses quantile-based demand estimation with probabilistic safety stock adjustments based on normalized cost ratios, while maintaining lead-time awareness through forward-looking inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    alpha = 0.2
    
    # Exponential smoothing for demand
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
    
    # Quantile-based adjustment
    demand_quantile = sorted(history_demand)[int(len(history_demand) * cost_ratio)] if history_demand else 0
    safety_stock = max(0, round(demand_quantile * cost_ratio))
    
    target = round(smoothed_demand) + safety_stock
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target - current_inv)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 6)
# Objective: 3991.16086
# Description: The new algorithm implements a Volatility-Adjusted Cost-Balancing Policy that combines weighted demand forecasting with a dynamic safety stock factor based on demand variability and cost-sensitive reorder thresholds.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    beta = 0.4
    cost_sensitivity = (lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)) ** 0.5
    avg_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    demand_variability = (max(history_demand) - min(history_demand)) / avg_demand if avg_demand > 0 else 0
    lost_sales_impact = sum(history_lost[-2:]) * beta if history_lost else 0
    safety_factor = 1 + (demand_variability * cost_sensitivity)
    target = (avg_demand * safety_factor) + lost_sales_impact
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 7)
# Objective: 4138.86056
# Description: New algorithm uses weighted demand forecasting with exponential smoothing, adjusts safety stock based on recent lost sales trend, and applies a dynamic cost-sensitive reorder multiplier.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    alpha = 0.3
    beta = 0.2
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        forecast = history_demand[0] if len(history_demand) > 0 else 0
    else:
        forecast = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * history_demand[-2]
    
    lost_sales_trend = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / 3 if len(history_lost) >= 3 else 0
    safety_factor = 1 + beta * lost_sales_trend / max(1, forecast)
    
    cost_sensitivity = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    reorder_multiplier = min(2.0, 1 + 0.5 * (cost_sensitivity - 1))
    
    target_level = forecast * safety_factor * reorder_multiplier
    current_position = history_inventory[-1] if len(history_inventory) > 0 else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - current_position)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 8)
# Objective: 4256.0
# Description: The algorithm implements a Demand-Adaptive Threshold Policy that dynamically adjusts order amounts based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, using exponential smoothing for demand forecasting and a variable ordering interval determined by inventory risk.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1])
    
    alpha = 0.3
    forecast = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * history_demand[-2]
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    threshold = int(forecast * (1 + cost_ratio))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1]
    safety_stock = int(forecast * 0.5)
    order_interval = max(1, int(2 * (1 - cost_ratio)))
    
    if len(history_inventory) % order_interval == 0 or current_inv < safety_stock:
        order_amount = max(0, threshold + safety_stock - current_inv)
    else:
        order_amount = 0
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 9)
# Objective: 4390.49637
# Description: An adaptive inventory algorithm that uses weighted demand forecasting with dynamic safety stock adjustment based on recent lost sales trends and cost ratios.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return 100
    
    # Weighted demand forecast (recent periods have higher weight)
    weights = [0.6, 0.4] if len(history_demand) >= 2 else [1.0]
    forecast = sum(w * d for w, d in zip(weights, history_demand[-len(weights):])) / sum(weights)
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on lost sales trend
    lost_sales_trend = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / 2 if len(history_lost) >= 2 else 0
    safety_factor = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)) * (1 + lost_sales_trend / forecast if forecast > 0 else 1)
    safety_stock = safety_factor * forecast
    
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1]
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 10)
# Objective: 4498.74552
# Description: A demand-weighted exponential smoothing algorithm that adjusts smoothing factor based on recent lost sales frequency and scales order amounts by cost-adaptive safety stock.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) == 0:
        order_amount = 50
    else:
        lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6)
        alpha = min(0.5, max(0.1, lost_ratio * 2))
        prev_forecast = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else (1-alpha)*history_demand[-2] + alpha*history_demand[-1]
        cost_factor = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)) ** 0.5
        safety_stock = cost_factor * prev_forecast
        order_amount = max(0, (prev_forecast + safety_stock) - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 11)
# Objective: 4532.05333
# Description: An adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock based on recent lost sales trends and demand variability, while using exponential smoothing for demand forecasting and cost-sensitive inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) == 0:
        return 0
    
    # Exponential smoothing for demand forecast
    alpha = 0.3
    forecast = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        forecast = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * forecast
    
    # Lost sales trend adjustment
    lost_window = 3
    if len(history_lost) >= lost_window:
        recent_lost = sum(history_lost[-lost_window:]) / lost_window
        lost_ratio = min(1.0, recent_lost / max(1, forecast))
    else:
        lost_ratio = 0
    
    # Cost-sensitive safety factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_factor = 1.0 + (cost_ratio * (1 + lost_ratio))
    
    # Calculate target inventory
    target_inventory = forecast * safety_factor
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if len(history_inventory) > 0 else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 12)
# Objective: 4873.47959
# Description: An adaptive moving average algorithm that dynamically adjusts base stock levels based on recent demand trends and cost ratios, while incorporating safety stock to balance holding and lost sales costs.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 3:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1])
    
    window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    demand_avg = sum(history_demand[-window:]) / window
    lost_avg = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / window
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / holding_cost
    safety_factor = min(2.0, max(1.0, cost_ratio * 0.5))
    adjusted_demand = demand_avg + (lost_avg * 0.5)
    
    base_stock = adjusted_demand * safety_factor
    order_amount = max(0, base_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 4880.06267
# Description: An adaptive inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on exponential smoothing of demand forecasts and cost-optimized safety stock levels, considering lead time and real-time inventory position.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return 100  # Default initial order
    
    alpha = 0.3  # Smoothing factor
    forecast = history_demand[-1] * alpha + (1 - alpha) * history_demand[-2]
    
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1]
    safety_stock = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)) * forecast
    
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 14)
# Objective: 4884.37369
# Description: A volatility-adjusted inventory algorithm that dynamically scales safety stock based on demand variability and cost-adaptive reorder thresholds, incorporating lead time and lost sales feedback.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 50
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(1e-6, holding_cost)
    avg_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    volatility = (max(history_demand[-5:]) - min(history_demand[-5:])) / max(1, avg_demand) if len(history_demand) >= 5 else 0.5
    
    lost_penalty = 0.3 * sum(history_lost[-2:]) if history_lost else 0
    reorder_multiplier = min(2.5, max(0.8, 0.5 * cost_ratio * (1 + volatility)))
    
    target_level = avg_demand * reorder_multiplier + lost_penalty
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Description: ```
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    alpha = 0.2
    prev_forecast = history_demand[0] if history_demand else 0
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        prev_forecast = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * prev_forecast
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = max(1, round(prev_forecast * cost_ratio))
    target = max(1, round(prev_forecast + safety_stock))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, min(round(2 * prev_forecast), target - current_inv))
    
    return order_amount

# Description: ```
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = 5
    if len(history_demand) < demand_window:
        avg_demand = sum(history_demand) / max(1, len(history_demand))
    else:
        avg_demand = sum(history_demand[-demand_window:]) / demand_window
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    dynamic_base_stock = avg_demand * (1 + cost_ratio)
    
    order_cap = avg_demand * 1.5
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1]
    order_amount = min(max(0, dynamic_base_stock - inventory_position), order_cap)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 17)
# Objective: 5174.3081
# Description: An adaptive threshold algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand volatility and cost-sensitive safety stocks.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 50
    
    beta = 0.3
    gamma = 0.7
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        base_demand = history_demand[-1]
        volatility = 0
    else:
        base_demand = gamma * history_demand[-1] + (1 - gamma) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/max(1, len(history_demand)-1)
        volatility = (max(history_demand) - min(history_demand)) / max(1, sum(history_demand)/len(history_demand))
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = int(base_demand * (1 + beta * volatility * cost_ratio))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1]
    recent_loss = sum(history_lost[-2:])
    
    if recent_loss > 0:
        safety_stock = int(safety_stock * (1 + cost_ratio))
    
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 18)
# Objective: 5497.12493
# Description: The algorithm implements a Threshold-Adaptive Policy with Demand Learning, which dynamically adjusts order thresholds based on weighted demand patterns and cost-sensitive safety stock calculations, incorporating lost sales feedback for inventory correction.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    beta = 0.2  # Demand learning rate
    gamma = 0.4  # Lost sales adjustment factor
    min_history = 3  # Minimum periods for calculation
    
    if len(history_demand) < min_history:
        base_demand = sum(history_demand) / max(1, len(history_demand))
        recent_trend = 0
    else:
        weights = [beta * (1-beta)**i for i in range(len(history_demand)-1, -1, -1)]
        base_demand = sum(w*d for w,d in zip(weights, history_demand)) / sum(weights)
        recent_trend = (sum(history_demand[-2:]) - sum(history_demand[-4:-2])) / 2
    
    lost_adj = gamma * sum(history_lost[-2:]) if len(history_lost) >= 2 else 0
    cost_factor = (lost_sales_cost - holding_cost) / max(lost_sales_cost, holding_cost, 1)
    threshold = base_demand + recent_trend + lost_adj + (cost_factor * base_demand)
    
    order_amount = max(0, threshold - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 19)
# Objective: 5753.8
# Description: The algorithm implements a Demand-Weighted Adaptive Policy that dynamically adjusts order amounts based on smoothed demand trends, cost ratios, and real-time inventory deviations from target levels.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    smoothing_factor = 0.3
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    demand_window = history_demand[-3:] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand
    avg_demand = sum(demand_window) / len(demand_window) if demand_window else 0
    smoothed_demand = (1 - smoothing_factor) * avg_demand + smoothing_factor * (history_demand[-1] if history_demand else 0)
    target_level = max(1, round(smoothed_demand * (1 + cost_ratio)))
    deviation = target_level - history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else target_level
    order_amount = max(0, min(deviation, round(1.5 * smoothed_demand)))
    return order_amount

# Objective: 5886.744
# Description: An adaptive threshold algorithm that dynamically adjusts base stock levels using exponential smoothing of demand and a cost-sensitive safety factor based on the holding-to-lost-sales cost ratio.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    alpha = 0.2  # Smoothing factor
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        forecast = history_demand[-1] if history_demand else 0
    else:
        forecast = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * history_demand[-2]
    
    cost_ratio = holding_cost / lost_sales_cost
    safety_factor = 1 + (1 / (1 + cost_ratio))
    base_stock = max(10, forecast * safety_factor)
    order_amount = max(0, base_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 21)
# Objective: 6069.99178
# Description: A momentum-based inventory policy that adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand trends and cost-optimized buffer levels, with lead-time compensation through inventory position awareness.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    trend_weight = 0.4
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    recent_demand = history_demand[-3:] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / len(recent_demand)
    trend = history_demand[-1] - history_demand[-2] if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 0
    adjusted_demand = avg_demand + trend_weight * trend
    buffer = cost_ratio * adjusted_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_demand + buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 22)
# Objective: 6353.91071
# Description: A demand-responsive algorithm that combines quantile-based demand estimation with dynamic safety stock adjustment based on cost ratios and recent inventory performance.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return max(0, sum(history_demand[-3:]) - history_inventory[-1]) if len(history_demand) > 0 else 50
    
    demand_window = min(7, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = sorted(history_demand[-demand_window:])
    demand_estimate = recent_demand[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))]
    
    fill_rate = 1 - (sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6))
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    dynamic_factor = min(2.5, max(0.8, 0.5 * cost_ratio * (2 - fill_rate)))
    
    target = demand_estimate * dynamic_factor
    order_amount = max(0, target - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 23)
# Objective: 6371.61619
# Description: A volatility-adjusted algorithm that combines weighted historical demand volatility with a dynamic safety stock multiplier, adjusting order amounts based on recent lost sales patterns and asymmetric cost sensitivity.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    demand_volatility = (max(recent_demand) - min(recent_demand)) / (sum(recent_demand)/window + 1e-6)
    
    cost_sensitivity = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_multiplier = 1 + min(3, 0.5 * cost_sensitivity * demand_volatility)
    
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / window
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(recent_demand) + 1e-6)
    adjusted_demand = avg_demand * (1 + lost_sales_ratio) * safety_multiplier
    
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_demand - history_inventory[-1] + avg_demand)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 24)
# Objective: 6384.76535
# Description: 
A demand-shape-aware algorithm that dynamically weights recent demand patterns using exponential smoothing, incorporates lead-time-adjusted inventory gaps, and optimizes order amounts through a cost-sensitive demand quantile approach.

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    alpha = 0.3
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    quantile_demand = smoothed_demand * (1 + 2 * cost_ratio)
    
    lead_time_adjustment = max(0, smoothed_demand - history_inventory[-1])
    order_amount = max(0, quantile_demand - history_inventory[-1] + lead_time_adjustment)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 25)
# Objective: 6665.236
# Description: The new algorithm implements a Demand-Weighted Adaptive Threshold Policy that dynamically adjusts reorder points based on weighted historical demand patterns and cost-sensitive safety stock calculations.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return max(0, sum(history_demand[:3])/3 - history_inventory[-1]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1])
    
    weights = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else [0.6, 0.4]
    weighted_demand = sum(w * d for w, d in zip(weights, history_demand[-len(weights):]))
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)
    safety_factor = min(3.0, max(0.25, cost_ratio ** 0.33))
    safety_stock = max(1, int(weighted_demand * safety_factor))
    
    threshold = weighted_demand + safety_stock
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1]
    lost_last_period = history_lost[-1] if len(history_lost) > 0 else 0
    
    if current_inv <= threshold or lost_last_period > 0:
        order_amount = max(0, (weighted_demand * (1 + lost_last_period / max(1, weighted_demand))) + safety_stock - current_inv)
    else:
        order_amount = 0
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 6812.91997
# Description: A momentum-driven adaptive inventory algorithm that adjusts order quantities based on recent demand trends and cost-weighted safety buffers.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)
    momentum_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    buffer_factor = 1.0 + 0.5 * (cost_ratio ** 0.6)
    
    recent_demand = history_demand[-momentum_window:]
    demand_momentum = sum(recent_demand) / momentum_window
    lost_momentum = sum(history_lost[-momentum_window:]) / momentum_window
    
    adjusted_demand = 0.7 * demand_momentum + 0.3 * lost_momentum
    target_inventory = adjusted_demand * buffer_factor
    
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Objective: 6822.52738
# Description: The algorithm implements a Dynamic Cost-Adjusted Base Stock Policy that adjusts the base stock level based on demand variability and cost ratios, while smoothing order quantities using exponential smoothing of past demand.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    alpha = 0.3  # Smoothing factor
    safety_factor = 1.5  # Multiplier for demand variability
    
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        avg_demand = 50 if not history_demand else history_demand[0]
        demand_var = 0
    else:
        avg_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/max(1, len(history_demand)-1)
        demand_var = max(0, (max(history_demand) - min(history_demand)) / 2)
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1)
    dynamic_base = avg_demand + (safety_factor * demand_var * cost_ratio)
    
    order_amount = max(0, dynamic_base - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Objective: 7034.82426
# Description: A momentum-based inventory algorithm that adjusts orders using weighted demand trends and a dynamic cost-sensitive safety stock multiplier, incorporating recent lost sales feedback.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 3:
        return 100
    
    beta = 0.4  # Trend weight
    gamma = 0.2  # Lost sales feedback factor
    recent_trend = history_demand[-1] - history_demand[-2]
    forecast = history_demand[-1] + beta * recent_trend
    
    lost_ratio = (1 + gamma * history_lost[-1]) if history_lost else 1
    safety_factor = (lost_sales_cost * lost_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost * lost_ratio)
    safety_stock = safety_factor * forecast
    
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1]
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    return order_amount

    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2337.74011
# Description: A quantile-based adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock using cost-weighted demand percentiles and recent stockout patterns, with exponential smoothing for stability.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    smoothing_factor = 0.2
    recent_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-recent_window:]
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    quantile_level = min(0.8, 0.5 + 0.1 * cost_ratio)
    demand_estimate = sorted(recent_demand)[int(quantile_level * (len(recent_demand) - 1))]
    smoothed_demand = (1 - smoothing_factor) * (sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)) + smoothing_factor * demand_estimate
    
    stockout_severity = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + stockout_severity * min(2.0, cost_ratio * 0.3))
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description:  A quantile-based adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock using cost-weighted lost sales volatility and recent demand trends, with non-linear scaling of buffer sizes. 
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Demand estimation using 75th percentile
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand))
    demand_estimate = sorted_demand[quantile_idx] if quantile_idx < len(sorted_demand) else sorted_demand[-1]
    
    # Lost sales volatility factor (last 3 periods)
    lost_sales_vol = (sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6)) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = demand_estimate * (1 + min(2.0, lost_sales_vol * cost_ratio ** 0.7))
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_10.json (item 2)
# Objective: 2375.4
# Description: "A quantile-based adaptive policy that combines demand percentile forecasting with non-linear cost-sensitive safety stock adjustments, emphasizing recent stockout patterns while smoothing extreme fluctuations."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 50
    
    # Dynamic demand quantile (75th percentile adjusted by cost ratio)
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    quantile = 0.5 + 0.25 * min(1.0, cost_ratio**0.7)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    idx = min(len(sorted_demand)-1, int(quantile * len(sorted_demand)))
    forecast_demand = sorted_demand[idx]
    
    # Recent stockout emphasis (last 3 periods)
    recent_stockouts = sum(history_lost[-3:]) if len(history_lost) >= 3 else 0
    stockout_factor = 1.0 + min(1.0, recent_stockouts / (3 * forecast_demand + 1e-6))
    
    # Non-linear safety stock scaling
    safety_multiplier = min(2.0, 1.0 + (cost_ratio**0.7) * stockout_factor / 3)
    
    # Current inventory position
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order calculation
    order_amount = max(0, forecast_demand * safety_multiplier - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: A quantile-based adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock using recent demand volatility and cost-weighted lost sales severity, with exponential smoothing for trend responsiveness.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    smoothing_factor = 0.3
    recent_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-recent_window:]
    quantile = 0.75 if lost_sales_cost > holding_cost else 0.6
    demand_estimate = sorted(recent_demand)[int(quantile * (len(recent_demand) - 1))]
    smoothed_demand = (1 - smoothing_factor) * (sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)) + smoothing_factor * demand_estimate
    
    lost_severity = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (3 * smoothed_demand + 1e-6) if len(history_lost) >= 3 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    volatility = (max(recent_demand) - min(recent_demand)) / (smoothed_demand + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + min(2.0, lost_severity * cost_ratio * volatility))
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_10.json (item 4)
# Objective: 2400.1517
# Description: A demand-shape-responsive algorithm using dynamic quantile blending with asymmetric cost-sensitive safety stock adjustment and lead-time-aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Asymmetric quantile blending based on cost ratio
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    high_quantile = 0.5 + 0.3 * min(1.0, cost_ratio / 10)
    low_quantile = max(0.1, 0.5 - 0.2 * min(1.0, 10 / (cost_ratio + 1e-6)))
    
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    q_high = sorted_demand[min(int(high_quantile * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand)-1)]
    q_low = sorted_demand[min(int(low_quantile * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand)-1)]
    demand_estimate = 0.4 * q_low + 0.6 * q_high
    
    # Lead-time-adjusted lost sales tracking
    lost_window = min(5, len(history_lost))
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-lost_window:]) / (sum(history_demand[-lost_window:]) + 1e-6)
    
    # Dynamic safety stock with asymmetric scaling
    cost_exponent = 0.3 + 0.5 * (1 - 1/(1 + cost_ratio))
    safety_multiplier = min(1.8, (1 + lost_ratio) ** cost_exponent)
    safety_stock = demand_estimate * safety_multiplier
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2432.56927
# Description: A new algorithm that combines quantile-based demand forecasting with dynamic safety stock adjustments using weighted historical stockout rates and a non-linear cost-sensitive scaling factor.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 50
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation (75th percentile)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    base_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    # Dynamic stockout weighting
    stockout_rate = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.7  # Non-linear scaling
    
    # Safety stock calculation
    safety_stock = base_demand * (1 + min(stockout_rate * cost_ratio * 0.3, 0.6))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_10.json (item 6)
# Objective: 2445.45465
# Description: A novel demand-shaping algorithm that combines quantile-based demand estimation with momentum-adjusted inventory targets and asymmetric cost-sensitive buffers.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 75
    
    momentum_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    momentum = (sum(history_demand[-momentum_window:]) - sum(history_demand[-2*momentum_window:-momentum_window])) / momentum_window if len(history_demand) >= 2*momentum_window else 0
    
    quantile_window = min(10, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-quantile_window:]
    demand_q = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.65 * len(recent_demand))] if recent_demand else 0
    
    stress_factor = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_asymmetry = min(2.5, max(0.5, (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.4))
    dynamic_buffer = demand_q * (1 + stress_factor * cost_asymmetry * 0.5) + momentum * 0.7
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_q + dynamic_buffer - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2455.4091
# Description: ```
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Full-history quantile estimation (80th percentile)
    demand_quantile = sorted(history_demand)[int(0.8 * len(history_demand))] if len(history_demand) > 1 else history_demand[0]
    
    # Double EMA for lost sales ratio (alpha=0.2, beta=0.4)
    ema1 = ema2 = 0.0
    alpha, beta = 0.2, 0.4
    for i in range(len(history_lost)):
        ratio = history_lost[i] / (history_demand[i] + 1e-6)
        ema1 = alpha * ratio + (1 - alpha) * ema1
        ema2 = beta * ema1 + (1 - beta) * ema2
    
    # Dynamic cost-sensitive scaling with logistic function
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost * ema2) / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    scaling_factor = 1 + (3 / (1 + 2.718 ** (-0.5 * (cost_ratio - 2))))
    
    # Lead-time adjusted order calculation
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_quantile * scaling_factor - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Demand estimation using weighted moving average of top 30% demands
    top_demands = sorted(history_demand)[int(0.7 * len(history_demand)):]
    demand_estimate = sum(top_demands) / (len(top_demands) + 1e-6)
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on cost-weighted lost sales momentum
    lost_momentum = sum(history_lost[-3:]) - sum(history_lost[-6:-3]) if len(history_lost) >= 6 else 0
    cost_weight = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.6
    safety_stock = demand_estimate * (1 + min(1.8, max(0, lost_momentum * cost_weight / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6))))
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 75
    
    window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    p50 = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.5 * len(recent_demand))]
    p85 = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.85 * len(recent_demand))]
    demand_estimate = 0.3 * p50 + 0.7 * p85
    
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_factor = min(3.0, (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.5)
    safety_stock = demand_estimate * (1 + lost_ratio * cost_factor * 0.5)
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0

    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_10.json (item 10)
# Objective: 2609.50421
# Description: A demand-responsive algorithm that uses weighted demand volatility and cost-adaptive safety stock, dynamically adjusted by recent inventory performance and lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.4
    beta = 0.3
    gamma = 0.5
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = 0
    else:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/len(history_demand[:-1])
        demand_volatility = max(0, (max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:]))/smoothed_demand) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    inventory_ratio = 1 - (sum(history_inventory[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:])) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_adjustment = gamma * (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio + holding_cost * inventory_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment + demand_volatility) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_10.json (item 11)
# Objective: 2616.18673
# Description: 
"A demand-shaping algorithm using weighted harmonic means of cost-adjusted demand percentiles with momentum-based safety stock scaling, where order quantities are bounded by recent fulfillment rates and normalized cost penalties."

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(40, sum(history_demand[:3])/3) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 40
    
    # Harmonic mean of 70th/80th percentiles weighted by cost ratio
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    p70 = sorted_demand[int(0.7 * len(sorted_demand))] if len(sorted_demand) > 1 else sorted_demand[0]
    p80 = sorted_demand[int(0.8 * len(sorted_demand))] if len(sorted_demand) > 1 else sorted_demand[0]
    cost_weight = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.7
    base_demand = (2 * p70 * p80) / (p70 + p80 + 1e-6) * (1 + 0.3 * min(1.5, cost_weight))
    
    # Momentum-based safety stock with fulfillment dampening
    recent_fulfillment = 1 - sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6)
    momentum = min(1.2, 0.5 * (recent_fulfillment + cost_weight ** 0.5))
    safety_stock = base_demand * momentum * 0.35
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, min(2 * base_demand, base_demand + safety_stock - current_inv))
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2623.18437
# Description: A demand-shape-aware algorithm using weighted historical percentiles with momentum-based safety stock adjustment, where order quantities adapt to both cost ratios and recent demand acceleration patterns.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(30, sum(history_demand[:3])/3) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 30
    
    # Momentum-weighted demand estimation (75th percentile + 0.1*recent trend)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    recent_trend = sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 - sum(history_demand[-6:-3])/3 if len(history_demand) >=6 else 0
    base_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx] + 0.1 * recent_trend
    
    # Momentum-adjusted cost scaling
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)) ** 0.7
    recent_lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6)
    
    # Dynamic safety stock with momentum dampening
    safety_stock = base_demand * min(1.0, 0.3 * cost_ratio * (1 + 2*recent_lost_ratio))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

        return 100

# Objective: 2649.73013
# Description: An inventory algorithm using quantile-based demand estimation with dynamic safety stock scaling and cost-aware exponential smoothing.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation
    demand_window = history_demand[-5:] if len(history_demand) >= 5 else history_demand
    quantile = 0.75
    demand_estimate = sorted(demand_window)[min(len(demand_window)-1, int(quantile * len(demand_window)))]
    
    # Dynamic safety stock scaling
    recent_lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.5
    safety_factor = min(1.5, 1 + recent_lost_ratio * cost_ratio)
    safety_stock = demand_estimate * safety_factor
    
    # Exponential smoothing blend
    alpha = 0.2
    if len(history_demand) > 1:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * (sum(history_demand[:-1]) / (len(history_demand) - 1))
        demand_estimate = 0.7 * demand_estimate + 0.3 * smoothed_demand
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2654.92663
# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that uses quantile-based demand estimation with non-linear cost scaling, dynamically adjusting safety stock based on exponential moving averages of stockout severity and cost ratios.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation (75th percentile)
    demand_quantile = sorted(history_demand)[int(0.75 * len(history_demand))] if len(history_demand) > 1 else history_demand[0]
    
    # Exponential moving average of lost sales ratio (alpha=0.3)
    ema_lost_ratio = 0.0
    if len(history_lost) >= 1:
        alpha = 0.3
        for i in range(len(history_lost)):
            ema_lost_ratio = alpha * (history_lost[i] / (history_demand[i] + 1e-6)) + (1 - alpha) * ema_lost_ratio
    
    # Non-linear cost scaling factor
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost * ema_lost_ratio) / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    scaling_factor = min(2.5, 1 + (cost_ratio ** 0.7))
    
    # Dynamic safety stock
    safety_stock = demand_quantile * scaling_factor
    
    # Current inventory position
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_quantile + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Description: "A quantile-regression-based inventory policy that dynamically adjusts safety stock levels using demand quantiles and cost-ratio-weighted lost sales feedback, with asymmetric response to inventory surpluses vs shortages."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    quantile_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-quantile_window:]
    high_quantile = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))]
    median_demand = sorted(recent_demand)[len(recent_demand) // 2]
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    lost_sales_adjustment = 1.5 * cost_ratio * sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    shortage_penalty = 2.0 if inventory_position < median_demand else 1.0
    
    safety_stock = high_quantile + shortage_penalty * lost_sales_adjustment * median_demand
    order_amount = max(0, median_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

# Description: A momentum-based inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities using exponential smoothing of demand trends, weighted by a cost-sensitive shortage prevention factor and lead-time aware buffer stock.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(50, sum(history_demand[:3]) / 3) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 50
    
    alpha = 0.3
    window = min(4, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    smoothed_demand = alpha * sum(recent_demand) / window + (1 - alpha) * (sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand) if history_demand else 0)
    
    shortage_ratio = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(recent_demand) + 1e-6)
    cost_weight = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.5
    buffer_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + shortage_ratio * cost_weight)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + buffer_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that combines exponential smoothing with lost-sales-triggered safety stock adjustments, dynamically weighted by cost ratios.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    smoothing_factor = 0.3
    avg_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else (1 - smoothing_factor) * sum(history_demand[:-1]) / (len(history_demand) - 1) + smoothing_factor * history_demand[-1]
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    dynamic_buffer = avg_demand * (1 + lost_sales_ratio * cost_weight * 0.5)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand + dynamic_buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_10.json (item 25)
# Objective: 2715.71695
# Description: A reinforcement learning-inspired bandit algorithm that adaptively selects between three demand estimation strategies based on their recent cost performance, with lead-time-aware inventory balancing.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Strategy 1: Median-based
    def strategy_median():
        window = min(7, len(history_demand))
        recent = history_demand[-window:]
        return sorted(recent)[len(recent)//2]
    
    # Strategy 2: EMA with decay
    def strategy_ema():
        alpha = 0.3
        est = history_demand[0]
        for d in history_demand[1:]:
            est = alpha * d + (1-alpha) * est
        return est
    
    # Strategy 3: Worst-case recent
    def strategy_worst():
        return max(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else max(history_demand)
    
    # Select best performing strategy
    strategies = [strategy_median, strategy_ema, strategy_worst]
    strategy_perf = [0, 0, 0]
    lookback = min(5, len(history_demand))
    
    for i in range(lookback):
        for j, strat in enumerate(strategies):
            est = strat()
            actual = history_demand[i]
            cost = holding_cost * max(0, est - actual) + lost_sales_cost * max(0, actual - est)
            strategy_perf[j] += cost
    
    best_strat = strategies[strategy_perf.index(min(strategy_perf))]
    demand_estimate = best_strat()
    
    # Dynamic safety stock
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6)
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.33
    safety_stock = demand_estimate * min(1.8, 1 + lost_ratio * cost_ratio)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_10.json (item 26)
# Objective: 2717.62908
# Description: "A momentum-adjusted inventory policy that blends exponential smoothing of demand with cost-sensitive safety stock scaling, using weighted lost sales velocity to dynamically adjust reorder points."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Exponential smoothing parameters
    alpha = 0.3
    beta = 0.2
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
    
    # Lost sales momentum (3-period weighted average)
    lost_sales_window = history_lost[-3:] if len(history_lost) >= 3 else history_lost
    lost_sales_momentum = sum(lost_sales_window) / (len(lost_sales_window) + 1e-6)
    
    # Cost-sensitive scaling factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_factor = 1.0 + beta * cost_ratio * (1 + lost_sales_momentum / (smoothed_demand + 1e-6))
    
    # Current inventory position
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order calculation
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand * safety_factor - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(30, 1.5 * (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))
    
    recent_window = history_demand[-3:] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand
    base_demand = sum(recent_window) / len(recent_window)
    long_term_avg = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    blended_demand = 0.7 * base_demand + 0.3 * long_term_avg

    percentile = min(0.95, 0.5 + 0.1 * cost_ratio)
    safety_stock = sorted(history_demand)[int(percentile * (len(history_demand)-1))] if history_demand else 0
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, blended_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)

# Objective: 2744.4004
# Description: A reinforcement learning-inspired bandit algorithm that dynamically balances exploration of demand patterns with exploitation of cost-optimal ordering levels through adaptive regret minimization.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(10, sum(history_demand)/len(history_demand)) if history_demand else 50
    
    recent_loss_ratio = sum(history_lost[-2:])/(sum(history_demand[-2:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    exploration_weight = min(0.3, 0.1 * cost_ratio * (1 + recent_loss_ratio))
    
    demand_mean = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    demand_std = (sum((d - demand_mean)**2 for d in history_demand)/len(history_demand))**0.5 if len(history_demand) > 1 else demand_mean*0.5
    
    target_coverage = min(0.99, 0.7 + 0.05 * cost_ratio)
    safety_stock = demand_mean + demand_std * (target_coverage * 3 - 1)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, (demand_mean + exploration_weight * demand_std) + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_2.json (item 0)
# Objective: 2609.50421
# Description: A demand-responsive algorithm that uses weighted demand volatility and cost-adaptive safety stock, dynamically adjusted by recent inventory performance and lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.4
    beta = 0.3
    gamma = 0.5
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = 0
    else:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/len(history_demand[:-1])
        demand_volatility = max(0, (max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:]))/smoothed_demand) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    inventory_ratio = 1 - (sum(history_inventory[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:])) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_adjustment = gamma * (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio + holding_cost * inventory_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment + demand_volatility) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

    high_quantile = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))]
    median_demand = sorted(recent_demand)[len(recent_demand) // 2]
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    lost_sales_adjustment = 1.5 * cost_ratio * sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    shortage_penalty = 2.0 if inventory_position < median_demand else 1.0
    
    safety_stock = high_quantile + shortage_penalty * lost_sales_adjustment * median_demand
    order_amount = max(0, median_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2804.63261
# Description: A demand-shaping inventory policy that combines exponential smoothing of demand with cost-sensitive safety stock adjustment, dynamically weighted by recent lost sales patterns.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.3
    beta = 0.2
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/len(history_demand[:-1])
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    cost_adjustment = (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3114.64736
# Description: A volatility-adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on demand variability, cost-sensitive safety stock, and lead-time-aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    volatility_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-volatility_window:]
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / volatility_window
    demand_std = (sum((x - avg_demand)**2 for x in recent_demand) / volatility_window)**0.5 if volatility_window > 1 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_factor = 1.5 * cost_ratio * (1 + demand_std / (avg_demand + 1e-6))
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand * (1 + safety_factor) - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3170.36458
# Description: The new algorithm implements a Demand-Quantile Cost-Balancing Policy that uses quantile-based demand estimation with asymmetric cost penalties and lead-time aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    quantile_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(len(sorted_demand) * quantile_weight), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    quantile_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    cost_asymmetry = (lost_sales_cost - holding_cost) / max(lost_sales_cost, holding_cost)
    lead_time_adj = quantile_demand * max(0, cost_asymmetry)
    
    lost_sales_adj = sum(history_lost[-2:]) * 0.3 if history_lost else 0
    target = quantile_demand + lead_time_adj + lost_sales_adj
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_2.json (item 5)
# Objective: 3189.40968
# Description: A demand-adaptive algorithm with dynamic safety stock based on exponential smoothing of demand trends and cost-sensitive adjustment factors, incorporating lead time and real-time inventory performance.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.2
    beta = 0.4
    gamma = 0.6
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = 0
    else:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[-2:])/2 if len(history_demand) >= 2 else history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = (sum([abs(d - smoothed_demand) for d in history_demand[-3:]])/smoothed_demand) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and smoothed_demand > 0 else 0
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-2:])/sum(history_demand[-2:]) if len(history_demand) >= 2 and sum(history_demand[-2:]) > 0 else 0
    inventory_ratio = sum(history_inventory[-2:])/sum(history_demand[-2:]) if len(history_demand) >= 2 and sum(history_demand[-2:]) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_adjustment = gamma * (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio - holding_cost * inventory_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment + demand_volatility) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3217.26667
# Description: A novel inventory algorithm that uses quantile-based demand estimation with asymmetric cost penalties and lead-time-adjusted inventory positioning, dynamically adjusting ordering thresholds based on recent stockout severity.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation (75th percentile)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand) - 1)
    demand_estimate = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    # Asymmetric cost penalty factor
    penalty_ratio = (lost_sales_cost - holding_cost) / (lost_sales_cost + holding_cost)
    cost_factor = 1.0 + max(0, penalty_ratio)
    
    # Lead-time adjustment (since lead time = 1)
    lead_time_factor = 1.2
    
    # Recent stockout severity
    stockout_severity = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (3 * demand_estimate) if len(history_lost) >= 3 else 0
    
    # Dynamic threshold calculation
    threshold = demand_estimate * cost_factor * lead_time_factor * (1 + stockout_severity)
    
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, threshold - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Description: A momentum-driven inventory algorithm that adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand trends and cost-sensitive safety stock, incorporating lead time awareness and lost sales momentum.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 50
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(1e-6, holding_cost)
    momentum_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    momentum = sum(history_demand[-momentum_window:]) / momentum_window
    
    lost_momentum = 0.4 * sum(history_lost[-3:]) / min(3, len(history_lost)) if history_lost else 0
    safety_factor = min(2.0, max(0.5, 0.7 * cost_ratio))
    
    base_demand = 0.6 * momentum + 0.4 * (sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand))
    target_level = base_demand * safety_factor + lost_momentum
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3475.55393
# Description: A reinforcement learning-inspired inventory algorithm that uses demand pattern clustering with cost-weighted exploration-exploitation tradeoffs, dynamic safety stock buffers based on regime detection, and lead-time-adjusted ordering with lost sales memory decay.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 50
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(1e-6, holding_cost)
    demand_clusters = [history_demand[i:i+3] for i in range(len(history_demand)-2)] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else [history_demand]
    current_pattern = history_demand[-3:] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand
    
    pattern_similarity = [sum(abs(a-b) for a,b in zip(current_pattern, cluster))/3 
                         for cluster in demand_clusters] if demand_clusters else [0]
    min_similarity = min(pattern_similarity) if pattern_similarity else 0
    exploration_weight = 1 - min(0.9, max(0.1, min_similarity/sum(history_demand)*3)) if sum(history_demand) > 0 else 0.5
    
    regime_demand = sum(current_pattern)/3 if current_pattern else sum(history_demand)/max(1, len(history_demand))
    historical_demand = sum(history_demand)/len(history_demand)
    blended_demand = exploration_weight * regime_demand + (1-exploration_weight) * historical_demand
    
    lost_sales_decay = sum(l*q for l,q in zip(history_lost[-3:], [0.5, 0.3, 0.2])) if history_lost else 0
    safety_buffer = min(2.2, max(0.7, 0.4 * cost_ratio * (1 + exploration_weight)))
    
    target_level = blended_demand * safety_buffer + lost_sales_decay
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Description: An adaptive quantile-based algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities using demand percentiles weighted by cost ratios, with exponential smoothing of recent lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) == 0:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1])
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    recent_lost = history_lost[-window:]
    smoothed_lost = 0.5 * sum(recent_lost) / window + 0.5 * recent_lost[-1] if recent_lost else 0
    
    q75 = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))] if recent_demand else 0
    base_quantity = (0.6 * q75 + 0.4 * max(recent_demand)) * (1 + min(1.0, cost_ratio * 0.3))
    
    adjusted_quantity = base_quantity + (0.7 * smoothed_lost * min(2.0, cost_ratio * 0.5))
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_quantity - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:

    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)

A hybrid algorithm combining exponential smoothing of demand percentiles with lead-time-adjusted safety stock that reacts to both inventory position and recent lost sales patterns.

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 50
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)
    alpha = min(0.9, max(0.1, 1 - 1/(1 + cost_ratio**0.5)))
    window = min(10, max(3, int(len(history_demand)**0.7)))
    
    adjusted_demand = [d + (0.8 * l if i >= len(history_demand)-3 else 0) 
                      for i, (d, l) in enumerate(zip(history_demand[-window:], history_lost[-window:]))]
    smoothed = sorted(adjusted_demand)[int(0.7 * len(adjusted_demand))]
    forecast = alpha * smoothed + (1-alpha) * (sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 if len(history_demand)>=3 else smoothed)
    
    safety_mult = min(2.5, max(0.3, cost_ratio**0.4))
    safety_stock = max(1, int(forecast * safety_mult * 0.6))
    
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3748.8626
# Description: A reinforcement learning-inspired bandit algorithm that dynamically balances exploration of demand patterns with exploitation of cost-optimal ordering strategies using adaptive regret minimization and lead-time-adjusted inventory targets.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(1e-6, holding_cost)
    exploration_factor = min(1.5, max(0.3, 1 - 1/(1 + cost_ratio)))
    decay_rate = 0.9
    
    weighted_demands = []
    weights = []
    for i in range(len(history_demand)):
        weight = decay_rate ** (len(history_demand) - i - 1)
        effective_demand = history_demand[i] + history_lost[i] * exploration_factor
        weighted_demands.append(effective_demand * weight)
        weights.append(weight)
    
    if not weighted_demands:
        return 0
    
    weighted_avg = sum(weighted_demands) / sum(weights)
    lead_time_adjusted = weighted_avg * (1 + exploration_factor)
    
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, lead_time_adjusted - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

    order_amount = max(0, target - current_inv)

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

        return 0
    beta = 0.4
    cost_sensitivity = (lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)) ** 0.5
    avg_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)

    lost_sales_impact = sum(history_lost[-2:]) * beta if history_lost else 0

    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target - current_inv)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

    beta = 0.2
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        forecast = history_demand[0] if len(history_demand) > 0 else 0

    order_amount = max(0, target_level - current_position)

# Description: The algorithm implements a Demand-Adaptive Threshold Policy that dynamically adjusts order amounts based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, using exponential smoothing for demand forecasting and a variable ordering interval determined by inventory risk.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1])
    
    alpha = 0.3
    forecast = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * history_demand[-2]
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    threshold = int(forecast * (1 + cost_ratio))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1]
    safety_stock = int(forecast * 0.5)
    order_interval = max(1, int(2 * (1 - cost_ratio)))
    
    if len(history_inventory) % order_interval == 0 or current_inv < safety_stock:
        order_amount = max(0, threshold + safety_stock - current_inv)
    else:
        order_amount = 0
    
    return order_amount

# Description: An adaptive inventory algorithm that uses weighted demand forecasting with dynamic safety stock adjustment based on recent lost sales trends and cost ratios.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return 100
    
    # Weighted demand forecast (recent periods have higher weight)
    weights = [0.6, 0.4] if len(history_demand) >= 2 else [1.0]
    forecast = sum(w * d for w, d in zip(weights, history_demand[-len(weights):])) / sum(weights)
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on lost sales trend
    lost_sales_trend = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / 2 if len(history_lost) >= 2 else 0
    safety_factor = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)) * (1 + lost_sales_trend / forecast if forecast > 0 else 1)
    safety_stock = safety_factor * forecast
    
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1]
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    return order_amount

# Description:

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    quantile_window = min(10, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-quantile_window:]
    demand_75 = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))]

    demand_variability = (demand_75 - demand_25) / max(1, demand_25)

    lost_sales_weight = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / max(1, sum(history_demand[-2:])) if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 0
    
    target_inventory = forecast_demand * (1 + safety_factor * (1 + lost_sales_weight))
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - inventory_position)
