# Objective: 4532.05333
# Description: An adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock based on recent lost sales trends and demand variability, while using exponential smoothing for demand forecasting and cost-sensitive inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) == 0:
        return 0
    
    # Exponential smoothing for demand forecast
    alpha = 0.3
    forecast = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        forecast = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * forecast
    
    # Lost sales trend adjustment
    lost_window = 3
    if len(history_lost) >= lost_window:
        recent_lost = sum(history_lost[-lost_window:]) / lost_window
        lost_ratio = min(1.0, recent_lost / max(1, forecast))
    else:
        lost_ratio = 0
    
    # Cost-sensitive safety factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_factor = 1.0 + (cost_ratio * (1 + lost_ratio))
    
    # Calculate target inventory
    target_inventory = forecast * safety_factor
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if len(history_inventory) > 0 else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 100 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 100
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(1e-6, holding_cost)
    smoothing_factor = 0.3
    recent_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else (
        smoothing_factor * history_demand[-1] + (1 - smoothing_factor) * sum(history_demand[-recent_window:-1])/(recent_window-1))
    
    forecast_errors = [abs(d - smoothed_demand) for d in history_demand[-recent_window:]]
    avg_error = sum(forecast_errors) / recent_window if forecast_errors else 0
    safety_multiplier = min(2.5, max(1.0, 0.7 * cost_ratio * (1 + avg_error/(smoothed_demand + 1e-6))))
    
    target_level = smoothed_demand * safety_multiplier
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Description: A demand-responsive inventory policy that combines quantile-based demand forecasting with asymmetric cost weighting, dynamically adjusting safety stock based on recent inventory coverage and lost sales severity.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    quantile_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-quantile_window:]
    demand_75th = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))]
    demand_forecast = 0.6 * demand_75th + 0.4 * (sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand))
    
    coverage_ratio = sum(history_inventory[-3:]) / sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 1
    severity = (lost_sales_cost * sum(history_lost[-2:])) / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    
    safety_factor = max(0.5, min(2.0, 1 + (severity / (coverage_ratio + 1e-6))))
    safety_stock = safety_factor * demand_forecast
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_forecast + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 4646.97239
# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand patterns, recent stockout severity, and a cost-adaptive safety buffer that evolves with inventory turnover trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 50
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)
    beta = 1 - 1/(1 + cost_ratio**0.3)
    recent_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    
    demand_weights = [beta**(recent_window-i) for i in range(recent_window)]
    weighted_demand = sum(d*w for d,w in zip(history_demand[-recent_window:], demand_weights)) / sum(demand_weights)
    
    stockout_penalty = 1 + (sum(history_lost[-3:])/3 if len(history_lost)>=3 else 0) / max(1, weighted_demand)
    volatility = (max(history_demand[-5:]) - min(history_demand[-5:]))/max(1, weighted_demand) if len(history_demand)>=5 else 0.5
    
    buffer_factor = min(3.0, max(0.5, cost_ratio**0.25 * stockout_penalty * (1 + volatility)))
    target_level = weighted_demand * buffer_factor
    
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Description: An adaptive moving average algorithm that dynamically adjusts base stock levels based on recent demand trends and cost ratios, while incorporating safety stock to balance holding and lost sales costs.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 3:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1])
    
    window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    demand_avg = sum(history_demand[-window:]) / window
    lost_avg = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / window
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / holding_cost
    safety_factor = min(2.0, max(1.0, cost_ratio * 0.5))
    adjusted_demand = demand_avg + (lost_avg * 0.5)
    
    base_stock = adjusted_demand * safety_factor
    order_amount = max(0, base_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return 100  # Default initial order
    
    alpha = 0.3  # Smoothing factor
    forecast = history_demand[-1] * alpha + (1 - alpha) * history_demand[-2]

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_2.json (item 29)
# Objective: 5032.7883
# Description: A novel algorithm that combines exponential smoothing demand forecasting with dynamic safety stock calculation and cost-weighted order adjustment, incorporating lead-time compensation and recent stockout trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Exponential smoothing demand forecast (alpha=0.3)
    forecast = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else 0
    for i in range(1, len(history_demand)):
        forecast = 0.3 * history_demand[i] + 0.7 * forecast
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on demand variance
    mean_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    variance = sum((d - mean_demand)**2 for d in history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    safety_stock = max(0, variance**0.5 * (1 + holding_cost / lost_sales_cost))
    
    # Cost-weighted adjustment factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    adjustment = min(2.0, max(0.5, 0.8 * cost_ratio))
    
    # Lead-time compensation (1 period)
    lead_time_buffer = forecast * 0.25
    
    # Recent stockout impact (last 2 periods)
    stockout_impact = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / (2 * forecast + 1e-6)
    
    # Target inventory level calculation
    target = (forecast + safety_stock) * adjustment + lead_time_buffer
    target *= (1 + min(1.0, stockout_impact))
    
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2609.50421
# Description: A demand-responsive algorithm that uses weighted demand volatility and cost-adaptive safety stock, dynamically adjusted by recent inventory performance and lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.4
    beta = 0.3
    gamma = 0.5
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = 0
    else:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/len(history_demand[:-1])
        demand_volatility = max(0, (max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:]))/smoothed_demand) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    inventory_ratio = 1 - (sum(history_inventory[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:])) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_adjustment = gamma * (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio + holding_cost * inventory_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment + demand_volatility) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: "A quantile-regression-based inventory policy that dynamically adjusts safety stock levels using demand quantiles and cost-ratio-weighted lost sales feedback, with asymmetric response to inventory surpluses vs shortages."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    quantile_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-quantile_window:]
    high_quantile = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))]
    median_demand = sorted(recent_demand)[len(recent_demand) // 2]
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    lost_sales_adjustment = 1.5 * cost_ratio * sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    shortage_penalty = 2.0 if inventory_position < median_demand else 1.0
    
    safety_stock = high_quantile + shortage_penalty * lost_sales_adjustment * median_demand
    order_amount = max(0, median_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

    weights = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2][:window]

    order_amount = max(0, weighted_demand + dynamic_buffer - inventory_position)

# Objective: 3085.23628
# Description: A reinforcement learning-inspired approach that dynamically weights demand patterns and lost sales severity to compute safety stock, using a moving average of cost-normalized demand deviations and lost sales ratios to determine optimal order quantities.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Weighted demand smoothing (recent 3 periods vs all history)
    recent_weight = 0.6
    if len(history_demand) >= 3:
        recent_demand = sum(history_demand[-3:]) / 3
        historical_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
        smoothed_demand = recent_weight * recent_demand + (1 - recent_weight) * historical_demand
    else:
        smoothed_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    
    # Normalized lost sales ratio
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on cost balance
    cost_balance = (lost_sales_cost * lost_ratio) / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + min(2.0, cost_balance))  # Cap at 3x demand
    
    # Compute order amount
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Description: A volatility-adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on demand variability, cost-sensitive safety stock, and lead-time-aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    volatility_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-volatility_window:]
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / volatility_window
    demand_std = (sum((x - avg_demand)**2 for x in recent_demand) / volatility_window)**0.5 if volatility_window > 1 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)

    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand * (1 + safety_factor) - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3140.96167
# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that combines quantile-based safety stock calculation with momentum-adjusted demand forecasting, dynamically weighted by cost-sensitive error correction.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_sensitivity = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    momentum_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    quantile_level = min(0.9, max(0.5, 1 - 1/(1 + cost_sensitivity)))
    
    recent_demand = history_demand[-momentum_window:]
    demand_momentum = sum(recent_demand) / len(recent_demand) if recent_demand else 0
    safety_stock = sorted(history_demand)[int(quantile_level * len(history_demand))] if history_demand else 0
    
    error_adjustment = 0
    if len(history_lost) > 1:
        recent_error = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / 2
        error_adjustment = recent_error * (cost_sensitivity / (1 + cost_sensitivity))
    
    forecast = demand_momentum + error_adjustment
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3155.93492
# Description: A momentum-driven inventory algorithm that adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand acceleration, cost-adaptive safety buffers, and recent stockout severity.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 150
    
    theta = 0.3
    kappa = 0.7
    phi = 0.5
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        base_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_momentum = 0
    else:
        base_demand = theta * history_demand[-1] + (1 - theta) * sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand[-1]
        demand_momentum = (history_demand[-1] - history_demand[-2]) if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 0
    
    stockout_severity = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost * stockout_severity) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    dynamic_buffer = (1 + kappa * cost_ratio + phi * abs(demand_momentum)/max(1, base_demand)) * base_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + dynamic_buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3170.36458
# Description: The new algorithm implements a Demand-Quantile Cost-Balancing Policy that uses quantile-based demand estimation with asymmetric cost penalties and lead-time aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    quantile_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(len(sorted_demand) * quantile_weight), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    quantile_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    cost_asymmetry = (lost_sales_cost - holding_cost) / max(lost_sales_cost, holding_cost)
    lead_time_adj = quantile_demand * max(0, cost_asymmetry)
    
    lost_sales_adj = sum(history_lost[-2:]) * 0.3 if history_lost else 0
    target = quantile_demand + lead_time_adj + lost_sales_adj
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target - current_inv)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 100 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 100
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(1e-6, holding_cost)
    alpha = 0.2
    forecast = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * (sum(history_demand[:-1])/len(history_demand[:-1]) if len(history_demand) > 1 else history_demand[-1])
    
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-2:])/sum(history_demand[-2:]) if len(history_demand) >= 2 and sum(history_demand[-2:]) > 0 else 0
    safety_factor = min(2.0, 1.0 + 0.5 * cost_ratio * (1 + lost_ratio))
    
    target = forecast * safety_factor
    order_amount = max(0, target - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3217.26667
# Description: A novel inventory algorithm that uses quantile-based demand estimation with asymmetric cost penalties and lead-time-adjusted inventory positioning, dynamically adjusting ordering thresholds based on recent stockout severity.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation (75th percentile)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand) - 1)
    demand_estimate = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    # Asymmetric cost penalty factor
    penalty_ratio = (lost_sales_cost - holding_cost) / (lost_sales_cost + holding_cost)
    cost_factor = 1.0 + max(0, penalty_ratio)
    
    # Lead-time adjustment (since lead time = 1)
    lead_time_factor = 1.2
    
    # Recent stockout severity
    stockout_severity = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (3 * demand_estimate) if len(history_lost) >= 3 else 0
    
    # Dynamic threshold calculation
    threshold = demand_estimate * cost_factor * lead_time_factor * (1 + stockout_severity)
    
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, threshold - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3224.10146
# Description: A demand-responsive algorithm that uses weighted moving averages with volatility-adjusted safety stock, dynamically scaled by cost ratios and recent demand variability.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(4, len(history_demand))
    weights = [0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1][:window]
    weighted_avg = sum(w * d for w, d in zip(weights, history_demand[-window:]))
    
    demand_std = (sum((d - weighted_avg)**2 for d in history_demand[-window:]) / window)**0.5 if window > 1 else 0
    volatility_factor = min(2.0, 1 + demand_std / (weighted_avg + 1e-6))
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    recent_lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / (sum(history_demand[-2:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 0
    
    safety_stock = weighted_avg * volatility_factor * (1 + recent_lost_ratio * cost_ratio * 0.3)
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, weighted_avg + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 16)
# Objective: 3331.88199
# Description: ```
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Exponential smoothing for demand forecast
    alpha = 0.3
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        forecast = history_demand[-1]
    else:
        forecast = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1]) / len(history_demand[:-1])
    
    # Demand volatility based on rolling standard deviation
    if len(history_demand) >= 3:
        recent_demand = history_demand[-3:]
        mean_demand = sum(recent_demand) / 3
        volatility = (sum((d - mean_demand)**2 for d in recent_demand) / 3)**0.5 / mean_demand if mean_demand > 0 else 0
    else:
        volatility = 0
    
    # Cost-adaptive risk factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    risk_factor = cost_ratio * (1 + volatility)
    
    # Dynamic adjustment based on recent lost sales performance
    if len(history_lost) >= 3:
        lost_performance = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / sum(history_demand[-3:]) if sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
        risk_factor *= (1 + lost_performance)
    
    # Order calculation
    safety_stock = risk_factor * forecast
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3423.84158
# Description: A momentum-driven inventory algorithm that adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand trends and cost-sensitive safety stock, incorporating lead time awareness and lost sales momentum.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 50
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(1e-6, holding_cost)
    momentum_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    momentum = sum(history_demand[-momentum_window:]) / momentum_window
    
    lost_momentum = 0.4 * sum(history_lost[-3:]) / min(3, len(history_lost)) if history_lost else 0
    safety_factor = min(2.0, max(0.5, 0.7 * cost_ratio))
    
    base_demand = 0.6 * momentum + 0.4 * (sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand))
    target_level = base_demand * safety_factor + lost_momentum
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 18)
# Objective: 3430.17944
# Description: An adaptive safety stock algorithm using exponential smoothing of demand and lost sales, weighted by cost ratios and adjusted for recent volatility.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 100 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 100
    
    alpha = 0.3  # Smoothing factor for demand
    beta = 0.2   # Smoothing factor for lost sales
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
    smoothed_lost = history_lost[-1] if history_lost else 0
    
    for i in range(len(history_demand)-1):
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[i] + (1-alpha) * smoothed_demand
        if history_lost:
            smoothed_lost = beta * history_lost[i] + (1-beta) * smoothed_lost
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    volatility = max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    safety_factor = cost_ratio * (1 + 0.5 * volatility / (smoothed_demand + 1e-6)) * (1 + smoothed_lost / (smoothed_demand + 1e-6))
    target_inventory = smoothed_demand * (1 + safety_factor)
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3475.55393
# Description: A reinforcement learning-inspired inventory algorithm that uses demand pattern clustering with cost-weighted exploration-exploitation tradeoffs, dynamic safety stock buffers based on regime detection, and lead-time-adjusted ordering with lost sales memory decay.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 50
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(1e-6, holding_cost)
    demand_clusters = [history_demand[i:i+3] for i in range(len(history_demand)-2)] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else [history_demand]
    current_pattern = history_demand[-3:] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand
    
    pattern_similarity = [sum(abs(a-b) for a,b in zip(current_pattern, cluster))/3 
                         for cluster in demand_clusters] if demand_clusters else [0]
    min_similarity = min(pattern_similarity) if pattern_similarity else 0
    exploration_weight = 1 - min(0.9, max(0.1, min_similarity/sum(history_demand)*3)) if sum(history_demand) > 0 else 0.5
    
    regime_demand = sum(current_pattern)/3 if current_pattern else sum(history_demand)/max(1, len(history_demand))
    historical_demand = sum(history_demand)/len(history_demand)
    blended_demand = exploration_weight * regime_demand + (1-exploration_weight) * historical_demand
    
    lost_sales_decay = sum(l*q for l,q in zip(history_lost[-3:], [0.5, 0.3, 0.2])) if history_lost else 0
    safety_buffer = min(2.2, max(0.7, 0.4 * cost_ratio * (1 + exploration_weight)))
    
    target_level = blended_demand * safety_buffer + lost_sales_decay
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3510.98855
# Description: Novel algorithm using weighted demand percentiles, dynamic safety stock based on cost imbalance, and lead-time-proportional adjustments with multiplicative stockout correction.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Weighted demand percentile (60th percentile with recent emphasis)
    weighted_demand = sorted(history_demand)[int(0.6 * len(history_demand))] if len(history_demand) > 1 else history_demand[-1]
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on cost imbalance
    cost_imbalance = (lost_sales_cost - holding_cost) / (lost_sales_cost + holding_cost)
    safety_stock = weighted_demand * max(0.1, min(0.9, 0.5 + cost_imbalance))
    
    # Lead-time-proportional adjustment
    lead_time_adj = weighted_demand * 0.3
    
    # Multiplicative stockout correction (3-period window)
    stockout_adj = 1 + (sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (3 * weighted_demand)) if len(history_lost) >= 3 else 1
    
    target = (weighted_demand + safety_stock + lead_time_adj) * stockout_adj
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3519.25455
# Description: An adaptive quantile-based algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities using demand percentiles weighted by cost ratios, with exponential smoothing of recent lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) == 0:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1])
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    recent_lost = history_lost[-window:]
    smoothed_lost = 0.5 * sum(recent_lost) / window + 0.5 * recent_lost[-1] if recent_lost else 0
    
    q75 = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))] if recent_demand else 0
    base_quantity = (0.6 * q75 + 0.4 * max(recent_demand)) * (1 + min(1.0, cost_ratio * 0.3))
    
    adjusted_quantity = base_quantity + (0.7 * smoothed_lost * min(2.0, cost_ratio * 0.5))
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_quantity - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 22)
# Objective: 3623.23763
# Description: Novel algorithm using weighted demand percentiles, dynamic safety stock scaling based on cost imbalance, and lead-time-proportional demand anticipation with multiplicative stockout correction.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Weighted demand percentile (60th percentile with 0.4 weight on recent demand)
    recent_weight = 0.4
    base_demand = sorted(history_demand)[int(0.6 * len(history_demand)) - 1] if len(history_demand) > 1 else history_demand[-1]
    weighted_demand = recent_weight * history_demand[-1] + (1 - recent_weight) * base_demand
    
    # Dynamic safety stock scaling based on cost imbalance
    cost_imbalance = (lost_sales_cost - holding_cost) / (lost_sales_cost + holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = weighted_demand * (0.5 + 0.5 * max(0, cost_imbalance))
    
    # Lead-time-proportional demand anticipation (multiplicative)
    lead_time_factor = 1.2 if len(history_demand) > 3 else 1.0
    
    # Multiplicative stockout correction (3-period window)
    stockout_factor = 1 + (sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (3 * weighted_demand + 1e-6)) if len(history_lost) >= 3 else 1.0
    
    target = (weighted_demand + safety_stock) * lead_time_factor * stockout_factor
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3703.76
# Description: A quantile-based demand shaping algorithm that dynamically adjusts order amounts using empirical demand percentiles weighted by cost-sensitive risk factors.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    risk_factor = min(2.0, max(0.5, cost_ratio ** 0.7))
    window = min(5, max(2, len(history_demand) // 2))
    
    effective_demands = [d + 0.5 * l for d, l in zip(history_demand[-window:], history_lost[-window:])]
    sorted_demands = sorted(effective_demands)
    quantile_idx = min(len(sorted_demands)-1, int(0.75 * len(sorted_demands)))
    target = sorted_demands[quantile_idx] * risk_factor
    
    order_amount = max(0, target - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3712.99323
# Description: 
A hybrid algorithm combining exponential smoothing of demand percentiles with lead-time-adjusted safety stock that reacts to both inventory position and recent lost sales patterns.

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 50
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)
    alpha = min(0.9, max(0.1, 1 - 1/(1 + cost_ratio**0.5)))
    window = min(10, max(3, int(len(history_demand)**0.7)))
    
    adjusted_demand = [d + (0.8 * l if i >= len(history_demand)-3 else 0) 
                      for i, (d, l) in enumerate(zip(history_demand[-window:], history_lost[-window:]))]
    smoothed = sorted(adjusted_demand)[int(0.7 * len(adjusted_demand))]
    forecast = alpha * smoothed + (1-alpha) * (sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 if len(history_demand)>=3 else smoothed)
    
    safety_mult = min(2.5, max(0.3, cost_ratio**0.4))
    safety_stock = max(1, int(forecast * safety_mult * 0.6))
    
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 25)
# Objective: 3748.8626
# Description: A reinforcement learning-inspired bandit algorithm that dynamically balances exploration of demand patterns with exploitation of cost-optimal ordering strategies using adaptive regret minimization and lead-time-adjusted inventory targets.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(1e-6, holding_cost)
    exploration_factor = min(1.5, max(0.3, 1 - 1/(1 + cost_ratio)))
    decay_rate = 0.9
    
    weighted_demands = []
    weights = []
    for i in range(len(history_demand)):
        weight = decay_rate ** (len(history_demand) - i - 1)
        effective_demand = history_demand[i] + history_lost[i] * exploration_factor
        weighted_demands.append(effective_demand * weight)
        weights.append(weight)
    
    if not weighted_demands:
        return 0
    
    weighted_avg = sum(weighted_demands) / sum(weights)
    lead_time_adjusted = weighted_avg * (1 + exploration_factor)
    
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, lead_time_adjusted - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3774.46086
# Description: An inventory momentum algorithm that combines volatility-adjusted demand forecasting with cost-sensitive inventory momentum and lead-time aware buffer stocks.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    avg_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    demand_volatility = (max(history_demand) - min(history_demand)) / (avg_demand + 1e-6)
    
    momentum_factor = 1.0
    if len(history_demand) > 1:
        recent_trend = (history_demand[-1] - history_demand[-2]) / (history_demand[-2] + 1e-6)
        momentum_factor = 1.0 + cost_ratio * recent_trend
    
    buffer_stock = avg_demand * (1 + demand_volatility) * cost_ratio
    lost_sales_buffer = sum(history_lost[-2:]) * cost_ratio if history_lost else 0
    
    target = (avg_demand * momentum_factor) + buffer_stock + lost_sales_buffer
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Description: A reinforcement learning-inspired algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on normalized cost ratios and demand volatility, using a weighted combination of recent demand patterns and lost sales history to optimize inventory decisions without forecasting.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 100 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 100
    
    window = min(4, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    recent_lost = history_lost[-window:] if history_lost else [0]*window
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / window
    avg_lost = sum(recent_lost) / window
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    volatility = (sum((d - avg_demand)**2 for d in recent_demand) / window)**0.5 if window > 1 else 0
    lost_ratio = avg_lost / (avg_demand + avg_lost + 1e-6)
    
    dynamic_factor = cost_ratio * (1 + volatility/(avg_demand + 1e-6)) * (1 + lost_ratio)
    target_inventory = avg_demand * (1 + dynamic_factor)
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 28)
# Objective: 3779.75604
# Description: Novel algorithm using weighted demand quartiles for stability, dynamic safety stock based on cost imbalance, and lead-time-proportional lost sales correction with decay.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Weighted demand quartiles (25th/75th as anchors)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    n = len(sorted_demand)
    q1 = sorted_demand[n//4] if n >=4 else sorted_demand[0]
    q3 = sorted_demand[3*n//4] if n >=4 else sorted_demand[-1]
    weighted_demand = 0.6*q3 + 0.4*q1
    
    # Dynamic safety stock (cost-imbalance weighted)
    cost_imbalance = (lost_sales_cost - holding_cost) / (lost_sales_cost + holding_cost)
    safety_stock = weighted_demand * (0.5 + 0.5*cost_imbalance)
    
    # Lead-time lost sales correction (with decay factor)
    recent_lost = sum(l * (0.7**i) for i, l in enumerate(reversed(history_lost[-3:])))
    lost_correction = recent_lost * (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))
    
    target = weighted_demand + safety_stock + lost_correction
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that combines exponential smoothing with lost-sales-triggered safety stock adjustments, using weighted historical performance to adapt order quantities.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    smoothing_factor = 0.3
    avg_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else (1 - smoothing_factor) * sum(history_demand[:-1]) / (len(history_demand) - 1) + smoothing_factor * history_demand[-1]
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_lost) >= 3 else 0
    cost_sensitivity = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    
    dynamic_multiplier = 1 + cost_sensitivity * (1 + lost_sales_ratio)
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand * dynamic_multiplier - inventory_position)
    
    return order_amount

# Description: A new algorithm that combines quantile-based demand forecasting with dynamic safety stock adjustments using weighted historical stockout rates and a non-linear cost-sensitive scaling factor.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 50
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation (75th percentile)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    base_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    # Dynamic stockout weighting
    stockout_rate = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.7  # Non-linear scaling

    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_4.json (item 1)
# Objective: 2609.50421
# Description: A demand-responsive algorithm that uses weighted demand volatility and cost-adaptive safety stock, dynamically adjusted by recent inventory performance and lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.4
    beta = 0.3
    gamma = 0.5
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = 0
    else:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/len(history_demand[:-1])
        demand_volatility = max(0, (max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:]))/smoothed_demand) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    inventory_ratio = 1 - (sum(history_inventory[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:])) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_adjustment = gamma * (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio + holding_cost * inventory_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment + demand_volatility) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: "A quantile-regression-based inventory policy that dynamically adjusts safety stock levels using demand quantiles and cost-ratio-weighted lost sales feedback, with asymmetric response to inventory surpluses vs shortages."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    quantile_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-quantile_window:]
    high_quantile = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))]
    median_demand = sorted(recent_demand)[len(recent_demand) // 2]
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    lost_sales_adjustment = 1.5 * cost_ratio * sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    shortage_penalty = 2.0 if inventory_position < median_demand else 1.0
    
    safety_stock = high_quantile + shortage_penalty * lost_sales_adjustment * median_demand
    order_amount = max(0, median_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:

    smoothing_factor = 0.3

    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand + dynamic_buffer - inventory_position)

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_4.json (item 6)
# Objective: 2926.03
# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock using quantile forecasting and asymmetric cost weighting, with lead-time-aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(100, 2 * (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))  # Cost-aware default
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    quantile = min(0.95, 0.5 + 0.3 * (cost_ratio - 1))  # Asymmetric adjustment
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    idx = min(len(sorted_demand) - 1, int(quantile * len(sorted_demand)))
    base_demand = sorted_demand[idx]
    
    # Lead-time demand correction
    if len(history_demand) > 1:
        lead_time_demand = sum(history_demand[-2:]) / 2  # L=1 period
        base_demand = 0.7 * base_demand + 0.3 * lead_time_demand
    
    # Dynamic lost sales penalty factor
    lost_penalty = 1.0
    if len(history_lost) >= 2:
        recent_lost = sum(history_lost[-2:])
        if recent_lost > 0:
            lost_penalty += min(1.0, recent_lost / (base_demand + 1e-6))
    
    # Inventory position with lead time
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    pipeline = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) >= 1 else 0  # L=1 assumption
    inventory_position = current_inventory - pipeline
    
    # Asymmetric order calculation
    order_amount = max(0, (base_demand * lost_penalty) - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    weights = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2][:window]
    weighted_demand = sum(d * w for d, w in zip(history_demand[-window:], weights)) / sum(weights)

    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    dynamic_buffer = weighted_demand * (1 + stockout_severity * cost_ratio * 2)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, weighted_demand + dynamic_buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3085.23628
# Description: A reinforcement learning-inspired approach that dynamically weights demand patterns and lost sales severity to compute safety stock, using a moving average of cost-normalized demand deviations and lost sales ratios to determine optimal order quantities.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Weighted demand smoothing (recent 3 periods vs all history)
    recent_weight = 0.6
    if len(history_demand) >= 3:
        recent_demand = sum(history_demand[-3:]) / 3
        historical_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
        smoothed_demand = recent_weight * recent_demand + (1 - recent_weight) * historical_demand
    else:
        smoothed_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    
    # Normalized lost sales ratio
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on cost balance
    cost_balance = (lost_sales_cost * lost_ratio) / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + min(2.0, cost_balance))  # Cap at 3x demand
    
    # Compute order amount
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    volatility_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-volatility_window:]

    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand * (1 + safety_factor) - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that combines quantile-based safety stock calculation with momentum-adjusted demand forecasting, dynamically weighted by cost-sensitive error correction.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_sensitivity = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    momentum_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    quantile_level = min(0.9, max(0.5, 1 - 1/(1 + cost_sensitivity)))
    
    recent_demand = history_demand[-momentum_window:]
    demand_momentum = sum(recent_demand) / len(recent_demand) if recent_demand else 0
    safety_stock = sorted(history_demand)[int(quantile_level * len(history_demand))] if history_demand else 0
    
    error_adjustment = 0
    if len(history_lost) > 1:
        recent_error = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / 2
        error_adjustment = recent_error * (cost_sensitivity / (1 + cost_sensitivity))
    
    forecast = demand_momentum + error_adjustment
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 150
    
    theta = 0.3
    kappa = 0.7
    phi = 0.5
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        base_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_momentum = 0
    else:
        base_demand = theta * history_demand[-1] + (1 - theta) * sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand[-1]

    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost * stockout_severity) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    dynamic_buffer = (1 + kappa * cost_ratio + phi * abs(demand_momentum)/max(1, base_demand)) * base_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + dynamic_buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: An adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock using exponential smoothing of demand volatility and non-linear cost-sensitive scaling.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(50, 2 * (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))
    
    decay = 0.3 + 0.5 * (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6))
    weighted_demand = sum(d * (decay ** (len(history_demand) - i - 1)) for i, d in enumerate(history_demand))
    total_weight = sum(decay ** i for i in range(len(history_demand)))
    avg_demand = weighted_demand / total_weight
    
    volatility = sum(abs(history_demand[i] - history_demand[i-1]) for i in range(1, len(history_demand))) / (len(history_demand) - 1) if len(history_demand) > 1 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = avg_demand * (1 + min(2, 0.5 * cost_ratio * (volatility / (avg_demand + 1e-6))))
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, min(3 * avg_demand, avg_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position))
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    quantile_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(len(sorted_demand) * quantile_weight), len(sorted_demand)-1)

    lead_time_adj = quantile_demand * max(0, cost_asymmetry)

    target = quantile_demand + lead_time_adj + lost_sales_adj
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target - current_inv)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    recent_lost = history_lost[-window:]
    recent_inv = history_inventory[-window:] if history_inventory else [0] * window

    safety_stock = (1 + adj_factor) * smoothed_demand + 0.3 * volatility
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3189.40968
# Description: A demand-adaptive algorithm with dynamic safety stock based on exponential smoothing of demand trends and cost-sensitive adjustment factors, incorporating lead time and real-time inventory performance.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.2
    beta = 0.4
    gamma = 0.6
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = 0
    else:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[-2:])/2 if len(history_demand) >= 2 else history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = (sum([abs(d - smoothed_demand) for d in history_demand[-3:]])/smoothed_demand) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and smoothed_demand > 0 else 0
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-2:])/sum(history_demand[-2:]) if len(history_demand) >= 2 and sum(history_demand[-2:]) > 0 else 0
    inventory_ratio = sum(history_inventory[-2:])/sum(history_demand[-2:]) if len(history_demand) >= 2 and sum(history_demand[-2:]) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_adjustment = gamma * (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio - holding_cost * inventory_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment + demand_volatility) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3217.26667
# Description: A novel inventory algorithm that uses quantile-based demand estimation with asymmetric cost penalties and lead-time-adjusted inventory positioning, dynamically adjusting ordering thresholds based on recent stockout severity.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation (75th percentile)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand) - 1)
    demand_estimate = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    # Asymmetric cost penalty factor
    penalty_ratio = (lost_sales_cost - holding_cost) / (lost_sales_cost + holding_cost)
    cost_factor = 1.0 + max(0, penalty_ratio)
    
    # Lead-time adjustment (since lead time = 1)
    lead_time_factor = 1.2
    
    # Recent stockout severity
    stockout_severity = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (3 * demand_estimate) if len(history_lost) >= 3 else 0
    
    # Dynamic threshold calculation
    threshold = demand_estimate * cost_factor * lead_time_factor * (1 + stockout_severity)
    
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, threshold - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3221.86433
# Description: 
A momentum-driven algorithm with asymmetric cost weighting, using weighted demand acceleration and inventory-lost sales imbalance to dynamically adjust safety stock multipliers, capped for stability.

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(100, sum(history_demand[:3])/3) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 100
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    momentum_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-momentum_window:]
    demand_momentum = (sum(recent_demand) - sum(history_demand[-2*momentum_window:-momentum_window])) / max(1, sum(history_demand[-2*momentum_window:-momentum_window]))
    
    lost_sales_imbalance = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / (sum(history_demand[-2:]) + 1e-6)
    inventory_imbalance = sum(history_inventory[-2:]) / (sum(history_demand[-2:]) + 1e-6)
    
    dynamic_weight = min(0.8, 0.3 * cost_ratio * (1 + lost_sales_imbalance - inventory_imbalance))
    base_demand = dynamic_weight * history_demand[-1] + (1 - dynamic_weight) * sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand[-1]
    
    safety_multiplier = min(2.0, 0.5 + 0.3 * cost_ratio + 0.2 * demand_momentum + 0.5 * lost_sales_imbalance)
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand * safety_multiplier - (history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0))
    return order_amount

    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / window
    recent_lost = sum(history_lost[-window:]) if len(history_lost) >= window else 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand * (1 + cost_ratio) + recent_lost * 0.8 - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: A momentum-based adaptive algorithm that combines exponential smoothing of demand with dynamic safety stock adjustment using weighted cost ratios and recent stockout trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(100, 2 * (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))
    
    alpha = 0.3 + 0.5 * (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6))
    smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * (sum(history_demand[-3:-1]) / 2 if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand[-1])
    
    stockout_trend = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / (sum(history_demand[-2:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 0
    cost_weight = min(3, lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.7
    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (0.5 + cost_weight * (1 + stockout_trend)) / 2
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, min(3 * smoothed_demand, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position))
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_4.json (item 24)
# Objective: 3331.88199
# Description: ```
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Exponential smoothing for demand forecast
    alpha = 0.3
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        forecast = history_demand[-1]
    else:
        forecast = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1]) / len(history_demand[:-1])
    
    # Demand volatility based on rolling standard deviation
    if len(history_demand) >= 3:
        recent_demand = history_demand[-3:]
        mean_demand = sum(recent_demand) / 3
        volatility = (sum((d - mean_demand)**2 for d in recent_demand) / 3)**0.5 / mean_demand if mean_demand > 0 else 0
    else:
        volatility = 0
    
    # Cost-adaptive risk factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    risk_factor = cost_ratio * (1 + volatility)
    
    # Dynamic adjustment based on recent lost sales performance
    if len(history_lost) >= 3:
        lost_performance = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / sum(history_demand[-3:]) if sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
        risk_factor *= (1 + lost_performance)
    
    # Order calculation
    safety_stock = risk_factor * forecast
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3380.11394
# Description: An inventory management algorithm that uses exponential smoothing of demand percentiles with momentum-based adjustment, where safety stock responds asymmetrically to increasing vs decreasing demand trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    p75 = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(0.75 * window), window-1)]
    
    momentum = 0
    if len(history_demand) >= 2:
        momentum = (history_demand[-1] - history_demand[-2]) / (history_demand[-2] + 1e-6)
    
    stockout_rate = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / (sum(history_demand[-2:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 0
    cost_factor = min(1.5, lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6))
    
    trend_adjusted = p75 * (1 + max(0, momentum) * 0.5 - min(0, momentum) * 0.2)
    safety_stock = trend_adjusted * (1 + stockout_rate * cost_factor * 0.4)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, trend_adjusted + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(7, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / window
    demand_std = (sum((x - avg_demand)**2 for x in recent_demand) / window)**0.5 if window > 1 else 0
    
    cost_normalized = lost_sales_cost / (lost_sales_cost + holding_cost + 1e-6)
    volatility_factor = demand_std / (avg_demand + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = avg_demand * cost_normalized * (1 + volatility_factor)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3412.54057
# Description: A novel demand-shape-aware algorithm that combines quantile-based demand estimation, non-linear cost balancing, and inventory trajectory optimization with lead-time compensation.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation
    window = min(7, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = sorted(history_demand[-window:])
    q1 = recent_demand[int(0.25 * window)]
    q3 = recent_demand[int(0.75 * window)]
    base_demand = (q1 + q3) / 2
    
    # Non-linear cost balancing
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost**0.7) / (holding_cost**0.3 + lost_sales_cost**0.7 + 1e-6)
    shape_factor = 1.2 * (q3 - q1) / (q3 + q1 + 1e-6)
    dynamic_multiplier = cost_ratio * (1 + shape_factor)
    
    # Inventory trajectory optimization
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    projected_need = base_demand * (1 + dynamic_multiplier)
    recent_lost = sum(history_lost[-2:]) if len(history_lost) >= 2 else 0
    compensation = min(projected_need * 0.5, recent_lost * 1.2) if recent_lost > 0 else 0
    
    order_amount = max(0, projected_need + compensation - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: A momentum-driven inventory algorithm that adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand trends and cost-sensitive safety stock, incorporating lead time awareness and lost sales momentum.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 50
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(1e-6, holding_cost)
    momentum_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    momentum = sum(history_demand[-momentum_window:]) / momentum_window
    
    lost_momentum = 0.4 * sum(history_lost[-3:]) / min(3, len(history_lost)) if history_lost else 0
    safety_factor = min(2.0, max(0.5, 0.7 * cost_ratio))
    
    base_demand = 0.6 * momentum + 0.4 * (sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand))
    target_level = base_demand * safety_factor + lost_momentum
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3430.17944
# Description: An adaptive safety stock algorithm using exponential smoothing of demand and lost sales, weighted by cost ratios and adjusted for recent volatility.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 100 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 100
    
    alpha = 0.3  # Smoothing factor for demand
    beta = 0.2   # Smoothing factor for lost sales
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
    smoothed_lost = history_lost[-1] if history_lost else 0
    
    for i in range(len(history_demand)-1):
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[i] + (1-alpha) * smoothed_demand
        if history_lost:
            smoothed_lost = beta * history_lost[i] + (1-beta) * smoothed_lost
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    volatility = max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    safety_factor = cost_ratio * (1 + 0.5 * volatility / (smoothed_demand + 1e-6)) * (1 + smoothed_lost / (smoothed_demand + 1e-6))
    target_inventory = smoothed_demand * (1 + safety_factor)
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

        return 50
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation (75th percentile)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)

    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0

    return order_amount

# Objective: 2609.50421
# Description: A demand-responsive algorithm that uses weighted demand volatility and cost-adaptive safety stock, dynamically adjusted by recent inventory performance and lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.4
    beta = 0.3
    gamma = 0.5
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = 0
    else:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/len(history_demand[:-1])
        demand_volatility = max(0, (max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:]))/smoothed_demand) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    inventory_ratio = 1 - (sum(history_inventory[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:])) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_adjustment = gamma * (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio + holding_cost * inventory_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment + demand_volatility) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    p60 = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(0.6 * window), window-1)]
    p80 = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(0.8 * window), window-1)]

    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = weighted_demand * (1 + stockout_trend * min(2.0, cost_ratio * 0.8))
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, weighted_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: "A quantile-regression-based inventory policy that dynamically adjusts safety stock levels using demand quantiles and cost-ratio-weighted lost sales feedback, with asymmetric response to inventory surpluses vs shortages."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    quantile_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-quantile_window:]
    high_quantile = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))]
    median_demand = sorted(recent_demand)[len(recent_demand) // 2]
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    lost_sales_adjustment = 1.5 * cost_ratio * sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    shortage_penalty = 2.0 if inventory_position < median_demand else 1.0
    
    safety_stock = high_quantile + shortage_penalty * lost_sales_adjustment * median_demand
    order_amount = max(0, median_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

        return 100
    
    smoothing_factor = 0.3
    avg_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else (1 - smoothing_factor) * sum(history_demand[:-1]) / (len(history_demand) - 1) + smoothing_factor * history_demand[-1]

    dynamic_buffer = avg_demand * (1 + lost_sales_ratio * cost_weight * 0.5)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand + dynamic_buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2717.62908
# Description: "A momentum-adjusted inventory policy that blends exponential smoothing of demand with cost-sensitive safety stock scaling, using weighted lost sales velocity to dynamically adjust reorder points."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Exponential smoothing parameters
    alpha = 0.3
    beta = 0.2
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
    
    # Lost sales momentum (3-period weighted average)
    lost_sales_window = history_lost[-3:] if len(history_lost) >= 3 else history_lost
    lost_sales_momentum = sum(lost_sales_window) / (len(lost_sales_window) + 1e-6)
    
    # Cost-sensitive scaling factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_factor = 1.0 + beta * cost_ratio * (1 + lost_sales_momentum / (smoothed_demand + 1e-6))
    
    # Current inventory position
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order calculation
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand * safety_factor - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 11)
# Objective: 2926.03
# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock using quantile forecasting and asymmetric cost weighting, with lead-time-aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(100, 2 * (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))  # Cost-aware default
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    quantile = min(0.95, 0.5 + 0.3 * (cost_ratio - 1))  # Asymmetric adjustment
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    idx = min(len(sorted_demand) - 1, int(quantile * len(sorted_demand)))
    base_demand = sorted_demand[idx]
    
    # Lead-time demand correction
    if len(history_demand) > 1:
        lead_time_demand = sum(history_demand[-2:]) / 2  # L=1 period
        base_demand = 0.7 * base_demand + 0.3 * lead_time_demand
    
    # Dynamic lost sales penalty factor
    lost_penalty = 1.0
    if len(history_lost) >= 2:
        recent_lost = sum(history_lost[-2:])
        if recent_lost > 0:
            lost_penalty += min(1.0, recent_lost / (base_demand + 1e-6))
    
    # Inventory position with lead time
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    pipeline = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) >= 1 else 0  # L=1 assumption
    inventory_position = current_inventory - pipeline
    
    # Asymmetric order calculation
    order_amount = max(0, (base_demand * lost_penalty) - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: An adaptive algorithm using exponential smoothing for demand forecasting with momentum-based safety stock adjustments and asymmetric cost-sensitive scaling.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 50
    
    # Exponential smoothing demand estimation (alpha=0.3)
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else 0.3 * history_demand[-1] + 0.7 * (sum(history_demand[:-1])/(len(history_demand)-1))
    
    # Momentum-based stockout adjustment
    momentum = min(max(sum(history_lost[-2:]) - sum(history_lost[-4:-2]), 0) / (sum(history_demand[-4:]) + 1e-6), 0.4) if len(history_demand) >= 4 else 0
    
    # Asymmetric cost scaling
    cost_factor = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.5 if lost_sales_cost > holding_cost else (holding_cost / (lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.3
    
    # Dynamic safety stock
    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + momentum * cost_factor * 0.4)
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 13)
# Objective: 3065.52775
# Description: A demand-responsive inventory policy that uses weighted moving average demand forecasting with dynamic safety stock adjustment based on cost ratios and recent stockout severity.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    weights = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2][:window]
    weighted_demand = sum(d * w for d, w in zip(history_demand[-window:], weights)) / sum(weights)
    
    stockout_severity = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(history_demand[-window:]) + 1e-6)
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    dynamic_buffer = weighted_demand * (1 + stockout_severity * cost_ratio * 2)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, weighted_demand + dynamic_buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 14)
# Objective: 3085.23628
# Description: A reinforcement learning-inspired approach that dynamically weights demand patterns and lost sales severity to compute safety stock, using a moving average of cost-normalized demand deviations and lost sales ratios to determine optimal order quantities.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Weighted demand smoothing (recent 3 periods vs all history)
    recent_weight = 0.6
    if len(history_demand) >= 3:
        recent_demand = sum(history_demand[-3:]) / 3
        historical_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
        smoothed_demand = recent_weight * recent_demand + (1 - recent_weight) * historical_demand
    else:
        smoothed_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    
    # Normalized lost sales ratio
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on cost balance
    cost_balance = (lost_sales_cost * lost_ratio) / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + min(2.0, cost_balance))  # Cap at 3x demand
    
    # Compute order amount
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 15)
# Objective: 3114.64736
# Description: A volatility-adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on demand variability, cost-sensitive safety stock, and lead-time-aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    volatility_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-volatility_window:]
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / volatility_window
    demand_std = (sum((x - avg_demand)**2 for x in recent_demand) / volatility_window)**0.5 if volatility_window > 1 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_factor = 1.5 * cost_ratio * (1 + demand_std / (avg_demand + 1e-6))
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand * (1 + safety_factor) - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 16)
# Objective: 3140.96167
# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that combines quantile-based safety stock calculation with momentum-adjusted demand forecasting, dynamically weighted by cost-sensitive error correction.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_sensitivity = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    momentum_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    quantile_level = min(0.9, max(0.5, 1 - 1/(1 + cost_sensitivity)))
    
    recent_demand = history_demand[-momentum_window:]
    demand_momentum = sum(recent_demand) / len(recent_demand) if recent_demand else 0
    safety_stock = sorted(history_demand)[int(quantile_level * len(history_demand))] if history_demand else 0
    
    error_adjustment = 0
    if len(history_lost) > 1:
        recent_error = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / 2
        error_adjustment = recent_error * (cost_sensitivity / (1 + cost_sensitivity))
    
    forecast = demand_momentum + error_adjustment
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 17)
# Objective: 3155.93492
# Description: A momentum-driven inventory algorithm that adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand acceleration, cost-adaptive safety buffers, and recent stockout severity.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 150
    
    theta = 0.3
    kappa = 0.7
    phi = 0.5
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        base_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_momentum = 0
    else:
        base_demand = theta * history_demand[-1] + (1 - theta) * sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand[-1]
        demand_momentum = (history_demand[-1] - history_demand[-2]) if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 0
    
    stockout_severity = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost * stockout_severity) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    dynamic_buffer = (1 + kappa * cost_ratio + phi * abs(demand_momentum)/max(1, base_demand)) * base_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + dynamic_buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 18)
# Objective: 3167.06625
# Description: An adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock using exponential smoothing of demand volatility and non-linear cost-sensitive scaling.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(50, 2 * (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))
    
    decay = 0.3 + 0.5 * (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6))
    weighted_demand = sum(d * (decay ** (len(history_demand) - i - 1)) for i, d in enumerate(history_demand))
    total_weight = sum(decay ** i for i in range(len(history_demand)))
    avg_demand = weighted_demand / total_weight
    
    volatility = sum(abs(history_demand[i] - history_demand[i-1]) for i in range(1, len(history_demand))) / (len(history_demand) - 1) if len(history_demand) > 1 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = avg_demand * (1 + min(2, 0.5 * cost_ratio * (volatility / (avg_demand + 1e-6))))
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, min(3 * avg_demand, avg_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position))
    return order_amount
