# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 19)
# Objective: 3170.36458
# Description: The new algorithm implements a Demand-Quantile Cost-Balancing Policy that uses quantile-based demand estimation with asymmetric cost penalties and lead-time aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    quantile_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(len(sorted_demand) * quantile_weight), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    quantile_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    cost_asymmetry = (lost_sales_cost - holding_cost) / max(lost_sales_cost, holding_cost)
    lead_time_adj = quantile_demand * max(0, cost_asymmetry)
    
    lost_sales_adj = sum(history_lost[-2:]) * 0.3 if history_lost else 0
    target = quantile_demand + lead_time_adj + lost_sales_adj
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 20)
# Objective: 3178.9759
# Description:  A new algorithm that uses moving average demand estimation with cost-weighted exponential smoothing of stockout events and capped buffer adjustments based on normalized holding-to-lost-sales cost ratio. 
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 50
    
    window_size = min(3, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window_size:]
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / window_size
    
    stockout_norm = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / (avg_demand * 2 + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 0
    cost_weight = min(lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6), 3)
    buffer = avg_demand * (1 + min(stockout_norm * cost_weight * 0.2, 0.5))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand + buffer - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 21)
# Objective: 3181.82848
# Description: Exponential smoothing of demand combined with cost-sensitive safety stock adjustment, dynamically weighted by recent lost sales and inventory performance.}  

{An adaptive inventory policy that uses demand clustering, trend-adjusted smoothing, and a dual-threshold safety stock mechanism based on cost ratios and recent performance deviations.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    recent_lost = history_lost[-window:]
    recent_inv = history_inventory[-window:] if history_inventory else [0] * window
    
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / window
    demand_trend = (recent_demand[-1] - recent_demand[0]) / window if window > 1 else 0
    smoothed_demand = avg_demand + 0.5 * demand_trend
    
    lost_ratio = sum(recent_lost) / sum(recent_demand) if sum(recent_demand) > 0 else 0
    inv_ratio = sum(recent_inv) / sum(recent_demand) if sum(recent_demand) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    adj_factor = (cost_ratio * lost_ratio) + ((1 - cost_ratio) * (1 - inv_ratio))
    
    volatility = max(recent_demand) - min(recent_demand) if window > 1 else 0
    safety_stock = (1 + adj_factor) * smoothed_demand + 0.3 * volatility
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 22)
# Objective: 3189.40968
# Description: A demand-adaptive algorithm with dynamic safety stock based on exponential smoothing of demand trends and cost-sensitive adjustment factors, incorporating lead time and real-time inventory performance.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.2
    beta = 0.4
    gamma = 0.6
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = 0
    else:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[-2:])/2 if len(history_demand) >= 2 else history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = (sum([abs(d - smoothed_demand) for d in history_demand[-3:]])/smoothed_demand) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and smoothed_demand > 0 else 0
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-2:])/sum(history_demand[-2:]) if len(history_demand) >= 2 and sum(history_demand[-2:]) > 0 else 0
    inventory_ratio = sum(history_inventory[-2:])/sum(history_demand[-2:]) if len(history_demand) >= 2 and sum(history_demand[-2:]) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_adjustment = gamma * (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio - holding_cost * inventory_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment + demand_volatility) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 23)
# Objective: 3202.82401
# Description: An adaptive quantile-based approach that dynamically adjusts safety stock using cost-weighted demand percentiles and exponential smoothing of lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Exponential smoothing for demand (75th percentile)
    window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = sorted(history_demand[-window:])
    quantile_idx = int(0.75 * (window - 1))
    smoothed_demand = recent_demand[quantile_idx] if window >= 3 else sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    
    # Lost sales trend (exponential smoothing)
    lost_alpha = 0.3
    if len(history_lost) >= 2:
        lost_trend = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / 2 * lost_alpha + (1 - lost_alpha) * (sum(history_lost) / len(history_lost))
    else:
        lost_trend = sum(history_lost) / max(1, len(history_lost))
    
    # Cost-sensitive safety factor
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost * lost_trend) / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_factor = 0.5 + min(1.5, cost_ratio * 0.8)
    
    # Order calculation
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, (smoothed_demand * safety_factor) - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 24)
# Objective: 3203.72959
# Description: A probabilistic demand-shaping algorithm that combines quantile forecasting with asymmetric cost-sensitive adjustments, using Bayesian updates on demand patterns and lost sales impact.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 50 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 50
    
    cost_asymmetry = lost_sales_cost / max(1e-6, holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    full_avg = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    recent_avg = sum(history_demand[-3:]) / 3 if len(history_demand) >= 3 else full_avg
    
    demand_quantile = sorted(history_demand)[int(len(history_demand) * cost_asymmetry)]
    lost_boost = 1.5 * sum(history_lost[-2:]) / 2 if len(history_lost) >= 2 else 0
    
    base_target = max(full_avg, recent_avg, demand_quantile)
    adjusted_target = base_target * (1 + cost_asymmetry) + lost_boost
    
    order_amount = max(0, min(adjusted_target * 1.5, adjusted_target - history_inventory[-1]))
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 25)
# Objective: 3209.54765
# Description: A demand-driven algorithm using quantile-based forecasting with dynamic cost-sensitive buffering, focusing on recent stockout patterns and limiting cost ratio impact through constrained exponential smoothing.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation (75th percentile)
    demand_window = history_demand[-4:] if len(history_demand) >= 4 else history_demand
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.75 * len(demand_window)), len(demand_window)-1)
    forecast = sorted(demand_window)[quantile_idx]
    
    # Constrained cost ratio impact
    cost_ratio = min(lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6), 2.0)
    
    # Recent stockout focus (last 2 periods)
    recent_stockout = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / (sum(history_demand[-2:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 0
    
    # Dynamic buffer calculation
    buffer_factor = 0.5 + 0.3 * cost_ratio + 0.4 * recent_stockout
    buffer = forecast * buffer_factor
    
    # Current inventory position
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order calculation
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + buffer - current_inventory)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(0, 100 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 100
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(1e-6, holding_cost)
    alpha = 0.2
    forecast = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * (sum(history_demand[:-1])/len(history_demand[:-1]) if len(history_demand) > 1 else history_demand[-1])
    
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-2:])/sum(history_demand[-2:]) if len(history_demand) >= 2 and sum(history_demand[-2:]) > 0 else 0
    safety_factor = min(2.0, 1.0 + 0.5 * cost_ratio * (1 + lost_ratio))
    
    target = forecast * safety_factor
    order_amount = max(0, target - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 27)
# Objective: 3217.26667
# Description: A novel inventory algorithm that uses quantile-based demand estimation with asymmetric cost penalties and lead-time-adjusted inventory positioning, dynamically adjusting ordering thresholds based on recent stockout severity.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation (75th percentile)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand) - 1)
    demand_estimate = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    # Asymmetric cost penalty factor
    penalty_ratio = (lost_sales_cost - holding_cost) / (lost_sales_cost + holding_cost)
    cost_factor = 1.0 + max(0, penalty_ratio)
    
    # Lead-time adjustment (since lead time = 1)
    lead_time_factor = 1.2
    
    # Recent stockout severity
    stockout_severity = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (3 * demand_estimate) if len(history_lost) >= 3 else 0
    
    # Dynamic threshold calculation
    threshold = demand_estimate * cost_factor * lead_time_factor * (1 + stockout_severity)
    
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, threshold - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 28)
# Objective: 3221.86433
# Description: 
A momentum-driven algorithm with asymmetric cost weighting, using weighted demand acceleration and inventory-lost sales imbalance to dynamically adjust safety stock multipliers, capped for stability.

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(100, sum(history_demand[:3])/3) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 100
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    momentum_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-momentum_window:]
    demand_momentum = (sum(recent_demand) - sum(history_demand[-2*momentum_window:-momentum_window])) / max(1, sum(history_demand[-2*momentum_window:-momentum_window]))
    
    lost_sales_imbalance = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / (sum(history_demand[-2:]) + 1e-6)
    inventory_imbalance = sum(history_inventory[-2:]) / (sum(history_demand[-2:]) + 1e-6)
    
    dynamic_weight = min(0.8, 0.3 * cost_ratio * (1 + lost_sales_imbalance - inventory_imbalance))
    base_demand = dynamic_weight * history_demand[-1] + (1 - dynamic_weight) * sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand[-1]
    
    safety_multiplier = min(2.0, 0.5 + 0.3 * cost_ratio + 0.2 * demand_momentum + 0.5 * lost_sales_imbalance)
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand * safety_multiplier - (history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0))
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_5.json (item 29)
# Objective: 3224.10146
# Description: A demand-responsive algorithm that uses weighted moving averages with volatility-adjusted safety stock, dynamically scaled by cost ratios and recent demand variability.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(4, len(history_demand))
    weights = [0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1][:window]
    weighted_avg = sum(w * d for w, d in zip(weights, history_demand[-window:]))
    
    demand_std = (sum((d - weighted_avg)**2 for d in history_demand[-window:]) / window)**0.5 if window > 1 else 0
    volatility_factor = min(2.0, 1 + demand_std / (weighted_avg + 1e-6))
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    recent_lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / (sum(history_demand[-2:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 0
    
    safety_stock = weighted_avg * volatility_factor * (1 + recent_lost_ratio * cost_ratio * 0.3)
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, weighted_avg + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: A new algorithm that combines quantile-based demand forecasting with dynamic safety stock adjustments using weighted historical stockout rates and a non-linear cost-sensitive scaling factor.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 50
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation (75th percentile)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    base_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    # Dynamic stockout weighting
    stockout_rate = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.7  # Non-linear scaling
    
    # Safety stock calculation
    safety_stock = base_demand * (1 + min(stockout_rate * cost_ratio * 0.3, 0.6))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Description: A demand-responsive algorithm that uses weighted demand volatility and cost-adaptive safety stock, dynamically adjusted by recent inventory performance and lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.4
    beta = 0.3
    gamma = 0.5
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = 0
    else:

    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    inventory_ratio = 1 - (sum(history_inventory[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:])) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_adjustment = gamma * (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio + holding_cost * inventory_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)

    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_6.json (item 2)
# Objective: 2634.97424
# Description: An inventory algorithm using weighted demand percentiles and cost-adaptive safety stock, with asymmetric response to stockout trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    p60 = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(0.6 * window), window-1)]
    p80 = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(0.8 * window), window-1)]
    weighted_demand = 0.7 * p60 + 0.3 * p80
    
    stockout_trend = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = weighted_demand * (1 + stockout_trend * min(2.0, cost_ratio * 0.8))
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, weighted_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2659.19435
# Description: "A quantile-regression-based inventory policy that dynamically adjusts safety stock levels using demand quantiles and cost-ratio-weighted lost sales feedback, with asymmetric response to inventory surpluses vs shortages."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    quantile_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-quantile_window:]
    high_quantile = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))]
    median_demand = sorted(recent_demand)[len(recent_demand) // 2]
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    lost_sales_adjustment = 1.5 * cost_ratio * sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    shortage_penalty = 2.0 if inventory_position < median_demand else 1.0
    
    safety_stock = high_quantile + shortage_penalty * lost_sales_adjustment * median_demand
    order_amount = max(0, median_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2666.61376
# Description: A momentum-based inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities using exponential smoothing of demand trends, weighted by a cost-sensitive shortage prevention factor and lead-time aware buffer stock.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(50, sum(history_demand[:3]) / 3) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 50
    
    alpha = 0.3
    window = min(4, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    smoothed_demand = alpha * sum(recent_demand) / window + (1 - alpha) * (sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand) if history_demand else 0)
    
    shortage_ratio = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(recent_demand) + 1e-6)
    cost_weight = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.5
    buffer_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + shortage_ratio * cost_weight)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + buffer_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    smoothing_factor = 0.3
    avg_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else (1 - smoothing_factor) * sum(history_demand[:-1]) / (len(history_demand) - 1) + smoothing_factor * history_demand[-1]

    cost_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    dynamic_buffer = avg_demand * (1 + lost_sales_ratio * cost_weight * 0.5)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand + dynamic_buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: A novel algorithm combining demand volatility-adjusted quantile forecasting with exponential cost-sensitive safety stock optimization, using recent stockout patterns to dynamically scale order quantities while maintaining lead-time awareness.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(50, sum(history_demand[:2])/2) if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 50
    
    # Volatility-adjusted quantile selection (60th percentile + 0.2*std_dev)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.6 * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    base_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx] + 0.2 * (max(history_demand) - min(history_demand))
    
    # Exponential cost scaling with recent stockout focus
    recent_stockout = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / (sum(history_demand[-2:]) + 1e-6)
    cost_factor = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.5
    
    # Dynamic safety stock with volatility dampening
    safety_stock = base_demand * min(0.8, 0.4 * cost_factor * (1 + recent_stockout))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_6.json (item 7)
# Objective: 2717.62908
# Description: "A momentum-adjusted inventory policy that blends exponential smoothing of demand with cost-sensitive safety stock scaling, using weighted lost sales velocity to dynamically adjust reorder points."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Exponential smoothing parameters
    alpha = 0.3
    beta = 0.2
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
    
    # Lost sales momentum (3-period weighted average)
    lost_sales_window = history_lost[-3:] if len(history_lost) >= 3 else history_lost
    lost_sales_momentum = sum(lost_sales_window) / (len(lost_sales_window) + 1e-6)
    
    # Cost-sensitive scaling factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_factor = 1.0 + beta * cost_ratio * (1 + lost_sales_momentum / (smoothed_demand + 1e-6))
    
    # Current inventory position
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order calculation
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand * safety_factor - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

# Description: A demand-percentile based adaptive algorithm with dynamic safety stock scaling using cost-weighted shortage penalties and inventory-dependent adjustment factors.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(4, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    recent_lost = history_lost[-window:] if history_lost else [0]*window
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    demand_q75 = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75*(window-1))] if window > 1 else recent_demand[0]
    lost_ratio = sum(recent_lost) / (sum(recent_demand) + 1e-6)
    
    cost_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    inv_penalty = 1.5 / (1 + current_inv) if current_inv > 0 else 2.0
    
    safety_factor = (1 + cost_weight * lost_ratio * inv_penalty)
    safety_stock = safety_factor * demand_q75
    
    order_amount = max(0, demand_q75 + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Description: A momentum-based demand forecasting algorithm that dynamically adjusts both the estimation window size and quantile threshold based on recent demand volatility, while using exponential smoothing of cost ratios for buffer calculation.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    volatility = (max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:])) / (sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    window = min(5 + int(volatility * 5), len(history_demand))
    quantile_threshold = 0.5 + min(0.3, volatility * 0.5)
    
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    demand_estimate = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(quantile_threshold * (window - 1)), window - 1)]
    
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(history_demand[-window:]) + 1e-6)
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.6
    buffer = demand_estimate * (1 + lost_ratio * cost_ratio)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_6.json (item 18)
# Objective: 2926.03
# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock using quantile forecasting and asymmetric cost weighting, with lead-time-aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(100, 2 * (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))  # Cost-aware default
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    quantile = min(0.95, 0.5 + 0.3 * (cost_ratio - 1))  # Asymmetric adjustment
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    idx = min(len(sorted_demand) - 1, int(quantile * len(sorted_demand)))
    base_demand = sorted_demand[idx]
    
    # Lead-time demand correction
    if len(history_demand) > 1:
        lead_time_demand = sum(history_demand[-2:]) / 2  # L=1 period
        base_demand = 0.7 * base_demand + 0.3 * lead_time_demand
    
    # Dynamic lost sales penalty factor
    lost_penalty = 1.0
    if len(history_lost) >= 2:
        recent_lost = sum(history_lost[-2:])
        if recent_lost > 0:
            lost_penalty += min(1.0, recent_lost / (base_demand + 1e-6))
    
    # Inventory position with lead time
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    pipeline = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) >= 1 else 0  # L=1 assumption
    inventory_position = current_inventory - pipeline
    
    # Asymmetric order calculation
    order_amount = max(0, (base_demand * lost_penalty) - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2980.48346
# Description: An adaptive algorithm using exponential smoothing for demand forecasting with momentum-based safety stock adjustments and asymmetric cost-sensitive scaling.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 50
    
    # Exponential smoothing demand estimation (alpha=0.3)
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else 0.3 * history_demand[-1] + 0.7 * (sum(history_demand[:-1])/(len(history_demand)-1))
    
    # Momentum-based stockout adjustment
    momentum = min(max(sum(history_lost[-2:]) - sum(history_lost[-4:-2]), 0) / (sum(history_demand[-4:]) + 1e-6), 0.4) if len(history_demand) >= 4 else 0
    
    # Asymmetric cost scaling
    cost_factor = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.5 if lost_sales_cost > holding_cost else (holding_cost / (lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.3
    
    # Dynamic safety stock
    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + momentum * cost_factor * 0.4)
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_6.json (item 22)
# Objective: 3014.56189
# Description: Adaptive demand estimation combined with cost-sensitive safety stock adjustments, using recent performance metrics to dynamically scale orders while accounting for lead time.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 50
    
    # Robust demand estimation using weighted median
    weights = [0.5**i for i in range(len(history_demand))]
    weighted_demand = sorted(zip(weights, history_demand), key=lambda x: x[1])
    cum_weight = 0
    median_demand = 0
    total_weight = sum(weights)
    for w, d in weighted_demand:
        cum_weight += w
        if cum_weight >= 0.5 * total_weight:
            median_demand = d
            break
    
    # Cost-adaptive scaling with recent stockout focus
    recent_stockout_ratio = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / (sum(history_demand[-2:]) + 1e-6)
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.75
    
    # Dynamic buffer with volatility dampening
    volatility = min(1.0, (max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:])) / (median_demand + 1e-6)) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    buffer = median_demand * min(1.2, 0.3 * cost_ratio * (1 + recent_stockout_ratio + 0.5 * volatility))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, median_demand + buffer - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_6.json (item 23)
# Objective: 3065.52775
# Description: A demand-responsive inventory policy that uses weighted moving average demand forecasting with dynamic safety stock adjustment based on cost ratios and recent stockout severity.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    weights = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2][:window]
    weighted_demand = sum(d * w for d, w in zip(history_demand[-window:], weights)) / sum(weights)
    
    stockout_severity = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(history_demand[-window:]) + 1e-6)
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    dynamic_buffer = weighted_demand * (1 + stockout_severity * cost_ratio * 2)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, weighted_demand + dynamic_buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_6.json (item 24)
# Objective: 3085.23628
# Description: A reinforcement learning-inspired approach that dynamically weights demand patterns and lost sales severity to compute safety stock, using a moving average of cost-normalized demand deviations and lost sales ratios to determine optimal order quantities.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Weighted demand smoothing (recent 3 periods vs all history)
    recent_weight = 0.6
    if len(history_demand) >= 3:
        recent_demand = sum(history_demand[-3:]) / 3
        historical_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
        smoothed_demand = recent_weight * recent_demand + (1 - recent_weight) * historical_demand
    else:
        smoothed_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    
    # Normalized lost sales ratio
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on cost balance
    cost_balance = (lost_sales_cost * lost_ratio) / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + min(2.0, cost_balance))  # Cap at 3x demand
    
    # Compute order amount
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3114.64736
# Description: A volatility-adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on demand variability, cost-sensitive safety stock, and lead-time-aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    volatility_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-volatility_window:]
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / volatility_window
    demand_std = (sum((x - avg_demand)**2 for x in recent_demand) / volatility_window)**0.5 if volatility_window > 1 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_factor = 1.5 * cost_ratio * (1 + demand_std / (avg_demand + 1e-6))
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand * (1 + safety_factor) - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_6.json (item 26)
# Objective: 3140.96167
# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that combines quantile-based safety stock calculation with momentum-adjusted demand forecasting, dynamically weighted by cost-sensitive error correction.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_sensitivity = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    momentum_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    quantile_level = min(0.9, max(0.5, 1 - 1/(1 + cost_sensitivity)))
    
    recent_demand = history_demand[-momentum_window:]
    demand_momentum = sum(recent_demand) / len(recent_demand) if recent_demand else 0
    safety_stock = sorted(history_demand)[int(quantile_level * len(history_demand))] if history_demand else 0
    
    error_adjustment = 0
    if len(history_lost) > 1:
        recent_error = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / 2
        error_adjustment = recent_error * (cost_sensitivity / (1 + cost_sensitivity))
    
    forecast = demand_momentum + error_adjustment
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3155.93492
# Description: A momentum-driven inventory algorithm that adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand acceleration, cost-adaptive safety buffers, and recent stockout severity.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 150
    
    theta = 0.3
    kappa = 0.7
    phi = 0.5
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        base_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_momentum = 0
    else:
        base_demand = theta * history_demand[-1] + (1 - theta) * sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand[-1]
        demand_momentum = (history_demand[-1] - history_demand[-2]) if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 0
    
    stockout_severity = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost * stockout_severity) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    dynamic_buffer = (1 + kappa * cost_ratio + phi * abs(demand_momentum)/max(1, base_demand)) * base_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + dynamic_buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3167.06625
# Description: An adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock using exponential smoothing of demand volatility and non-linear cost-sensitive scaling.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(50, 2 * (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))
    
    decay = 0.3 + 0.5 * (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6))
    weighted_demand = sum(d * (decay ** (len(history_demand) - i - 1)) for i, d in enumerate(history_demand))
    total_weight = sum(decay ** i for i in range(len(history_demand)))
    avg_demand = weighted_demand / total_weight
    
    volatility = sum(abs(history_demand[i] - history_demand[i-1]) for i in range(1, len(history_demand))) / (len(history_demand) - 1) if len(history_demand) > 1 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = avg_demand * (1 + min(2, 0.5 * cost_ratio * (volatility / (avg_demand + 1e-6))))
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, min(3 * avg_demand, avg_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position))
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3170.36458
# Description: The new algorithm implements a Demand-Quantile Cost-Balancing Policy that uses quantile-based demand estimation with asymmetric cost penalties and lead-time aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    quantile_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(len(sorted_demand) * quantile_weight), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    quantile_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    cost_asymmetry = (lost_sales_cost - holding_cost) / max(lost_sales_cost, holding_cost)
    lead_time_adj = quantile_demand * max(0, cost_asymmetry)
    
    lost_sales_adj = sum(history_lost[-2:]) * 0.3 if history_lost else 0
    target = quantile_demand + lead_time_adj + lost_sales_adj
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2609.50421
# Description: A demand-responsive algorithm that uses weighted demand volatility and cost-adaptive safety stock, dynamically adjusted by recent inventory performance and lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.4
    beta = 0.3
    gamma = 0.5
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = 0
    else:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/len(history_demand[:-1])
        demand_volatility = max(0, (max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:]))/smoothed_demand) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    inventory_ratio = 1 - (sum(history_inventory[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:])) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_adjustment = gamma * (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio + holding_cost * inventory_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment + demand_volatility) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(30, sum(history_demand[:3])/3) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 30
    
    # Momentum-weighted demand estimation (75th percentile + 0.1*recent trend)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    recent_trend = sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 - sum(history_demand[-6:-3])/3 if len(history_demand) >=6 else 0
    base_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx] + 0.1 * recent_trend
    
    # Momentum-adjusted cost scaling
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)) ** 0.7
    recent_lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6)

    safety_stock = base_demand * min(1.0, 0.3 * cost_ratio * (1 + 2*recent_lost_ratio))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2654.92663
# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that uses quantile-based demand estimation with non-linear cost scaling, dynamically adjusting safety stock based on exponential moving averages of stockout severity and cost ratios.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation (75th percentile)
    demand_quantile = sorted(history_demand)[int(0.75 * len(history_demand))] if len(history_demand) > 1 else history_demand[0]
    
    # Exponential moving average of lost sales ratio (alpha=0.3)
    ema_lost_ratio = 0.0
    if len(history_lost) >= 1:
        alpha = 0.3
        for i in range(len(history_lost)):
            ema_lost_ratio = alpha * (history_lost[i] / (history_demand[i] + 1e-6)) + (1 - alpha) * ema_lost_ratio
    
    # Non-linear cost scaling factor
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost * ema_lost_ratio) / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    scaling_factor = min(2.5, 1 + (cost_ratio ** 0.7))
    
    # Dynamic safety stock
    safety_stock = demand_quantile * scaling_factor
    
    # Current inventory position
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_quantile + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Description: "A quantile-regression-based inventory policy that dynamically adjusts safety stock levels using demand quantiles and cost-ratio-weighted lost sales feedback, with asymmetric response to inventory surpluses vs shortages."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    quantile_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-quantile_window:]
    high_quantile = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))]
    median_demand = sorted(recent_demand)[len(recent_demand) // 2]
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    lost_sales_adjustment = 1.5 * cost_ratio * sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0

    shortage_penalty = 2.0 if inventory_position < median_demand else 1.0
    
    safety_stock = high_quantile + shortage_penalty * lost_sales_adjustment * median_demand
    order_amount = max(0, median_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_7.json (item 13)
# Objective: 2717.62908
# Description: "A momentum-adjusted inventory policy that blends exponential smoothing of demand with cost-sensitive safety stock scaling, using weighted lost sales velocity to dynamically adjust reorder points."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Exponential smoothing parameters
    alpha = 0.3
    beta = 0.2
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
    
    # Lost sales momentum (3-period weighted average)
    lost_sales_window = history_lost[-3:] if len(history_lost) >= 3 else history_lost
    lost_sales_momentum = sum(lost_sales_window) / (len(lost_sales_window) + 1e-6)
    
    # Cost-sensitive scaling factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_factor = 1.0 + beta * cost_ratio * (1 + lost_sales_momentum / (smoothed_demand + 1e-6))
    
    # Current inventory position
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order calculation
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand * safety_factor - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, blended_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    quantile = min(0.8, max(0.7, lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)))
    demand_estimate = sorted(recent_demand)[int(quantile * (len(recent_demand) - 1))]

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_7.json (item 17)
# Objective: 2804.63261
# Description: A demand-shaping inventory policy that combines exponential smoothing of demand with cost-sensitive safety stock adjustment, dynamically weighted by recent lost sales patterns.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.3
    beta = 0.2
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/len(history_demand[:-1])
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    cost_adjustment = (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2812.65437
# Description: An adaptive quantile-based algorithm that blends short-term demand quantiles with cost-sensitive safety stock adjustments, using exponential smoothing for trend adaptation.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(50, sum(history_demand[:3])/3) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 100
    
    window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    quantile = sorted(history_demand[-window:])[int(window*0.75)]
    trend = 0.5 * quantile + 0.5 * (sum(history_demand[-window:]) / window)
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    lost_severity = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(history_demand[-window:]) + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = trend * (1 + 0.2 * cost_ratio * lost_severity)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, trend + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2838.90771
# Description: A demand-percentile based adaptive algorithm with dynamic safety stock scaling using cost-weighted shortage penalties and inventory-dependent adjustment factors.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(4, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    recent_lost = history_lost[-window:] if history_lost else [0]*window
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    demand_q75 = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75*(window-1))] if window > 1 else recent_demand[0]
    lost_ratio = sum(recent_lost) / (sum(recent_demand) + 1e-6)
    
    cost_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    inv_penalty = 1.5 / (1 + current_inv) if current_inv > 0 else 2.0
    
    safety_factor = (1 + cost_weight * lost_ratio * inv_penalty)
    safety_stock = safety_factor * demand_q75
    
    order_amount = max(0, demand_q75 + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2849.1213
# Description: A momentum-based demand forecasting algorithm that dynamically adjusts both the estimation window size and quantile threshold based on recent demand volatility, while using exponential smoothing of cost ratios for buffer calculation.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    volatility = (max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:])) / (sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    window = min(5 + int(volatility * 5), len(history_demand))
    quantile_threshold = 0.5 + min(0.3, volatility * 0.5)
    
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    demand_estimate = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(quantile_threshold * (window - 1)), window - 1)]
    
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(history_demand[-window:]) + 1e-6)
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.6
    buffer = demand_estimate * (1 + lost_ratio * cost_ratio)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_7.json (item 27)
# Objective: 2926.03
# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock using quantile forecasting and asymmetric cost weighting, with lead-time-aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(100, 2 * (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))  # Cost-aware default
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    quantile = min(0.95, 0.5 + 0.3 * (cost_ratio - 1))  # Asymmetric adjustment
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    idx = min(len(sorted_demand) - 1, int(quantile * len(sorted_demand)))
    base_demand = sorted_demand[idx]
    
    # Lead-time demand correction
    if len(history_demand) > 1:
        lead_time_demand = sum(history_demand[-2:]) / 2  # L=1 period
        base_demand = 0.7 * base_demand + 0.3 * lead_time_demand
    
    # Dynamic lost sales penalty factor
    lost_penalty = 1.0
    if len(history_lost) >= 2:
        recent_lost = sum(history_lost[-2:])
        if recent_lost > 0:
            lost_penalty += min(1.0, recent_lost / (base_demand + 1e-6))
    
    # Inventory position with lead time
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    pipeline = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) >= 1 else 0  # L=1 assumption
    inventory_position = current_inventory - pipeline
    
    # Asymmetric order calculation
    order_amount = max(0, (base_demand * lost_penalty) - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2971.74586
# Description: An asymmetric cost-weighted algorithm that uses demand clustering for baseline estimation, applies non-linear safety stock adjustments based on cost imbalance, and makes inventory corrections proportional to recent errors.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100 if len(history_demand) < 3 else max(50, sum(history_demand[:3])/3)
    
    window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    cluster_size = min(3, window)
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    clusters = [sorted(recent_demand[i:i+cluster_size]) for i in range(0, window-cluster_size+1)]
    base_demand = sum([c[cluster_size//2] for c in clusters]) / len(clusters)
    
    cost_imbalance = (lost_sales_cost - holding_cost) / (lost_sales_cost + holding_cost + 1e-6)
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(recent_demand) + 1e-6)
    error_correction = 0.5 * (history_lost[-1] if history_lost else 0)
    
    safety_stock = base_demand * (1 + 0.5 * abs(cost_imbalance) + 0.3 * lost_ratio)
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position + error_correction)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

        return 100
    
    window = min(4, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    p75 = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(0.75 * window), window-1)]
    p90 = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(0.9 * window), window-1)]

    cost_factor = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.5

    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, weighted_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_8.json (item 4)
# Objective: 2609.50421
# Description: A demand-responsive algorithm that uses weighted demand volatility and cost-adaptive safety stock, dynamically adjusted by recent inventory performance and lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.4
    beta = 0.3
    gamma = 0.5
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = 0
    else:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/len(history_demand[:-1])
        demand_volatility = max(0, (max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:]))/smoothed_demand) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    inventory_ratio = 1 - (sum(history_inventory[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:])) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_adjustment = gamma * (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio + holding_cost * inventory_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment + demand_volatility) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2623.18437
# Description: A demand-shape-aware algorithm using weighted historical percentiles with momentum-based safety stock adjustment, where order quantities adapt to both cost ratios and recent demand acceleration patterns.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(30, sum(history_demand[:3])/3) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 30
    
    # Momentum-weighted demand estimation (75th percentile + 0.1*recent trend)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    recent_trend = sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 - sum(history_demand[-6:-3])/3 if len(history_demand) >=6 else 0
    base_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx] + 0.1 * recent_trend
    
    # Momentum-adjusted cost scaling
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)) ** 0.7
    recent_lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6)
    
    # Dynamic safety stock with momentum dampening
    safety_stock = base_demand * min(1.0, 0.3 * cost_ratio * (1 + 2*recent_lost_ratio))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Description: An inventory algorithm using weighted demand percentiles and cost-adaptive safety stock, with asymmetric response to stockout trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    p60 = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(0.6 * window), window-1)]
    p80 = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(0.8 * window), window-1)]
    weighted_demand = 0.7 * p60 + 0.3 * p80
    
    stockout_trend = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = weighted_demand * (1 + stockout_trend * min(2.0, cost_ratio * 0.8))
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, weighted_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

    demand_est = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(quantile * len(recent_demand)), len(recent_demand)-1)]

# Objective: 2649.73013
# Description: An inventory algorithm using quantile-based demand estimation with dynamic safety stock scaling and cost-aware exponential smoothing.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation
    demand_window = history_demand[-5:] if len(history_demand) >= 5 else history_demand
    quantile = 0.75
    demand_estimate = sorted(demand_window)[min(len(demand_window)-1, int(quantile * len(demand_window)))]
    
    # Dynamic safety stock scaling
    recent_lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.5
    safety_factor = min(1.5, 1 + recent_lost_ratio * cost_ratio)
    safety_stock = demand_estimate * safety_factor
    
    # Exponential smoothing blend
    alpha = 0.2
    if len(history_demand) > 1:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * (sum(history_demand[:-1]) / (len(history_demand) - 1))
        demand_estimate = 0.7 * demand_estimate + 0.3 * smoothed_demand
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.3
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1]) / (len(history_demand) - 1)
    
    stockout_rate = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0

    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + stockout_rate * cost_factor)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2654.92663
# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that uses quantile-based demand estimation with non-linear cost scaling, dynamically adjusting safety stock based on exponential moving averages of stockout severity and cost ratios.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation (75th percentile)
    demand_quantile = sorted(history_demand)[int(0.75 * len(history_demand))] if len(history_demand) > 1 else history_demand[0]
    
    # Exponential moving average of lost sales ratio (alpha=0.3)
    ema_lost_ratio = 0.0
    if len(history_lost) >= 1:
        alpha = 0.3
        for i in range(len(history_lost)):
            ema_lost_ratio = alpha * (history_lost[i] / (history_demand[i] + 1e-6)) + (1 - alpha) * ema_lost_ratio
    
    # Non-linear cost scaling factor
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost * ema_lost_ratio) / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    scaling_factor = min(2.5, 1 + (cost_ratio ** 0.7))
    
    # Dynamic safety stock
    safety_stock = demand_quantile * scaling_factor
    
    # Current inventory position
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_quantile + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2659.19435
# Description: "A quantile-regression-based inventory policy that dynamically adjusts safety stock levels using demand quantiles and cost-ratio-weighted lost sales feedback, with asymmetric response to inventory surpluses vs shortages."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    quantile_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-quantile_window:]
    high_quantile = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))]
    median_demand = sorted(recent_demand)[len(recent_demand) // 2]
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    lost_sales_adjustment = 1.5 * cost_ratio * sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    shortage_penalty = 2.0 if inventory_position < median_demand else 1.0
    
    safety_stock = high_quantile + shortage_penalty * lost_sales_adjustment * median_demand
    order_amount = max(0, median_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

# Description: A momentum-based inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities using exponential smoothing of demand trends, weighted by a cost-sensitive shortage prevention factor and lead-time aware buffer stock.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(50, sum(history_demand[:3]) / 3) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 50
    
    alpha = 0.3
    window = min(4, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    smoothed_demand = alpha * sum(recent_demand) / window + (1 - alpha) * (sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand) if history_demand else 0)
    
    shortage_ratio = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(recent_demand) + 1e-6)
    cost_weight = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.5
    buffer_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + shortage_ratio * cost_weight)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + buffer_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that combines exponential smoothing with lost-sales-triggered safety stock adjustments, dynamically weighted by cost ratios.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    smoothing_factor = 0.3
    avg_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else (1 - smoothing_factor) * sum(history_demand[:-1]) / (len(history_demand) - 1) + smoothing_factor * history_demand[-1]
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    dynamic_buffer = avg_demand * (1 + lost_sales_ratio * cost_weight * 0.5)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand + dynamic_buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_8.json (item 18)
# Objective: 2717.62908
# Description: "A momentum-adjusted inventory policy that blends exponential smoothing of demand with cost-sensitive safety stock scaling, using weighted lost sales velocity to dynamically adjust reorder points."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Exponential smoothing parameters
    alpha = 0.3
    beta = 0.2
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
    
    # Lost sales momentum (3-period weighted average)
    lost_sales_window = history_lost[-3:] if len(history_lost) >= 3 else history_lost
    lost_sales_momentum = sum(lost_sales_window) / (len(lost_sales_window) + 1e-6)
    
    # Cost-sensitive scaling factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_factor = 1.0 + beta * cost_ratio * (1 + lost_sales_momentum / (smoothed_demand + 1e-6))
    
    # Current inventory position
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order calculation
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand * safety_factor - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(30, 1.5 * (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))
    
    recent_window = history_demand[-3:] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand
    base_demand = sum(recent_window) / len(recent_window)
    long_term_avg = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)

    percentile = min(0.95, 0.5 + 0.1 * cost_ratio)
    safety_stock = sorted(history_demand)[int(percentile * (len(history_demand)-1))] if history_demand else 0
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, blended_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)

# Objective: 2755.40297
# Description:  A volatility-adjusted demand algorithm that combines weighted historical demand percentiles with non-linear safety stock scaling based on recent lost sales intensity and inventory velocity. 
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    volatility_window = min(10, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-volatility_window:]
    demand_volatility = (max(recent_demand) - min(recent_demand)) / (sum(recent_demand)/len(recent_demand) + 1e-6)
    
    quantile_level = min(0.9, 0.6 + 0.05 * cost_ratio * (1 + demand_volatility))
    demand_estimate = sorted(history_demand)[int(quantile_level * (len(history_demand) - 1))]
    
    lost_sales_intensity = sum(history_lost[-4:]) / (sum(history_demand[-4:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 4 else 0
    safety_multiplier = min(2.5, 1.0 + lost_sales_intensity * cost_ratio**0.7)
    
    inventory_velocity = (history_inventory[-1] - history_inventory[-3])/3 if len(history_inventory) >= 3 else 0
    adjusted_demand = demand_estimate * (1 + 0.3 * min(1.0, max(-1.0, inventory_velocity / (demand_estimate + 1e-6))))
    
    safety_stock = adjusted_demand * safety_multiplier
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2812.65437
# Description: An adaptive quantile-based algorithm that blends short-term demand quantiles with cost-sensitive safety stock adjustments, using exponential smoothing for trend adaptation.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(50, sum(history_demand[:3])/3) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 100
    
    window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    quantile = sorted(history_demand[-window:])[int(window*0.75)]
    trend = 0.5 * quantile + 0.5 * (sum(history_demand[-window:]) / window)
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    lost_severity = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(history_demand[-window:]) + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = trend * (1 + 0.2 * cost_ratio * lost_severity)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, trend + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_8.json (item 26)
# Objective: 2838.90771
# Description: A demand-percentile based adaptive algorithm with dynamic safety stock scaling using cost-weighted shortage penalties and inventory-dependent adjustment factors.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(4, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    recent_lost = history_lost[-window:] if history_lost else [0]*window
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    demand_q75 = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75*(window-1))] if window > 1 else recent_demand[0]
    lost_ratio = sum(recent_lost) / (sum(recent_demand) + 1e-6)
    
    cost_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    inv_penalty = 1.5 / (1 + current_inv) if current_inv > 0 else 2.0
    
    safety_factor = (1 + cost_weight * lost_ratio * inv_penalty)
    safety_stock = safety_factor * demand_q75
    
    order_amount = max(0, demand_q75 + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2839.45163
# Description: Adaptive quantile-based ordering with exponential smoothing of demand trends and non-linear safety stock scaling based on cost ratios and recent stockout patterns.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 50
    
    window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    quantile = 0.7 + 0.1 * (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6))
    demand_est = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(quantile * len(recent_demand)), len(recent_demand)-1)]
    
    smoothed_demand = 0.6 * demand_est + 0.4 * (sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 if len(history_demand) >=3 else demand_est)
    
    stockout_rate = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >=3 else 0
    cost_factor = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.7
    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + min(stockout_rate * cost_factor * 0.3, 1.0))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2849.1213
# Description: A momentum-based demand forecasting algorithm that dynamically adjusts both the estimation window size and quantile threshold based on recent demand volatility, while using exponential smoothing of cost ratios for buffer calculation.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    volatility = (max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:])) / (sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    window = min(5 + int(volatility * 5), len(history_demand))
    quantile_threshold = 0.5 + min(0.3, volatility * 0.5)
    
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    demand_estimate = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(quantile_threshold * (window - 1)), window - 1)]
    
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(history_demand[-window:]) + 1e-6)
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.6
    buffer = demand_estimate * (1 + lost_ratio * cost_ratio)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2855.19252
# Description: An adaptive quantile-based algorithm that dynamically adjusts demand estimation using weighted historical windows and cost-sensitive safety stock, with exponential smoothing of shortage impacts.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    window = min(5 + int(cost_ratio * 0.2), len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    quantile = min(0.3 + 0.5 * (1 - 1/(1 + cost_ratio)), 0.9)
    demand_estimate = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(quantile * (window - 1)), window - 1)]
    
    shortage_impact = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    safety_stock = demand_estimate * (1 + shortage_impact * cost_ratio**0.5)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: A quantile-based adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock using cost-weighted demand percentiles and recent stockout patterns, with exponential smoothing for stability.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    smoothing_factor = 0.2
    recent_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-recent_window:]
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    quantile_level = min(0.8, 0.5 + 0.1 * cost_ratio)
    demand_estimate = sorted(recent_demand)[int(quantile_level * (len(recent_demand) - 1))]
    smoothed_demand = (1 - smoothing_factor) * (sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)) + smoothing_factor * demand_estimate
    
    stockout_severity = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + stockout_severity * min(2.0, cost_ratio * 0.3))
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description:  A quantile-based adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock using cost-weighted lost sales volatility and recent demand trends, with non-linear scaling of buffer sizes. 
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Demand estimation using 75th percentile
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand))
    demand_estimate = sorted_demand[quantile_idx] if quantile_idx < len(sorted_demand) else sorted_demand[-1]
    
    # Lost sales volatility factor (last 3 periods)
    lost_sales_vol = (sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6)) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = demand_estimate * (1 + min(2.0, lost_sales_vol * cost_ratio ** 0.7))
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2375.4
# Description: "A quantile-based adaptive policy that combines demand percentile forecasting with non-linear cost-sensitive safety stock adjustments, emphasizing recent stockout patterns while smoothing extreme fluctuations."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 50
    
    # Dynamic demand quantile (75th percentile adjusted by cost ratio)
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    quantile = 0.5 + 0.25 * min(1.0, cost_ratio**0.7)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    idx = min(len(sorted_demand)-1, int(quantile * len(sorted_demand)))
    forecast_demand = sorted_demand[idx]
    
    # Recent stockout emphasis (last 3 periods)
    recent_stockouts = sum(history_lost[-3:]) if len(history_lost) >= 3 else 0
    stockout_factor = 1.0 + min(1.0, recent_stockouts / (3 * forecast_demand + 1e-6))
    
    # Non-linear safety stock scaling
    safety_multiplier = min(2.0, 1.0 + (cost_ratio**0.7) * stockout_factor / 3)
    
    # Current inventory position
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order calculation
    order_amount = max(0, forecast_demand * safety_multiplier - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Description: A quantile-based adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock using recent demand volatility and cost-weighted lost sales severity, with exponential smoothing for trend responsiveness.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    smoothing_factor = 0.3
    recent_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-recent_window:]
    quantile = 0.75 if lost_sales_cost > holding_cost else 0.6
    demand_estimate = sorted(recent_demand)[int(quantile * (len(recent_demand) - 1))]
    smoothed_demand = (1 - smoothing_factor) * (sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)) + smoothing_factor * demand_estimate
    
    lost_severity = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (3 * smoothed_demand + 1e-6) if len(history_lost) >= 3 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    volatility = (max(recent_demand) - min(recent_demand)) / (smoothed_demand + 1e-6)
    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + min(2.0, lost_severity * cost_ratio * volatility))
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount
