def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 50
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation (75th percentile)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    base_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    # Dynamic stockout weighting
    stockout_rate = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.7  # Non-linear scaling
    
    # Safety stock calculation
    safety_stock = base_demand * (1 + min(stockout_rate * cost_ratio * 0.3, 0.6))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2445.45465
# Description: A novel demand-shaping algorithm that combines quantile-based demand estimation with momentum-adjusted inventory targets and asymmetric cost-sensitive buffers.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 75
    
    momentum_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    momentum = (sum(history_demand[-momentum_window:]) - sum(history_demand[-2*momentum_window:-momentum_window])) / momentum_window if len(history_demand) >= 2*momentum_window else 0
    
    quantile_window = min(10, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-quantile_window:]
    demand_q = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.65 * len(recent_demand))] if recent_demand else 0
    
    stress_factor = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_asymmetry = min(2.5, max(0.5, (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.4))
    dynamic_buffer = demand_q * (1 + stress_factor * cost_asymmetry * 0.5) + momentum * 0.7
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_q + dynamic_buffer - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2455.4091
# Description: ```
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Full-history quantile estimation (80th percentile)
    demand_quantile = sorted(history_demand)[int(0.8 * len(history_demand))] if len(history_demand) > 1 else history_demand[0]
    
    # Double EMA for lost sales ratio (alpha=0.2, beta=0.4)
    ema1 = ema2 = 0.0
    alpha, beta = 0.2, 0.4
    for i in range(len(history_lost)):
        ratio = history_lost[i] / (history_demand[i] + 1e-6)
        ema1 = alpha * ratio + (1 - alpha) * ema1
        ema2 = beta * ema1 + (1 - beta) * ema2
    
    # Dynamic cost-sensitive scaling with logistic function
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost * ema2) / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    scaling_factor = 1 + (3 / (1 + 2.718 ** (-0.5 * (cost_ratio - 2))))
    
    # Lead-time adjusted order calculation
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_quantile * scaling_factor - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Demand estimation using weighted moving average of top 30% demands
    top_demands = sorted(history_demand)[int(0.7 * len(history_demand)):]
    demand_estimate = sum(top_demands) / (len(top_demands) + 1e-6)
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on cost-weighted lost sales momentum
    lost_momentum = sum(history_lost[-3:]) - sum(history_lost[-6:-3]) if len(history_lost) >= 6 else 0
    cost_weight = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.6
    safety_stock = demand_estimate * (1 + min(1.8, max(0, lost_momentum * cost_weight / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6))))
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

    p85 = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.85 * len(recent_demand))]
    demand_estimate = 0.3 * p50 + 0.7 * p85

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_9.json (item 9)
# Objective: 2609.50421
# Description: A demand-responsive algorithm that uses weighted demand volatility and cost-adaptive safety stock, dynamically adjusted by recent inventory performance and lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.4
    beta = 0.3
    gamma = 0.5
    
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
        demand_volatility = 0
    else:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/len(history_demand[:-1])
        demand_volatility = max(0, (max(history_demand[-3:]) - min(history_demand[-3:]))/smoothed_demand) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    inventory_ratio = 1 - (sum(history_inventory[-3:])/sum(history_demand[-3:])) if len(history_demand) >= 3 and sum(history_demand[-3:]) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_adjustment = gamma * (lost_sales_cost * lost_sales_ratio + holding_cost * inventory_ratio) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    
    safety_stock = (1 + beta * cost_adjustment + demand_volatility) * smoothed_demand
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2623.18437
# Description: A demand-shape-aware algorithm using weighted historical percentiles with momentum-based safety stock adjustment, where order quantities adapt to both cost ratios and recent demand acceleration patterns.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(30, sum(history_demand[:3])/3) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 30
    
    # Momentum-weighted demand estimation (75th percentile + 0.1*recent trend)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    recent_trend = sum(history_demand[-3:])/3 - sum(history_demand[-6:-3])/3 if len(history_demand) >=6 else 0
    base_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx] + 0.1 * recent_trend
    
    # Momentum-adjusted cost scaling
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)) ** 0.7
    recent_lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6)
    
    # Dynamic safety stock with momentum dampening
    safety_stock = base_demand * min(1.0, 0.3 * cost_ratio * (1 + 2*recent_lost_ratio))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

        return 100

# Objective: 2649.73013
# Description: An inventory algorithm using quantile-based demand estimation with dynamic safety stock scaling and cost-aware exponential smoothing.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation
    demand_window = history_demand[-5:] if len(history_demand) >= 5 else history_demand
    quantile = 0.75
    demand_estimate = sorted(demand_window)[min(len(demand_window)-1, int(quantile * len(demand_window)))]
    
    # Dynamic safety stock scaling
    recent_lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.5
    safety_factor = min(1.5, 1 + recent_lost_ratio * cost_ratio)
    safety_stock = demand_estimate * safety_factor
    
    # Exponential smoothing blend
    alpha = 0.2
    if len(history_demand) > 1:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * (sum(history_demand[:-1]) / (len(history_demand) - 1))
        demand_estimate = 0.7 * demand_estimate + 0.3 * smoothed_demand
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    alpha = 0.3
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1]) / (len(history_demand) - 1)
    
    stockout_rate = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0

    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + stockout_rate * cost_factor)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0

# Objective: 2654.92663
# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that uses quantile-based demand estimation with non-linear cost scaling, dynamically adjusting safety stock based on exponential moving averages of stockout severity and cost ratios.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation (75th percentile)
    demand_quantile = sorted(history_demand)[int(0.75 * len(history_demand))] if len(history_demand) > 1 else history_demand[0]
    
    # Exponential moving average of lost sales ratio (alpha=0.3)
    ema_lost_ratio = 0.0
    if len(history_lost) >= 1:
        alpha = 0.3
        for i in range(len(history_lost)):
            ema_lost_ratio = alpha * (history_lost[i] / (history_demand[i] + 1e-6)) + (1 - alpha) * ema_lost_ratio
    
    # Non-linear cost scaling factor
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost * ema_lost_ratio) / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    scaling_factor = min(2.5, 1 + (cost_ratio ** 0.7))
    
    # Dynamic safety stock
    safety_stock = demand_quantile * scaling_factor
    
    # Current inventory position
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_quantile + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_9.json (item 16)
# Objective: 2659.19435
# Description: "A quantile-regression-based inventory policy that dynamically adjusts safety stock levels using demand quantiles and cost-ratio-weighted lost sales feedback, with asymmetric response to inventory surpluses vs shortages."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    quantile_window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-quantile_window:]
    high_quantile = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))]
    median_demand = sorted(recent_demand)[len(recent_demand) // 2]
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    lost_sales_adjustment = 1.5 * cost_ratio * sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    shortage_penalty = 2.0 if inventory_position < median_demand else 1.0
    
    safety_stock = high_quantile + shortage_penalty * lost_sales_adjustment * median_demand
    order_amount = max(0, median_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_9.json (item 17)
# Objective: 2663.71129
# Description: An inventory algorithm using exponential smoothing of demand extremes with dynamic safety stock scaling based on cost-adaptive stockout penalties.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window = min(4, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    p70 = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(0.7 * window), window-1)]
    p90 = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(0.9 * window), window-1)]
    smoothed_demand = 0.6 * p70 + 0.4 * p90
    
    stockout_ratio = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / (sum(history_demand[-2:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 0
    cost_factor = min(3.0, (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.5)
    safety_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + stockout_ratio * cost_factor * 0.5)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_9.json (item 18)
# Objective: 2666.61376
# Description: A momentum-based inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities using exponential smoothing of demand trends, weighted by a cost-sensitive shortage prevention factor and lead-time aware buffer stock.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(50, sum(history_demand[:3]) / 3) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 50
    
    alpha = 0.3
    window = min(4, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    smoothed_demand = alpha * sum(recent_demand) / window + (1 - alpha) * (sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand) if history_demand else 0)
    
    shortage_ratio = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(recent_demand) + 1e-6)
    cost_weight = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.5
    buffer_stock = smoothed_demand * (1 + shortage_ratio * cost_weight)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand + buffer_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_9.json (item 19)
# Objective: 2675.07103
# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that combines exponential smoothing with lost-sales-triggered safety stock adjustments, dynamically weighted by cost ratios.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    smoothing_factor = 0.3
    avg_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else (1 - smoothing_factor) * sum(history_demand[:-1]) / (len(history_demand) - 1) + smoothing_factor * history_demand[-1]
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    dynamic_buffer = avg_demand * (1 + lost_sales_ratio * cost_weight * 0.5)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand + dynamic_buffer - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_9.json (item 21)
# Objective: 2706.4672
# Description: A novel algorithm combining demand volatility-adjusted quantile forecasting with exponential cost-sensitive safety stock optimization, using recent stockout patterns to dynamically scale order quantities while maintaining lead-time awareness.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(50, sum(history_demand[:2])/2) if len(history_demand) >= 2 else 50
    
    # Volatility-adjusted quantile selection (60th percentile + 0.2*std_dev)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(0.6 * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    base_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx] + 0.2 * (max(history_demand) - min(history_demand))
    
    # Exponential cost scaling with recent stockout focus
    recent_stockout = sum(history_lost[-2:]) / (sum(history_demand[-2:]) + 1e-6)
    cost_factor = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.5
    
    # Dynamic safety stock with volatility dampening
    safety_stock = base_demand * min(0.8, 0.4 * cost_factor * (1 + recent_stockout))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

        return 100
    
    window = min(7, len(history_demand))

    p90 = sorted(recent_demand)[min(int(0.9 * window), window-1)]

    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-4:]) / (sum(history_demand[-4:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 4 else 0

    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_9.json (item 23)
# Objective: 2715.71695
# Description: A reinforcement learning-inspired bandit algorithm that adaptively selects between three demand estimation strategies based on their recent cost performance, with lead-time-aware inventory balancing.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Strategy 1: Median-based
    def strategy_median():
        window = min(7, len(history_demand))
        recent = history_demand[-window:]
        return sorted(recent)[len(recent)//2]
    
    # Strategy 2: EMA with decay
    def strategy_ema():
        alpha = 0.3
        est = history_demand[0]
        for d in history_demand[1:]:
            est = alpha * d + (1-alpha) * est
        return est
    
    # Strategy 3: Worst-case recent
    def strategy_worst():
        return max(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else max(history_demand)
    
    # Select best performing strategy
    strategies = [strategy_median, strategy_ema, strategy_worst]
    strategy_perf = [0, 0, 0]
    lookback = min(5, len(history_demand))
    
    for i in range(lookback):
        for j, strat in enumerate(strategies):
            est = strat()
            actual = history_demand[i]
            cost = holding_cost * max(0, est - actual) + lost_sales_cost * max(0, actual - est)
            strategy_perf[j] += cost
    
    best_strat = strategies[strategy_perf.index(min(strategy_perf))]
    demand_estimate = best_strat()
    
    # Dynamic safety stock
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6)
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.33
    safety_stock = demand_estimate * min(1.8, 1 + lost_ratio * cost_ratio)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_9.json (item 24)
# Objective: 2717.62908
# Description: "A momentum-adjusted inventory policy that blends exponential smoothing of demand with cost-sensitive safety stock scaling, using weighted lost sales velocity to dynamically adjust reorder points."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    # Exponential smoothing parameters
    alpha = 0.3
    beta = 0.2
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
    
    # Lost sales momentum (3-period weighted average)
    lost_sales_window = history_lost[-3:] if len(history_lost) >= 3 else history_lost
    lost_sales_momentum = sum(lost_sales_window) / (len(lost_sales_window) + 1e-6)
    
    # Cost-sensitive scaling factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_factor = 1.0 + beta * cost_ratio * (1 + lost_sales_momentum / (smoothed_demand + 1e-6))
    
    # Current inventory position
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order calculation
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand * safety_factor - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2723.87344
# Description: An adaptive quantile-based algorithm that dynamically weights recent demand spikes and adjusts safety stock using cost-sensitive percentile targeting.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return max(30, 1.5 * (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))
    
    recent_window = history_demand[-3:] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand
    base_demand = sum(recent_window) / len(recent_window)
    long_term_avg = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    blended_demand = 0.7 * base_demand + 0.3 * long_term_avg
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    percentile = min(0.95, 0.5 + 0.1 * cost_ratio)
    safety_stock = sorted(history_demand)[int(percentile * (len(history_demand)-1))] if history_demand else 0
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, blended_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

        return 50
    
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)) ** 0.7

    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + safety_stock - inventory_position)

# Source: results\pops/population_generation_9.json (item 28)
# Objective: 2755.40297
# Description:  A volatility-adjusted demand algorithm that combines weighted historical demand percentiles with non-linear safety stock scaling based on recent lost sales intensity and inventory velocity. 
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    volatility_window = min(10, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-volatility_window:]
    demand_volatility = (max(recent_demand) - min(recent_demand)) / (sum(recent_demand)/len(recent_demand) + 1e-6)
    
    quantile_level = min(0.9, 0.6 + 0.05 * cost_ratio * (1 + demand_volatility))
    demand_estimate = sorted(history_demand)[int(quantile_level * (len(history_demand) - 1))]
    
    lost_sales_intensity = sum(history_lost[-4:]) / (sum(history_demand[-4:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 4 else 0
    safety_multiplier = min(2.5, 1.0 + lost_sales_intensity * cost_ratio**0.7)
    
    inventory_velocity = (history_inventory[-1] - history_inventory[-3])/3 if len(history_inventory) >= 3 else 0
    adjusted_demand = demand_estimate * (1 + 0.3 * min(1.0, max(-1.0, inventory_velocity / (demand_estimate + 1e-6))))
    
    safety_stock = adjusted_demand * safety_multiplier
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_demand + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2765.59465
# Description: An adaptive quantile-based algorithm with dynamic safety stock adjustment, using exponential smoothing of demand and cost-sensitive scaling of buffer levels.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 50
    
    # Demand estimation with exponential smoothing
    alpha = 0.3
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
    
    # Dynamic quantile calculation
    window = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
    demand_quantile = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))]
    blended_demand = 0.7 * demand_quantile + 0.3 * smoothed_demand
    
    # Cost-sensitive safety stock
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    cost_factor = min(lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6), 3.0)
    safety_stock = blended_demand * (1 + min(lost_ratio * cost_factor * 0.2, 1.5))
    
    # Order calculation
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, blended_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 7441.8
# Description: The algorithm implements Constant Order Policy by ordering a fixed quantity at regular intervals 't', regardless of the current inventory level.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    t = len(history_inventory)-1
    interval = 2
    if t%interval:
        order_amount = 0
    else:
        order_amount = 100
    return order_amount

# Description: The algorithm implements Capped Base Stock Policy, where the order quantity is limited by a cap (maximum order size). If the inventory position is below the base stock level,  an order is placed to bring it back up to 'base_stock'. However, the order quantity cannot exceed a predefined cap 'order_cap'.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

# Source: results\results_archive\results_create_None_None\pops/population_generation_0.json (item 3)
# Objective: 4294.06667
# Description: An adaptive inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order amounts based on smoothed demand trends, real-time stockouts, and cost-optimized safety stock levels.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Adaptive parameters
    alpha = 0.3  # Smoothing factor for demand
    beta = 0.2   # Smoothing factor for lost sales
    
    # Calculate smoothed demand and lost sales
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/(len(history_demand)-1)
    smoothed_lost = history_lost[-1] if len(history_lost) == 1 else beta * history_lost[-1] + (1 - beta) * sum(history_lost[:-1])/(len(history_lost)-1)
    
    # Cost-optimized safety stock
    critical_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = max(0, int(smoothed_demand * critical_ratio))
    
    # Adjusted demand forecast
    forecast = smoothed_demand + smoothed_lost
    
    # Current inventory position
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order amount calculation
    order_amount = max(0, int(forecast + safety_stock - inventory_position))
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_create_None_None\pops/population_generation_0.json (item 4)
# Objective: 5004.1899
# Description:  "A novel adaptive inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand patterns, real-time stockouts, and cost-optimized thresholds." 
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Calculate adaptive demand forecast (weighted recent demand + lost sales)
    lookback = min(3, len(history_demand))
    weighted_demand = sum(0.5**i * (history_demand[-i-1] + history_lost[-i-1]) for i in range(lookback))
    forecast = weighted_demand / sum(0.5**i for i in range(lookback))
    
    # Cost-optimized threshold calculation
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    critical_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = forecast * critical_ratio
    
    # Dynamic order calculation
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_create_None_None\pops/population_generation_0.json (item 5)
# Objective: 11719.11951
# Description: An adaptive inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand patterns, real-time stock levels, and cost-optimized thresholds for lost sales prevention.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Calculate adaptive demand forecast (weighted recent demand + lost sales)
    recent_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    weighted_demand = sum(0.5 ** i * d for i, d in enumerate(reversed(history_demand[-recent_window:])))
    weighted_lost = sum(0.3 ** i * l for i, l in enumerate(reversed(history_lost[-recent_window:])))
    forecast = (weighted_demand + weighted_lost) / (sum(0.5 ** i for i in range(recent_window)) + sum(0.3 ** i for i in range(recent_window)))
    
    # Cost-optimized threshold calculation
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = forecast * cost_ratio
    
    # Current inventory position (on-hand + in-transit)
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order amount calculation
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 11693.23518
# Description: An adaptive inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on smoothed demand trends, real-time stockouts, and cost-optimized thresholds considering lead time.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Calculate smoothed demand trend
    alpha = 0.3  # Smoothing factor
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
    
    # Calculate average lost sales ratio
    if sum(history_demand) > 0:
        lost_ratio = sum(history_lost) / sum(history_demand)
    else:
        lost_ratio = 0
    
    # Cost-adjusted safety factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_factor = 1 + (cost_ratio * lost_ratio)
    
    # Current inventory position
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order amount calculation
    order_amount = max(0, (smoothed_demand * safety_factor) - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 4219.62229
# Description: An adaptive inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand patterns, real-time stock levels, and cost-optimized thresholds to minimize total costs.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Calculate weighted demand (recent periods have higher weight)
    weights = [0.5 ** (len(history_demand) - i) for i in range(len(history_demand))]
    weighted_demand = sum(d * w for d, w in zip(history_demand, weights)) / sum(weights)
    
    # Adjust for lost sales
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost) / (sum(history_demand) + 1e-6)
    adjusted_demand = weighted_demand * (1 + lost_sales_ratio)
    
    # Cost-based safety factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = adjusted_demand * cost_ratio
    
    # Current inventory position
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order amount calculation
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_demand + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 8982.17436
# Description:  "A novel adaptive inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand patterns, real-time stock levels, and cost-optimized thresholds to minimize total costs." 
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Calculate weighted demand (recent periods have higher weight)
    n = len(history_demand)
    weights = [0.9 ** (n - i - 1) for i in range(n)]
    weighted_demand = sum(d * w for d, w in zip(history_demand, weights)) / sum(weights)
    
    # Adjust for lost sales (penalize understocking)
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost) / (sum(history_demand) + 1e-6)
    demand_adjustment = 1 + lost_sales_ratio * (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))
    adjusted_demand = weighted_demand * demand_adjustment
    
    # Consider current inventory and lead time
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    safety_stock = adjusted_demand * 0.2  # 20% buffer
    
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_demand + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    return order_amount

# Description:  "A novel adaptive inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand patterns, real-time stock levels, and cost-optimized thresholds to minimize total costs." 
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Calculate adaptive demand forecast (weighted average of recent demand and lost sales)
    recent_demand = history_demand[-3:] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand
    recent_lost = history_lost[-3:] if len(history_lost) >= 3 else history_lost
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / len(recent_demand)
    avg_lost = sum(recent_lost) / len(recent_lost) if recent_lost else 0
    forecast = 0.7 * avg_demand + 0.3 * avg_lost
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on cost ratio
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = cost_ratio * forecast
    
    # Current inventory position (on-hand + in-transit)
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Compute order amount
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Description:  "A novel adaptive inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on smoothed demand trends, real-time stockouts, and cost-optimized thresholds considering lead time." 
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Calculate smoothed demand trend
    alpha = 0.2  # Smoothing factor
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
    
    # Calculate recent lost sales ratio
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 0
    
    # Dynamic safety factor based on cost ratio
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_factor = min(2.0, max(0.5, 0.5 * cost_ratio ** 0.5))
    
    # Current inventory position
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Calculate order amount
    order_amount = max(0, smoothed_demand * (1 + lost_ratio) * safety_factor - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 4727.257
# Description: "A demand-pattern-adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts order amounts based on weighted historical demand, real-time inventory levels, and cost-optimized thresholds."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Calculate adaptive demand estimate (weighted recent demand + lost sales adjustment)
    recent_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    weighted_demand = sum(d * (i+1) for i, d in enumerate(history_demand[-recent_window:])) / sum(range(1, recent_window+1))
    lost_sales_adjustment = sum(history_lost[-recent_window:]) / recent_window if history_lost else 0
    demand_estimate = weighted_demand + lost_sales_adjustment
    
    # Cost-optimized threshold calculation
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = demand_estimate * cost_ratio
    
    # Dynamic order amount
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Description: An adaptive inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order amounts based on weighted demand patterns, real-time inventory levels, and cost-optimized thresholds considering lead time.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Calculate adaptive weights for recent demand and lost sales
    recent_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    weights = [0.5 ** i for i in range(recent_window, 0, -1)]
    weighted_demand = sum(w * d for w, d in zip(weights, history_demand[-recent_window:]))
    weighted_lost = sum(w * l for w, l in zip(weights, history_lost[-recent_window:]))
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on cost ratio
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = cost_ratio * weighted_demand
    
    # Current inventory position (on-hand + in-transit)
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Calculate order amount
    order_amount = max(0, weighted_demand + weighted_lost + safety_stock - current_inv)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 9671.71785
# Description: An adaptive inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on weighted demand patterns, real-time stockouts, and cost-optimized thresholds considering lead time.
import numpy as np

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    alpha = 0.3  # Smoothing factor for demand adaptation
    beta = 0.2   # Smoothing factor for lost sales adaptation
    
    # Calculate adaptive demand estimate
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        demand_estimate = history_demand[-1]
    else:
        demand_estimate = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * np.mean(history_demand[:-1])
    
    # Calculate lost sales adjustment
    if len(history_lost) == 1:
        lost_adjustment = history_lost[-1]
    else:
        lost_adjustment = beta * history_lost[-1] + (1 - beta) * np.mean(history_lost[:-1])
    
    # Cost-optimized threshold
    critical_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = np.percentile(history_demand, 100 * critical_ratio) if len(history_demand) > 1 else demand_estimate
    
    # Current inventory position (on-hand minus lost sales plus in-transit)
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    in_transit = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) > 1 else 0  # Lead time = 1 period
    
    inventory_position = current_inventory + in_transit
    
    # Calculate order amount
    order_amount = max(0, demand_estimate + lost_adjustment + safety_stock - inventory_position)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_create_None_None\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 0)
# Objective: 3759.76667
# Description: "A demand-shaping algorithm that combines quantile-based safety stock with momentum-adjusted demand forecasting and asymmetric cost sensitivity."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Algorithm parameters
    demand_window = 3
    momentum_factor = 0.4
    quantile_level = 0.75
    cost_sensitivity = 1.5
    
    # Momentum-adjusted demand forecast
    if len(history_demand) >= demand_window:
        recent_trend = history_demand[-1] - history_demand[-demand_window]
        base_forecast = sum(history_demand[-demand_window:]) / demand_window
        forecast = base_forecast + momentum_factor * recent_trend
    else:
        forecast = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    
    # Quantile-based safety stock
    if len(history_demand) > 1:
        sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
        idx = min(int(quantile_level * len(sorted_demand)), len(sorted_demand)-1)
        safety_stock = max(0, sorted_demand[idx] - forecast)
    else:
        safety_stock = 0
    
    # Cost-asymmetric adjustment
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost**cost_sensitivity) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost**cost_sensitivity)
    adjusted_forecast = forecast * (1 + cost_ratio)
    
    # Current inventory position
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order amount calculation
    order_amount = max(0, int(adjusted_forecast + safety_stock - inventory_position))
    
    return order_amount

    base_stock = 160
    order_amount = max(0, base_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Objective: 4294.06667
# Description: An adaptive inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts order amounts based on smoothed demand trends, real-time stockouts, and cost-optimized safety stock levels.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Adaptive parameters
    alpha = 0.3  # Smoothing factor for demand
    beta = 0.2   # Smoothing factor for lost sales
    
    # Calculate smoothed demand and lost sales
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/(len(history_demand)-1)
    smoothed_lost = history_lost[-1] if len(history_lost) == 1 else beta * history_lost[-1] + (1 - beta) * sum(history_lost[:-1])/(len(history_lost)-1)
    
    # Cost-optimized safety stock
    critical_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = max(0, int(smoothed_demand * critical_ratio))
    
    # Adjusted demand forecast
    forecast = smoothed_demand + smoothed_lost
    
    # Current inventory position
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order amount calculation
    order_amount = max(0, int(forecast + safety_stock - inventory_position))
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_create_None_None\pops/population_generation_2.json (item 0)
# Objective: 3089.75767
# Description: An inventory algorithm that uses demand clustering, probabilistic lost sales estimation, and dynamic reorder points based on cost-sensitive quantiles.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Cluster demand into high/medium/low bins
    demand_sorted = sorted(history_demand)
    n = len(demand_sorted)
    low_demand = demand_sorted[:n//3]
    medium_demand = demand_sorted[n//3:2*n//3]
    high_demand = demand_sorted[2*n//3:]
    
    # Calculate cluster probabilities
    p_low = len(low_demand) / n
    p_med = len(medium_demand) / n
    p_high = len(high_demand) / n
    
    # Expected demand per cluster
    avg_low = sum(low_demand) / (len(low_demand) or 1)
    avg_med = sum(medium_demand) / (len(medium_demand) or 1)
    avg_high = sum(high_demand) / (len(high_demand) or 1)
    
    # Weighted expected demand
    expected_demand = p_low*avg_low + p_med*avg_med + p_high*avg_high
    
    # Lost sales probability adjustment
    lost_prob = sum(1 for x in history_lost if x > 0) / (len(history_lost) or 1)
    adjusted_demand = expected_demand * (1 + lost_prob)
    
    # Cost-sensitive quantile
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    quantile_idx = min(int(cost_ratio * n), n-1)
    safety_stock = demand_sorted[quantile_idx]
    
    # Current inventory
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order amount
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_demand + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_create_None_None\pops/population_generation_2.json (item 1)
# Objective: 3642.93333
# Description: "A volatility-weighted demand forecasting algorithm with dynamic safety stock adjustment and cost-proportional reorder balancing."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Algorithm parameters
    volatility_window = 5
    reorder_aggression = 0.6
    min_coverage = 1.2
    cost_balance = 0.8
    
    # Volatility-weighted demand forecast
    if len(history_demand) >= volatility_window:
        avg_demand = sum(history_demand[-volatility_window:]) / volatility_window
        volatility = (max(history_demand[-volatility_window:]) - min(history_demand[-volatility_window:])) / avg_demand if avg_demand > 0 else 0
        forecast = avg_demand * (1 + reorder_aggression * volatility)
    else:
        forecast = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand) if history_demand else 0
    
    # Dynamic safety stock based on lost sales history
    if history_lost:
        lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-volatility_window:]) / sum(history_demand[-volatility_window:]) if sum(history_demand[-volatility_window:]) > 0 else 0
        safety_stock = forecast * min_coverage * lost_ratio
    else:
        safety_stock = 0
    
    # Cost-proportional reorder adjustment
    cost_factor = (lost_sales_cost * cost_balance) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost * cost_balance)
    adjusted_order = forecast * (1 + cost_factor) + safety_stock
    
    # Current inventory position
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order amount calculation
    order_amount = max(0, int(adjusted_order - inventory_position))
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_create_None_None\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 1)
# Objective: 3346.83071
# Description: An inventory algorithm that uses exponential smoothing for demand forecasting, dynamic safety stock based on cost-weighted demand variability, and lead-time adjusted reorder points.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Exponential smoothing parameters
    alpha = 0.3  # smoothing factor for demand
    beta = 0.2   # smoothing factor for variability
    
    # Initialize smoothed values
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[0] if history_demand else 0
    smoothed_var = 0
    
    # Update smoothed values
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
        smoothed_var = beta * abs(d - smoothed_demand) + (1 - beta) * smoothed_var
    
    # Cost-weighted adjustment factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_factor = 1 + cost_ratio * (smoothed_var / (smoothed_demand + 1e-6))
    
    # Lead-time adjusted forecast
    lead_time = 1
    forecast = smoothed_demand * (1 + lead_time)
    
    # Safety stock calculation
    safety_stock = safety_factor * smoothed_var * (lead_time ** 0.5)
    
    # Current inventory
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order amount
    order_amount = max(0, forecast + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_create_None_None\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 2)
# Objective: 3373.52926
# Description: An inventory algorithm using quantile-based demand forecasting, dynamic cost-balancing weights, and lead-time-adjusted safety stock with demand variability compensation.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Parameters
    demand_quantile = 0.75
    variability_factor = 0.5
    cost_sensitivity = 0.8
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(len(sorted_demand) * demand_quantile), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    forecast_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    # Demand variability compensation
    if len(history_demand) > 1:
        demand_std = (max(history_demand) - min(history_demand)) / 2
        variability_comp = demand_std * variability_factor
    else:
        variability_comp = 0
    
    # Lost sales adjustment
    lost_ratio = sum(history_lost) / (sum(history_demand) + 1e-6) if history_lost else 0
    
    # Cost-based weighting
    cost_weight = (lost_sales_cost * cost_sensitivity) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost * cost_sensitivity + 1e-6)
    
    # Lead time adjustment
    adjusted_demand = forecast_demand * (1 + lost_ratio) + variability_comp
    
    # Safety stock
    safety_stock = adjusted_demand * cost_weight
    
    # Current inventory
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order amount
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_demand + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3498.34854
# Description: An inventory algorithm using exponential smoothing of demand with lead-time adjustment, weighted cost optimization, and dynamic safety stock based on recent lost sales patterns.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Exponential smoothing parameters
    alpha = 0.3  # demand smoothing factor
    beta = 0.2    # lost sales smoothing factor
    
    # Calculate smoothed demand
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
    
    # Calculate smoothed lost sales rate
    if history_lost:
        lost_rate = sum(1 for x in history_lost if x > 0) / len(history_lost)
        smoothed_lost = beta * lost_rate + (1 - beta) * (sum(history_lost)/sum(history_demand) if sum(history_demand) > 0 else 0)
    else:
        smoothed_lost = 0
    
    # Lead time adjustment
    lead_time_demand = smoothed_demand * (1 + smoothed_lost)
    
    # Cost-weighted safety factor
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_factor = 1 + cost_ratio * (1 + smoothed_lost)
    
    # Current inventory
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order amount
    order_amount = max(0, lead_time_demand * safety_factor - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2974.54134
# Description: A novel algorithm that combines quantile-based demand forecasting, weighted cost-sensitive reorder points, and lead-time-adjusted inventory buffers with asymmetric loss sensitivity.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Quantile-based demand estimation
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    quantile_idx = min(int(len(sorted_demand) * cost_ratio), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    forecast_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    # Asymmetric loss adjustment
    loss_adjustment = 1.0
    if history_lost:
        recent_loss_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) or 1)
        loss_adjustment = 1 + (cost_ratio * recent_loss_ratio)
    
    # Weighted reorder point calculation
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    lead_time_factor = 1  # Since lead time = 1 period
    weighted_demand = forecast_demand * loss_adjustment * lead_time_factor
    
    # Dynamic buffer based on cost asymmetry
    buffer_factor = 0.5 + (0.5 * cost_ratio)
    buffer_stock = forecast_demand * buffer_factor
    
    order_amount = max(0, weighted_demand + buffer_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3000.14625
# Description: An inventory algorithm that uses demand volatility bands, exponential smoothing of lost sales patterns, and a dynamic safety stock multiplier based on cost ratios.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Calculate demand volatility bands
    mean_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    std_demand = (sum((x - mean_demand)**2 for x in history_demand) / len(history_demand))**0.5
    volatility_ratio = std_demand / (mean_demand or 1)
    
    # Exponential smoothing for lost sales
    alpha = 0.3
    smoothed_lost = 0
    if history_lost:
        smoothed_lost = history_lost[-1]
        for lost in reversed(history_lost[:-1]):
            smoothed_lost = alpha * lost + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_lost
    
    # Dynamic safety stock multiplier
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_multiplier = 1 + cost_ratio * volatility_ratio
    
    # Base order calculation
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    adjusted_demand = mean_demand * (1 + smoothed_lost / (mean_demand or 1))
    safety_stock = mean_demand * safety_multiplier
    
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_demand + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_create_None_None\pops/population_generation_4.json (item 2)
# Objective: 3089.75767
# Description: An inventory algorithm that uses demand clustering, probabilistic lost sales estimation, and dynamic reorder points based on cost-sensitive quantiles.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Cluster demand into high/medium/low bins
    demand_sorted = sorted(history_demand)
    n = len(demand_sorted)
    low_demand = demand_sorted[:n//3]
    medium_demand = demand_sorted[n//3:2*n//3]
    high_demand = demand_sorted[2*n//3:]
    
    # Calculate cluster probabilities
    p_low = len(low_demand) / n
    p_med = len(medium_demand) / n
    p_high = len(high_demand) / n
    
    # Expected demand per cluster
    avg_low = sum(low_demand) / (len(low_demand) or 1)
    avg_med = sum(medium_demand) / (len(medium_demand) or 1)
    avg_high = sum(high_demand) / (len(high_demand) or 1)
    
    # Weighted expected demand
    expected_demand = p_low*avg_low + p_med*avg_med + p_high*avg_high
    
    # Lost sales probability adjustment
    lost_prob = sum(1 for x in history_lost if x > 0) / (len(history_lost) or 1)
    adjusted_demand = expected_demand * (1 + lost_prob)
    
    # Cost-sensitive quantile
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    quantile_idx = min(int(cost_ratio * n), n-1)
    safety_stock = demand_sorted[quantile_idx]
    
    # Current inventory
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    
    # Order amount
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_demand + safety_stock - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_create_None_None\pops/population_generation_4.json (item 3)
# Objective: 3238.74918
# Description: A novel algorithm that uses exponential smoothing with adaptive learning rates, demand volatility scoring, and cost-weighted inventory balancing with lead time anticipation.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Exponential smoothing parameters
    alpha = 0.2
    beta = 0.1
    
    # Initialize smoothed values
    S = history_demand[0] if history_demand else 0
    B = 0
    
    # Adaptive smoothing with volatility
    volatility = []
    for i in range(1, len(history_demand)):
        prev_S = S
        S = alpha * history_demand[i] + (1 - alpha) * (S + B)
        B = beta * (S - prev_S) + (1 - beta) * B
        volatility.append(abs(history_demand[i] - history_demand[i-1]))
    
    avg_volatility = sum(volatility) / len(volatility) if volatility else 0
    
    # Adjust smoothing based on volatility
    if avg_volatility > 0:
        alpha_adj = min(0.5, alpha * (1 + avg_volatility / max(history_demand)))
    else:
        alpha_adj = alpha
    
    # Final forecast with adjusted parameters
    forecast = S + B
    
    # Cost-weighted inventory balancing
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_factor = 1 + cost_ratio * avg_volatility / (max(history_demand) or 1)
    
    # Lead time anticipation (1 period)
    anticipated_inventory = (history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0) - forecast
    
    # Order amount calculation
    order_amount = max(0, forecast * safety_factor - anticipated_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

    if not history_demand:
        return 100

    quantile = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    demand_mean = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    demand_var = sum((d - demand_mean)**2 for d in history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    demand_std = demand_var**0.5
    
    lead_time_factor = 1.0
    dynamic_multiplier = 1.0 + cost_ratio * (1.0 + demand_std / max(1, demand_mean))

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    demand_window = history_demand[-3:] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand
    q75 = sorted(demand_window)[int(0.75*len(demand_window))] if demand_window else 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)
    demand_cluster = max(1, int(sum(demand_window)/len(demand_window)) if demand_window else 0)
    
    threshold_factor = min(2.0, 0.5 + cost_ratio * 0.1)
    safety_stock = int(q75 * threshold_factor)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    target_level = demand_cluster + safety_stock
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - inventory_position)
    
    return order_amount

    quantile_demand = demand_sorted[quantile_idx]
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = int(quantile_demand * cost_ratio * 1.2)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1]
    target_level = quantile_demand + safety_stock
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - inventory_position)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_high\pops/population_generation_4.json (item 0)
# Objective: 2644.22222
# Description: Demand-Driven Quantile Policy: Adjust order-up-to level based on empirical demand quantiles weighted by cost ratio, with dynamic adjustment for recent lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return max(0, 160 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    recent_lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6)
    quantile_level = min(0.95, max(0.5, cost_ratio + recent_lost_ratio * 0.2))
    demand_sorted = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_index = min(len(demand_sorted)-1, int(quantile_level * len(demand_sorted)))
    target_level = demand_sorted[quantile_index] * (1 + cost_ratio)
    
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2767.89472
# Description: Adaptive Threshold Policy: Dynamically adjust order-up-to level based on weighted demand patterns and cost ratio, smoothed with exponential moving average.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return max(0, 160 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 0
    
    alpha = 0.3
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    demand_ema = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1]) / max(1, len(history_demand)-1)
    safety_factor = 1.5 * cost_ratio
    dynamic_base = demand_ema * (1 + safety_factor)
    
    order_amount = max(0, dynamic_base - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Objective: 2827.10556
# Description: Demand-Quantile Balancing Policy: Use historical demand quantiles weighted by cost ratio to set order thresholds, with dynamic adjustment based on recent lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return max(0, 160 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    recent_lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6)
    quantile_level = min(0.95, max(0.5, cost_ratio + recent_lost_ratio * 0.5))
    demand_threshold = sorted(history_demand)[int(quantile_level * (len(history_demand)-1))]
    
    order_amount = max(0, demand_threshold * (1 + cost_ratio) - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_high\pops/population_generation_4.json (item 3)
# Objective: 3424.73333
# Description: "New algorithm: 'Demand-driven quantile optimization with lead-time-aware inventory balancing, using historical lost sales patterns to adjust safety stock levels based on cost-optimal quantiles of demand distribution.'"
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    quantile = min(0.99, max(0.01, cost_ratio))
    
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    idx = min(len(sorted_demand)-1, int(quantile * len(sorted_demand)))
    quantile_demand = sorted_demand[idx]
    
    lost_sales_ratio = sum(history_lost) / (sum(history_demand) + 1e-6)
    adjustment_factor = 1.0 + (2.0 * lost_sales_ratio * cost_ratio)
    
    safety_stock = int(quantile_demand * adjustment_factor)
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1]
    order_amount = max(0, (quantile_demand + safety_stock) - inventory_position)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_high\pops/population_generation_4.json (item 4)
# Objective: 3508.96667
# Description: "New algorithm: 'Adaptive exponential smoothing with dynamic safety stock adjustment, using weighted demand trends and cost-balancing factors to determine optimal order quantities while accounting for lead time.'"
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    alpha = 0.3
    if len(history_demand) > 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1] * alpha + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1]) / (len(history_demand) - 1)
    else:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
    
    cost_factor = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    dynamic_multiplier = 1.0 + (cost_factor * 0.5)
    safety_stock = int(smoothed_demand * dynamic_multiplier)
    
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1]
    target_level = int(smoothed_demand) + safety_stock
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - inventory_position)
    
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    t = len(history_inventory)-1
    interval = 2
    if t%interval:
        order_amount = 0
    else:
        order_amount = 100
    return order_amount

# Description: The algorithm implements a Demand-Weighted Adaptive Policy that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on recent demand patterns, current inventory levels, and cost ratios, while accounting for lead time.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return max(0, 100 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 100
    
    recent_demand = history_demand[-3:] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / len(recent_demand)
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = avg_demand * cost_ratio * 1.5
    target_level = avg_demand + safety_stock
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_low\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 1)
# Objective: 3681.88667
# Description: The algorithm implements a demand-driven adaptive threshold policy that dynamically adjusts the order-up-to level based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, while respecting lead time constraints.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = 5
    if len(history_demand) < demand_window:
        avg_demand = sum(history_demand) / max(1, len(history_demand))
    else:
        avg_demand = sum(history_demand[-demand_window:]) / demand_window
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1)
    safety_factor = min(2.0, max(1.0, cost_ratio * 0.5))
    target_level = avg_demand * safety_factor
    
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - current_inventory)
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return max(0, 100 - history_inventory[-1])
    
    alpha = 0.3

    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_factor = 1.5 * cost_ratio
    target = forecast * (1 + safety_factor)
    order_amount = max(0, target - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

# Objective: 3167.9
# Description: The algorithm implements a Quantile-Based Dynamic Threshold policy that sets order amounts based on demand quantiles, cost-adjusted thresholds, and lead-time-aware inventory positioning.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    demand_window = history_demand[-min(5, len(history_demand)):]
    sorted_demand = sorted(demand_window)
    quantile_idx = min(int(len(sorted_demand) * cost_ratio), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    threshold = sorted_demand[quantile_idx] * (1 + cost_ratio)
    
    if history_inventory:
        order_amount = max(0, threshold - history_inventory[-1])
    else:
        order_amount = threshold
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3158.89152
# Description: An adaptive quantile-based algorithm that uses demand percentiles and dynamic cost-weighting to adjust safety stock levels based on historical lost sales patterns.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    window_size = min(5, len(history_demand))
    recent_demand = history_demand[-window_size:]
    recent_lost = history_lost[-window_size:] if history_lost else [0]*window_size
    
    demand_75 = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75*(window_size-1))]
    lost_ratio = sum(recent_lost) / (sum(recent_demand) + 1e-6)
    
    cost_factor = (lost_sales_cost * (1 + lost_ratio)) / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = demand_75 * cost_factor * 1.2
    
    target_level = demand_75 + safety_stock
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - current_inventory)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_low\pops/population_generation_4.json (item 4)
# Objective: 3451.86471
# Description: The new algorithm implements a Demand Volatility-Adjusted Policy that dynamically adjusts order amounts based on recent demand volatility, cost-sensitive safety factors, and lead-time inventory coverage.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 100
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    recent_demand = history_demand[-min(7, len(history_demand)):]
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / len(recent_demand)
    demand_std = (sum((x - avg_demand)**2 for x in recent_demand) / len(recent_demand))**0.5
    
    volatility_factor = 1 + (demand_std / avg_demand if avg_demand > 0 else 0)
    safety_stock = avg_demand * cost_ratio * volatility_factor
    
    if history_inventory:
        order_amount = max(0, (avg_demand + safety_stock) - history_inventory[-1])
    else:
        order_amount = avg_demand + safety_stock
    
    return order_amount

# Objective: 9998.86667
# Description: The algorithm implements Constant Order Policy by ordering a fixed quantity at regular intervals 't', regardless of the current inventory level.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    t = len(history_inventory)-1
    interval = 2
    if t%interval:
        order_amount = 0
    else:
        order_amount = 100
    return order_amount

If the inventory position is below the base stock level,  an order is placed to bring it back up to 'base_stock'. However, the order quantity cannot exceed a predefined cap 'order_cap'.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_median\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 1)
# Objective: 4800.4
# Description: The algorithm implements a Dynamic Threshold Policy that adjusts the target inventory level based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, then orders the difference between this dynamic target and current inventory if positive.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = 5
    if len(history_demand) >= demand_window:
        recent_demand = sum(history_demand[-demand_window:]) / demand_window
    else:
        recent_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand) if history_demand else 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    dynamic_target = int(recent_demand * (1 + cost_ratio * 2))
    order_amount = max(0, dynamic_target - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_median\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 2)
# Objective: 4830.76111
# Description: The new algorithm dynamically adjusts the target inventory level based on recent demand patterns and cost parameters, then orders to reach this adaptive target.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 3:
        adaptive_stock = 160
    else:
        recent_demand = sum(history_demand[-3:]) / 3
        cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
        adaptive_stock = recent_demand * (1 + cost_ratio)
    
    order_amount = max(0, adaptive_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_median\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 3)
# Objective: 4838.54667
# Description: An adaptive threshold algorithm that dynamically adjusts the base stock level based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, then orders to reach this level while accounting for lead time.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = history_demand[-5:] if len(history_demand) >= 5 else history_demand
    avg_demand = sum(demand_window) / len(demand_window) if demand_window else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    adaptive_stock = avg_demand * (1 + cost_ratio * 2)
    order_amount = max(0, adaptive_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_median\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 4)
# Objective: 4879.14556
# Description: 
"A demand-weighted adaptive threshold algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, smoothing orders while preventing stockouts."

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = 5
    if len(history_demand) < demand_window:
        avg_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand) if len(history_demand) > 0 else 0
    else:
        avg_demand = sum(history_demand[-demand_window:]) / demand_window
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_factor = 1.0 + cost_ratio
    
    target_inventory = avg_demand * safety_factor
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if len(history_inventory) > 0 else 0
    
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - current_inventory)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_median\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 1)
# Objective: 4017.16246
# Description: An adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock based on recent demand volatility and cost-weighted lost sales ratio, while smoothing orders with exponential weighting.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        base_demand = 160
        volatility = 0.0
        lost_ratio = 0.0
    else:
        recent_demand = history_demand[-4:]
        base_demand = sum(recent_demand) / len(recent_demand)
        volatility = max(recent_demand) - min(recent_demand) if len(recent_demand) > 1 else 0.0
        lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-4:]) / (sum(history_demand[-4:]) + 1e-6)
    
    cost_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_factor = 1 + (cost_weight * (1 + lost_ratio + volatility/base_demand))
    smoothed_demand = 0.7 * base_demand + 0.3 * (history_demand[-1] if history_demand else base_demand)
    target_level = smoothed_demand * safety_factor
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_median\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 2)
# Objective: 4276.36384
# Description: An adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock based on recent demand volatility and cost ratios, while smoothing orders with exponential weighting.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        avg_demand = 160
        std_demand = 0
        lost_ratio = 0.0
    else:
        window = min(4, len(history_demand))
        recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
        avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / window
        std_demand = (sum((x - avg_demand)**2 for x in recent_demand) / window)**0.5
        lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(history_demand[-window:]) + 1e-6)
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    volatility_factor = 1 + (std_demand / (avg_demand + 1e-6))
    safety_stock = avg_demand * cost_ratio * volatility_factor * (1 + lost_ratio)
    target_level = avg_demand + safety_stock
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_median\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 3)
# Objective: 4284.23756
# Description: A novel algorithm that uses demand volatility-adjusted safety stock and cost-optimized reorder points, dynamically adjusting based on recent demand variability and cost ratios.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        volatility_factor = 1.0
        avg_demand = 160
    else:
        recent_demand = history_demand[-3:] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand
        avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / len(recent_demand)
        demand_std = (sum((x - avg_demand)**2 for x in recent_demand) / len(recent_demand))**0.5
        volatility_factor = 1 + (demand_std / avg_demand) if avg_demand > 0 else 1.0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    target_level = avg_demand * volatility_factor * (1 + cost_ratio)
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Description: ```
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        avg_demand = 160
        lost_ratio = 0.0
    else:
        avg_demand = sum(history_demand[-4:]) / min(4, len(history_demand))
        lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-4:]) / (sum(history_demand[-4:]) + 1e-6)
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    dynamic_multiplier = 1 + (cost_ratio * (1 + lost_ratio))
    target_level = avg_demand * dynamic_multiplier
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Description: An adaptive algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock based on recent demand volatility and cost-weighted lost sales ratio, while smoothing orders with exponential weighting.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        base_demand = 160
        volatility = 0.0
        lost_ratio = 0.0
    else:
        recent_demand = history_demand[-4:]
        base_demand = sum(recent_demand) / len(recent_demand)
        volatility = max(recent_demand) - min(recent_demand) if len(recent_demand) > 1 else 0.0
        lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-4:]) / (sum(history_demand[-4:]) + 1e-6)
    
    cost_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_factor = 1 + (cost_weight * (1 + lost_ratio + volatility/base_demand))
    smoothed_demand = 0.7 * base_demand + 0.3 * (history_demand[-1] if history_demand else base_demand)
    target_level = smoothed_demand * safety_factor
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount
