# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_median\pops/population_generation_4.json (item 2)
# Objective: 4097.45556
# Description: A risk-sensitive inventory algorithm that adjusts order quantities based on demand quantiles, cost imbalance, and recent stockout severity, while incorporating lead-time anticipation.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 3:
        base_demand = 160
        demand_q1 = base_demand
        demand_q3 = base_demand
        stockout_severity = 0.0
    else:
        recent_demand = history_demand[-6:]
        base_demand = sum(recent_demand) / len(recent_demand)
        sorted_demand = sorted(recent_demand)
        demand_q1 = sorted_demand[int(0.25 * len(sorted_demand))]
        demand_q3 = sorted_demand[int(0.75 * len(sorted_demand))]
        stockout_severity = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6)
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    risk_factor = min(2.0, 0.5 + cost_ratio * (1 + stockout_severity))
    lead_time_adjustment = max(demand_q3 - base_demand, 0)
    target_level = (base_demand + lead_time_adjustment) * risk_factor
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_median\pops/population_generation_4.json (item 3)
# Objective: 4132.26013
# Description:  A novel algorithm that uses a demand prediction error-based approach to adjust safety stock, incorporating both historical forecast accuracy and cost-sensitive rebalancing. 
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        avg_demand = 160
        forecast_error = 0
        lost_ratio = 0.0
    else:
        window = min(5, len(history_demand))
        recent_demand = history_demand[-window:]
        avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / window
        forecast_error = sum(abs(recent_demand[i] - avg_demand) for i in range(window)) / window
        lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(history_demand[-window:]) + 1e-6)
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    error_factor = 1 + (forecast_error / (avg_demand + 1e-6))
    safety_stock = avg_demand * cost_ratio * error_factor * (1 + lost_ratio)
    target_level = avg_demand + safety_stock
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        avg_demand = 160
        std_demand = 0
        lost_ratio = 0.0
    else:
        window = min(4, len(history_demand))

        lost_ratio = sum(history_lost[-window:]) / (sum(history_demand[-window:]) + 1e-6)
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost + 1e-6)
    volatility_factor = 1 + (std_demand / (avg_demand + 1e-6))

    target_level = avg_demand + safety_stock
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])

# Description: Adaptive Base Stock Policy: Dynamically adjusts the base stock level based on recent demand trends and cost ratio, then orders to reach this adjusted level.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = 5
    if len(history_demand) >= demand_window:
        recent_demand = sum(history_demand[-demand_window:]) / demand_window
    else:
        recent_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand) if len(history_demand) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    adaptive_base_stock = recent_demand * (1 + cost_ratio)
    
    order_amount = max(0, adaptive_base_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_None\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 2)
# Objective: 6274.98356
# Description: Demand-Weighted Cost-Balanced Policy: Adjusts order amounts by applying exponential smoothing to demand trends and dynamically scaling based on a nonlinear cost-balancing factor.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    smoothing_factor = 0.3
    if len(history_demand) > 0:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1] * smoothing_factor + (1 - smoothing_factor) * (sum(history_demand[:-1]) / (len(history_demand) - 1)) if len(history_demand) > 1 else history_demand[-1]
    else:
        smoothed_demand = 0
    
    cost_balance = (lost_sales_cost ** 2) / (holding_cost ** 2 + lost_sales_cost ** 2)
    target_level = smoothed_demand * (1 + cost_balance * 2)
    
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Objective: 9907.10361
# Description: An adaptive base stock policy where the target level is dynamically adjusted based on recent demand trends and cost ratio.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = history_demand[-5:] if len(history_demand) >= 5 else history_demand
    avg_demand = sum(demand_window) / len(demand_window) if demand_window else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / holding_cost if holding_cost != 0 else 1
    adaptive_stock = avg_demand * (1 + cost_ratio * 0.5)
    order_amount = max(0, adaptive_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_None\pops/population_generation_2.json (item 0)
# Objective: 3692.92583
# Description: An adaptive threshold algorithm that dynamically adjusts the order-up-to level based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, using exponential smoothing for demand forecasting and a safety factor derived from cost balance.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        alpha = 0.3
    else:
        alpha = 0.3
    
    if len(history_demand) == 0:
        forecast = 100
    else:
        forecast = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * (history_demand[-2] if len(history_demand) > 1 else history_demand[-1])
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_factor = 1.5 * cost_ratio
    
    target_level = forecast * (1 + safety_factor)
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_None\pops/population_generation_2.json (item 2)
# Objective: 3995.1637
# Description: Adaptive Threshold Policy with Demand Volatility Adjustment: Uses a moving window of demand variance to dynamically adjust safety stock levels while balancing cost ratios.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    window_size = min(5, len(history_demand))
    if window_size > 0:
        recent_demand = history_demand[-window_size:]
        avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / window_size
        demand_variance = sum((x - avg_demand)**2 for x in recent_demand) / window_size
        volatility_factor = 1 + (demand_variance ** 0.5) / (avg_demand + 1)
    else:
        avg_demand = 0
        volatility_factor = 1
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_multiplier = 1 + cost_ratio * volatility_factor
    
    target_level = avg_demand * safety_multiplier
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

================================================================================

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_None\pops/population_generation_2.json (item 3)
# Objective: 4879.14556
# Description: Adaptive Base Stock Policy: Dynamically adjusts the base stock level based on recent demand trends and cost ratio, then orders to reach this adjusted level.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = 5
    if len(history_demand) >= demand_window:
        recent_demand = sum(history_demand[-demand_window:]) / demand_window
    else:
        recent_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand) if len(history_demand) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    adaptive_base_stock = recent_demand * (1 + cost_ratio)
    
    order_amount = max(0, adaptive_base_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

================================================================================

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_None\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 0)
# Objective: 3261.76667
# Description:  A probabilistic demand coverage algorithm that dynamically sets order quantities based on demand percentiles weighted by cost ratios, with a lead-time buffer. 
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) == 0:
        return 100
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    percentile = min(0.95, max(0.5, cost_ratio * 1.2))
    
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    idx = min(len(sorted_demand) - 1, int(percentile * len(sorted_demand)))
    demand_estimate = sorted_demand[idx]
    
    lead_time_buffer = max(history_demand[-3:]) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else max(history_demand)
    target_level = demand_estimate + lead_time_buffer
    
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

================================================================================

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_60_None\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 1)
# Objective: 3692.92583
# Description: An adaptive threshold algorithm that dynamically adjusts the order-up-to level based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, using exponential smoothing for demand forecasting and a safety factor derived from cost balance.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        alpha = 0.3
    else:
        alpha = 0.3
    
    if len(history_demand) == 0:
        forecast = 100
    else:
        forecast = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * (history_demand[-2] if len(history_demand) > 1 else history_demand[-1])
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_factor = 1.5 * cost_ratio
    
    target_level = forecast * (1 + safety_factor)
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    
    return order_amount

================================================================================

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    base_stock = 160
    order_amount = max(0, base_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Objective: 9998.86667
# Description: The algorithm implements Constant Order Policy by ordering a fixed quantity at regular intervals 't', regardless of the current inventory level.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    t = len(history_inventory)-1
    interval = 2
    if t%interval:
        order_amount = 0
    else:
        order_amount = 100
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_high\pops/population_generation_0.json (item 2)
# Objective: 17443.06667
# Description: The algorithm implements Capped Base Stock Policy, where the order quantity is limited by a cap (maximum order size). If the inventory position is below the base stock level,  an order is placed to bring it back up to 'base_stock'. However, the order quantity cannot exceed a predefined cap 'order_cap'.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    base_stock = 70
    order_cap = 30
    order_amount = min(base_stock - history_inventory[-1], order_cap)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_high\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 1)
# Objective: 4879.14556
# Description: Adaptive Base Stock Policy: Dynamically adjusts the base stock level based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, then orders up to this adjusted level.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = 5
    if len(history_demand) >= demand_window:
        recent_demand = history_demand[-demand_window:]
        avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / demand_window
    else:
        avg_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand) if len(history_demand) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    adjusted_base_stock = avg_demand * (1 + cost_ratio)
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_base_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_high\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 2)
# Objective: 5068.34872
# Description: "Demand-Weighted Cost-Balancing Policy: Combines moving average demand estimation with a dynamic safety stock calculation weighted by cost ratios, adjusting order amounts based on recent demand volatility and cost structure."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    if window > 0:
        avg_demand = sum(history_demand[-window:]) / window
        demand_std = (sum((d - avg_demand)**2 for d in history_demand[-window:]) / window)**0.5 if window > 1 else 0
    else:
        avg_demand = 0
        demand_std = 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = cost_ratio * avg_demand + (1 - cost_ratio) * demand_std
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, avg_demand + safety_stock - current_inv)
    return order_amount

================================================================================

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_high\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 3)
# Objective: 5149.56296
# Description: "A demand-adaptive threshold policy that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, smoothing orders while preventing stockouts."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 3:
        return max(0, 100 - history_inventory[-1])
    
    recent_demand = history_demand[-3:]
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / 3
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_factor = 1.0 + cost_ratio
    target_level = avg_demand * safety_factor
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_high\pops/population_generation_2.json (item 1)
# Objective: 3979.1246
# Description: Adaptive Threshold Policy: Dynamically adjusts order-up-to level based on weighted demand and lost sales history, using a rolling window to balance holding and shortage costs.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    window_size = min(5, len(history_demand))
    if window_size == 0:
        return 0
    weighted_demand = sum(history_demand[-window_size:]) / window_size
    weighted_lost = sum(history_lost[-window_size:]) / window_size
    safety_factor = 1 + (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))
    adaptive_stock = safety_factor * (weighted_demand + weighted_lost)
    order_amount = max(0, adaptive_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_high\pops/population_generation_2.json (item 2)
# Objective: 4257.32667
# Description: The algorithm dynamically adjusts the target inventory level based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, using exponential smoothing for demand forecasting and a cost-sensitive safety factor.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return max(0, 100 - history_inventory[-1]) if history_inventory else 100
    
    alpha = 0.3
    forecast = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * history_demand[-2]
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / holding_cost if holding_cost > 0 else 1
    safety_factor = min(2.0, max(0.5, cost_ratio * 0.2))
    target = forecast * (1 + safety_factor)
    order_amount = max(0, target - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_high\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 1)
# Objective: 3970.84631
# Description: 
Demand-Weighted Cost-Balancing Policy: Dynamically adjusts order amounts by balancing holding and lost sales costs through a demand-weighted cost ratio, incorporating lead time by considering current inventory position and recent demand variability.

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    current_inventory = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    recent_demand = history_demand[-min(3, len(history_demand)):] if history_demand else [0]
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / len(recent_demand) if recent_demand else 0
    cost_ratio = (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)) * (1 + (max(recent_demand) - min(recent_demand)) / (avg_demand + 1e-6))
    target_inventory = avg_demand * (1 + cost_ratio)
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - current_inventory)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_low\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 0)
# Objective: 3230.66207
# Description: The algorithm implements a Demand-Weighted Adaptive Threshold Policy that dynamically adjusts the order-up-to level based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, while smoothing order quantities to avoid extreme fluctuations.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = history_demand[-5:] if len(history_demand) >= 5 else history_demand
    avg_demand = sum(demand_window) / len(demand_window) if demand_window else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    dynamic_stock = avg_demand * (1 + cost_ratio) * 1.5  # Lead time factor
    smoothing_factor = 0.7
    order_amount = max(0, smoothing_factor * (dynamic_stock - history_inventory[-1]))
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_low\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 1)
# Objective: 3471.93333
# Description: Weighted Demand-Smoothing Policy: Adjusts order amounts by smoothing demand with exponential weighting of recent periods and incorporating cost-sensitive safety stock.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    alpha = 0.3  # Smoothing factor
    if len(history_demand) > 0:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1] * alpha + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1]) / max(1, len(history_demand)-1)
    else:
        smoothed_demand = 0
    
    safety_factor = 1.5 * (lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost))
    target_level = int(smoothed_demand * (1 + safety_factor))
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    base_stock = 160

    return order_amount

# Objective: 4944.56667
# Description: Adaptive Base Stock Policy: Dynamically adjusts the base stock level based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, then orders up to this adjusted level.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = 5
    if len(history_demand) >= demand_window:
        recent_demand = sum(history_demand[-demand_window:]) / demand_window
    else:
        recent_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand) if len(history_demand) > 0 else 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    adaptive_base_stock = int(recent_demand * (1 + cost_ratio))
    order_amount = max(0, adaptive_base_stock - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_low\pops/population_generation_2.json (item 0)
# Objective: 2957.3
# Description: Propose a probabilistic safety stock policy that dynamically adjusts order amounts based on demand volatility and cost-optimal service levels, using quantile estimation from historical demand distribution.
import numpy as np

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        recent_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) > 0 else 0
    else:
        recent_demand = np.mean(history_demand)
    
    demand_std = np.std(history_demand) if len(history_demand) > 1 else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_factor = np.percentile(history_demand, int(100 * cost_ratio)) if len(history_demand) > 0 else 0
    target_level = int(recent_demand + safety_factor + demand_std * cost_ratio)
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_low\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 2)
# Objective: 3350.61564
# Description: An adaptive safety stock algorithm that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on demand volatility and cost-optimal service levels, incorporating exponential smoothing of demand and lost sales trends.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if len(history_demand) < 2:
        return max(0, sum(history_demand) - history_inventory[-1]) if history_demand else 0
    
    alpha = 0.3  # Smoothing factor for demand
    beta = 0.2   # Smoothing factor for lost sales
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[-1]
    smoothed_lost = history_lost[-1] if history_lost else 0
    
    for i in range(len(history_demand)-1):
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[i] + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
        if i < len(history_lost):
            smoothed_lost = beta * history_lost[i] + (1 - beta) * smoothed_lost
    
    service_level = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_stock = (smoothed_demand + smoothed_lost) * service_level * 1.25  # Lead time buffer
    order_amount = max(0, (smoothed_demand + safety_stock - history_inventory[-1]))
    return order_amount

================================================================================

def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):

        recent_demand = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) > 0 else 0

    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_low\pops/population_generation_4.json (item 1)
# Objective: 3014.23333
# Description: Develop a demand-shaping policy that uses quantile regression on historical demand patterns to set target inventory levels, adjusted by a dynamic cost-ratio-based multiplier.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    demand_quantile = sorted(history_demand)[int(len(history_demand) * cost_ratio)]
    lost_adjustment = sum(history_lost[-3:]) / 3 if history_lost else 0
    
    target_level = int(demand_quantile * (1 + cost_ratio) + lost_adjustment)
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Objective: 9998.86667
# Description: The algorithm implements Constant Order Policy by ordering a fixed quantity at regular intervals 't', regardless of the current inventory level.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    t = len(history_inventory)-1
    interval = 2
    if t%interval:
        order_amount = 0
    else:
        order_amount = 100
    return order_amount

# Description: The algorithm implements Capped Base Stock Policy, where the order quantity is limited by a cap (maximum order size). If the inventory position is below the base stock level,  an order is placed to bring it back up to 'base_stock'. However, the order quantity cannot exceed a predefined cap 'order_cap'.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    base_stock = 70
    order_cap = 30
    order_amount = min(base_stock - history_inventory[-1], order_cap)
    return order_amount

# Objective: 3805.15463
# Description: An adaptive threshold policy that dynamically adjusts the order-up-to level based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, while enforcing a lead-time-aware safety stock buffer.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = history_demand[-3:] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand
    avg_demand = sum(demand_window) / len(demand_window) if demand_window else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    dynamic_base = avg_demand * (1 + cost_ratio)
    safety_stock = max(history_demand[-3:], default=0) * 0.5
    target_level = dynamic_base + safety_stock
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_median\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 2)
# Objective: 3989.54927
# Description: An adaptive threshold policy that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on exponential smoothing of demand, lost sales penalty ratio, and inventory deviation from target.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    alpha = 0.3
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    else:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * (sum(history_demand[:-1]) / max(1, len(history_demand)-1))
    
    penalty_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)
    target_inv = smoothed_demand * (1 + min(1.5, penalty_ratio * 0.3))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    recent_lost = history_lost[-1] if history_lost else 0
    
    adjustment = recent_lost * (penalty_ratio / (penalty_ratio + 1))
    order_amount = max(0, target_inv - current_inv + adjustment)
    
    return order_amount

================================================================================

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_median\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 3)
# Objective: 4073.04444
# Description: The algorithm implements a Demand-Weighted Adaptive Policy that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, smoothing orders while preventing stockouts.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = 3
    if len(history_demand) < demand_window:
        avg_demand = sum(history_demand) / max(1, len(history_demand))
    else:
        avg_demand = sum(history_demand[-demand_window:]) / demand_window
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1)
    safety_factor = min(2.0, max(1.0, cost_ratio * 0.5))
    target_level = avg_demand * safety_factor
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - current_inv)
    
    return order_amount

================================================================================

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_median\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 4)
# Objective: 4133.42891
# Description: A momentum-based adaptive policy that adjusts order quantities using weighted demand trends and cost-sensitive safety stock, with exponential smoothing for demand forecasting.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Calculate momentum factor (weighted recent demand trend)
    momentum_window = min(3, len(history_demand))
    weights = [0.5**i for i in range(momentum_window, 0, -1)]
    weighted_demand = sum(d * w for d, w in zip(history_demand[-momentum_window:], weights))
    momentum_factor = weighted_demand / sum(weights)
    
    # Cost-adjusted safety stock
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    safety_multiplier = min(1.5, 0.5 + 0.1 * cost_ratio)
    safety_stock = momentum_factor * safety_multiplier
    
    # Exponential smoothing forecast
    alpha = 0.3
    forecast = history_demand[-1] if len(history_demand) == 1 else alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * sum(history_demand[:-1])/(len(history_demand)-1)
    
    target_level = forecast + safety_stock
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

================================================================================

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_median\pops/population_generation_2.json (item 0)
# Objective: 3488.78578
# Description: ```
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)
    demand_quantiles = sorted(history_demand)
    median_demand = demand_quantiles[len(demand_quantiles)//2]
    recent_trend = sum(history_demand[-min(3, len(history_demand)):]) / min(3, len(history_demand))
    
    dynamic_multiplier = 1.0 + (cost_ratio * 0.2) + (0.1 if history_lost and history_lost[-1] > 0 else 0)
    base_target = (0.6 * median_demand + 0.4 * recent_trend) * dynamic_multiplier
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_target - current_inv)
    
    return order_amount

    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)

    median_demand = demand_quantiles[len(demand_quantiles)//2]
    recent_trend = sum(history_demand[-min(3, len(history_demand)):]) / min(3, len(history_demand))
    
    dynamic_multiplier = 1.0 + (cost_ratio * 0.2) + (0.1 if history_lost and history_lost[-1] > 0 else 0)
    base_target = (0.6 * median_demand + 0.4 * recent_trend) * dynamic_multiplier
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, base_target - current_inv)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_median\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 1)
# Objective: 3708.19821
# Description: An adaptive inventory algorithm that dynamically adjusts safety stock based on demand volatility and recent lost sales incidents, while smoothing order quantities using exponential weighting.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)
    avg_demand = sum(history_demand) / len(history_demand)
    recent_demand = history_demand[-1] if history_demand else 0
    
    # Demand volatility measure
    demand_std = (sum((d - avg_demand)**2 for d in history_demand) / len(history_demand))**0.5
    volatility_factor = min(2.0, 1.0 + (demand_std / max(avg_demand, 1e-6)))
    
    # Lost sales response factor
    lost_sales_factor = 1.0 + (0.3 if history_lost and sum(history_lost[-2:]) > 0 else 0)
    
    # Exponential smoothing between avg and recent demand
    smoothed_demand = 0.7 * recent_demand + 0.3 * avg_demand
    
    safety_stock = volatility_factor * lost_sales_factor * (cost_ratio ** 0.5) * avg_demand * 0.5
    target_inventory = smoothed_demand + safety_stock
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - current_inv)
    
    return order_amount

================================================================================

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_median\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 2)
# Objective: 3805.15463
# Description: An adaptive threshold policy that dynamically adjusts the order-up-to level based on recent demand patterns and cost ratios, while enforcing a lead-time-aware safety stock buffer.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = history_demand[-3:] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand
    avg_demand = sum(demand_window) / len(demand_window) if demand_window else 0
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    dynamic_base = avg_demand * (1 + cost_ratio)
    safety_stock = max(history_demand[-3:], default=0) * 0.5
    target_level = dynamic_base + safety_stock
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_median\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 4)
# Objective: 3989.54927
# Description: An adaptive threshold policy that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on exponential smoothing of demand, lost sales penalty ratio, and inventory deviation from target.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    alpha = 0.3
    if len(history_demand) == 1:
        smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    else:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * history_demand[-1] + (1 - alpha) * (sum(history_demand[:-1]) / max(1, len(history_demand)-1))
    
    penalty_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)
    target_inv = smoothed_demand * (1 + min(1.5, penalty_ratio * 0.3))
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    recent_lost = history_lost[-1] if history_lost else 0
    
    adjustment = recent_lost * (penalty_ratio / (penalty_ratio + 1))
    order_amount = max(0, target_inv - current_inv + adjustment)
    
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_median\pops/population_generation_4.json (item 0)
# Objective: 3065.28581
# Description:  A probabilistic demand coverage algorithm that uses quantile estimation of historical demand weighted by cost ratios, with lead-time-aware inventory positioning. 
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(len(sorted_demand) * cost_ratio), len(sorted_demand) - 1)
    target_level = sorted_demand[quantile_idx] * (1 + cost_ratio)
    
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    recent_lost = history_lost[-1] if history_lost else 0
    
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - current_inv + recent_lost * cost_ratio)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_median\pops/population_generation_4.json (item 3)
# Objective: 3754.33607
# Description: An inventory algorithm that uses quantile-based demand forecasting with dynamic reorder points adjusted by a non-linear cost sensitivity function and lead-time-aware buffer stock.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / max(holding_cost, 1e-6)
    sorted_demand = sorted(history_demand)
    quantile_idx = min(int(len(sorted_demand) * 0.75), len(sorted_demand)-1)
    quantile_demand = sorted_demand[quantile_idx]
    
    recent_trend = 0.6 * history_demand[-1] + 0.3 * history_demand[-2] + 0.1 * history_demand[-3] if len(history_demand) >= 3 else history_demand[-1]
    
    cost_sensitivity = 1 - 1/(1 + cost_ratio**0.8)
    buffer_stock = (quantile_demand * cost_sensitivity) * (1 + 0.2 * (len(history_demand) > 5))
    
    lost_sales_adjustment = 1.5 if any(history_lost[-2:]) else 1.0
    
    target_inventory = max(quantile_demand, recent_trend) * lost_sales_adjustment + buffer_stock
    current_inv = history_inventory[-1] if history_inventory else 0
    order_amount = max(0, target_inventory - current_inv)
    
    return order_amount

# Description: The algorithm implements Constant Order Policy by ordering a fixed quantity at regular intervals 't', regardless of the current inventory level.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    t = len(history_inventory)-1
    interval = 2
    if t%interval:
        order_amount = 0
    else:
        order_amount = 100
    return order_amount

If the inventory position is below the base stock level,  an order is placed to bring it back up to 'base_stock'. However, the order quantity cannot exceed a predefined cap 'order_cap'.

# Description: The algorithm implements a Demand-Adaptive Threshold Policy that dynamically adjusts order quantities based on recent demand patterns, inventory position, and cost ratio, while respecting lead time constraints.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = 3
    recent_demand = history_demand[-demand_window:]
    avg_demand = sum(recent_demand) / demand_window
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost)
    safety_factor = 1.5 * cost_ratio
    target_level = avg_demand * (1 + safety_factor)
    current_position = history_inventory[-1]
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - current_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_None\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 2)
# Objective: 4176.32635
# Description: A demand-shaping algorithm that combines quantile-based safety stock calculation with weighted cost optimization, using recent lost sales as a feedback mechanism to adjust ordering aggressiveness.
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    # Calculate demand quantiles
    recent_demand = history_demand[-min(5, len(history_demand)):]
    q75 = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))] if len(recent_demand) > 1 else recent_demand[-1]
    
    # Lost sales feedback factor
    lost_feedback = 1.0 + (sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 1)
    
    # Cost-weighted adjustment
    cost_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    adjusted_q75 = q75 * min(2.0, max(1.0, cost_weight ** 0.5)) * lost_feedback
    
    # Current inventory position
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1]
    
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_q75 - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_None\pops/population_generation_1.json (item 3)
# Objective: 4467.54732
# Description: "Adaptive Threshold Policy with Dynamic Safety Stock: Adjusts order quantity based on recent demand volatility and cost ratio, maintaining dynamic safety stock that evolves with observed patterns."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    demand_window = history_demand[-5:] if len(history_demand) >= 5 else history_demand
    avg_demand = sum(demand_window) / len(demand_window) if demand_window else 0
    demand_std = (sum((x - avg_demand)**2 for x in demand_window) / len(demand_window))**0.5 if len(demand_window) > 1 else 0
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost) if (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost) != 0 else 0.5
    safety_stock = max(0, avg_demand * cost_ratio + 2 * demand_std * (1 - cost_ratio))
    
    target_level = avg_demand + safety_stock
    order_amount = max(0, target_level - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

# Source: results\results_archive\results_None_None_None\pops/population_generation_3.json (item 0)
# Objective: 3373.35036
# Description: "Demand-Weighted Cost-Balancing Policy: Uses exponential smoothing of demand trends weighted by cost ratios to determine order amounts, with lead-time adjusted safety buffers."
def compute_order_amount(history_demand, history_inventory, history_lost, holding_cost, lost_sales_cost):
    if not history_demand:
        return 0
    
    alpha = 0.3  # Smoothing factor
    smoothed_demand = history_demand[0]
    for d in history_demand[1:]:
        smoothed_demand = alpha * d + (1 - alpha) * smoothed_demand
    
    cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost) if (holding_cost + lost_sales_cost) != 0 else 0.5
    weighted_demand = smoothed_demand * (1 + cost_ratio)
    
    recent_lost = history_lost[-1] if history_lost else 0
    buffer = max(0, weighted_demand * 0.2 + recent_lost * 0.8)
    
    target = weighted_demand + buffer
    order_amount = max(0, target - history_inventory[-1])
    return order_amount

    q75 = sorted(recent_demand)[int(0.75 * len(recent_demand))] if len(recent_demand) > 1 else recent_demand[-1]
    
    # Lost sales feedback factor
    lost_feedback = 1.0 + (sum(history_lost[-3:]) / (sum(history_demand[-3:]) + 1e-6) if len(history_demand) >= 3 else 1)
    
    # Cost-weighted adjustment
    cost_weight = lost_sales_cost / (holding_cost + 1e-6)
    adjusted_q75 = q75 * min(2.0, max(1.0, cost_weight ** 0.5)) * lost_feedback
    
    # Current inventory position
    inventory_position = history_inventory[-1]
    
    order_amount = max(0, adjusted_q75 - inventory_position)
    return order_amount

================================================================================
